    "delete_position": "删除职位",
    "system_config": "修改系统配置",
}


def write_audit(uid, action, detail=""):
    try:
        conn = get_db()
        ip = request.remote_addr if request else ""
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO audit_log (uid, action, detail, ip, ts) VALUES (?,?,?,?,datetime('now','localtime'))",
            (uid, action, detail, ip),
        )
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        pass


def get_action_label(action):
    return ACTION_LABELS.get(action, action)

5. 权限管理（核心） core/permissions.py

项目级角色（superAdmin/projectAdmin/pd）与文件夹级权限（none/read/edit）模型。提供可见项目计算、文件夹权限解析、权限树构建；实现项目权限的增删改查、单个用户文件夹权限树读写、以及批量授权（含重复检测与自选初始权限：只读/可编辑/无权限，不再由系统按角色硬定）。项目权限列表 GET 接口会排除系统管理员（superAdmin），因为 superAdmin 已拥有全局管理权限，无需在项目权限列表中占一行。

方法路径处理函数
函数get_user_project_role()
函数is_project_admin()
函数get_visible_projects()
函数can_access_project()
函数get_folder_perm()
函数phase_summary()
函数build_folder_tree()
函数register_perm_routes()
查看 / 复制 源代码（594 行） 
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
权限管理模块（整合原增强服务权限逻辑 + 主服务可见项目逻辑）
"""
import json

from flask import request, jsonify
from core.db import get_db
from core.auth import get_request_user, is_admin


def get_user_project_role(uid, pid):
    conn = get_db()
    u = conn.execute("SELECT role FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone()
    if u and u["role"] == "superAdmin":
        return "superAdmin"
    rec = conn.execute(
        "SELECT role_in_project FROM project_permissions WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=?",
        (pid, uid),
    ).fetchone()
    if rec:
        return rec["role_in_project"] or "pd"
    return None


def is_project_admin(uid, pid):
    return get_user_project_role(uid, pid) in ("superAdmin", "projectAdmin")


def can_edit_project_content(uid, pid):
    """零件图片/零件信息等『项目内容』的编辑权判定。

    项目管理员 / 系统管理员，或本人在该项目任一文件夹被授予『可编辑』
    （folder_permissions.permission='edit'）即可编辑；
    与授权界面里各用户的阶段/文档类型权限实时联动。
    """
    if is_project_admin(uid, pid):
        return True
    conn = get_db()
    r = conn.execute(
        """SELECT 1 FROM folder_permissions
           WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL AND permission='edit'
           LIMIT 1""",
        (pid, uid),
    ).fetchone()
    return bool(r)


def get_visible_projects(uid):
    conn = get_db()
    u = conn.execute("SELECT role FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone()
    if u and u["role"] == "superAdmin":
        rows = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects").fetchall()
        return {r["id"]: True for r in rows}
    rows = conn.execute(
        "SELECT project_id FROM project_permissions WHERE user_id=?", (uid,)
    ).fetchall()
    return {r["project_id"]: True for r in rows}


def can_access_project(uid, pid):
    """判断 uid 是否为 pid 项目成员（superAdmin 视为全体成员）。"""
    conn = get_db()
    u = conn.execute("SELECT role FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone()
    if u and u["role"] == "superAdmin":
        return True
    rec = conn.execute(
        "SELECT 1 FROM project_permissions WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=?",
        (pid, uid),
    ).fetchone()
    return bool(rec)


def get_folder_perm(uid, pid, phase, doc_type):
    conn = get_db()
    # 阶段为 none 时，子文件夹强制无权限
    if phase is not None and doc_type is not None:
        ph = conn.execute(
            """SELECT permission FROM folder_permissions
               WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL AND phase=? AND doc_type IS NULL""",
            (pid, uid, phase),
        ).fetchone()
        if ph and ph["permission"] == "none":
            return ""
    if phase is not None and doc_type is not None:
        r = conn.execute(
            """SELECT permission FROM folder_permissions
               WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL AND phase=? AND doc_type=?""",
            (pid, uid, phase, doc_type),
        ).fetchone()
        if r and r["permission"]:
            return r["permission"]
    if phase is not None:
        r = conn.execute(
            """SELECT permission FROM folder_permissions
               WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL AND phase=? AND doc_type IS NULL""",
            (pid, uid, phase),
        ).fetchone()
        if r and r["permission"]:
            return r["permission"]
    r = conn.execute(
        """SELECT permission FROM folder_permissions
           WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL AND phase IS NULL AND doc_type IS NULL""",
        (pid, uid),
    ).fetchone()
    if r and r["permission"]:
        return r["permission"]
    return ""


def phase_summary(phase_permissions):
    if not phase_permissions:
        return "无权限"
    label = {"edit": "可编辑", "read": "只读", "none": "无权限"}
    parts = []
    for ph in sorted(phase_permissions.keys()):
        perm = phase_permissions[ph]
        if perm in ("inherit", "none", ""):
            continue
        parts.append("%s:%s" % (ph, label.get(perm, perm)))
    return " ".join(parts) if parts else "无权限"


def build_folder_tree(conn, pid, uid):
    proj = conn.execute("SELECT id, project_no FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
    if not proj:
        return None
    root = conn.execute(
        """SELECT permission FROM folder_permissions
           WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL AND phase IS NULL AND doc_type IS NULL""",
        (pid, uid),
    ).fetchone()
    tree = {"projectNo": proj["project_no"], "permission": (root["permission"] if root else "")}
    # 只取该项目实际存在的阶段（基于零件上传记录），按阶段模板 sort_order 排序
    phase_rows = conn.execute(
        """SELECT DISTINCT pp.phase, MIN(COALESCE(t.sort_order, 0)) AS so
           FROM part_phases pp
           JOIN parts pt ON pt.id = pp.part_id
           LEFT JOIN phase_doc_templates t ON t.phase = pp.phase
           WHERE pt.project_id = ?
           GROUP BY pp.phase
           ORDER BY so, pp.phase""",
        (pid,),
    ).fetchall()
    phase_order = [r["phase"] for r in phase_rows]
    phase_map = {}
    for phase in phase_order:
        dts = [
            dt["doc_type"]
            for dt in conn.execute(
                """SELECT DISTINCT doc_type, COALESCE(sort_order, 0) AS so
                   FROM phase_doc_templates
                   WHERE phase = ?
                   ORDER BY so, doc_type""",
                (phase,),
            ).fetchall()
        ]
        phase_map[phase] = dts
    fp_rows = conn.execute(
        """SELECT phase, doc_type, permission FROM folder_permissions
           WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL""",
        (pid, uid),
    ).fetchall()
    fp = {}
    for r in fp_rows:
        fp[(r["phase"], r["doc_type"])] = r["permission"]
    phases = []
    for phase in phase_order:
        phase_perm = fp.get((phase, None), "")
        dts = sorted(set(phase_map.get(phase, [])))
        # 阶段为 none 时，子文件夹强制无权限；阶段 edit/read 时，子文件夹无记录则继承阶段
        if phase_perm == "none":
            doc_types = [{"docType": dt, "permission": "none"} for dt in dts]
        elif phase_perm in ("edit", "read"):
            doc_types = [{"docType": dt, "permission": (fp.get((phase, dt)) or phase_perm)} for dt in dts]
        else:
            doc_types = [{"docType": dt, "permission": fp.get((phase, dt), "")} for dt in dts]
        phases.append({"phase": phase, "permission": phase_perm, "docTypes": doc_types})
    tree["phases"] = phases
    return tree


def register_perm_routes(app):

    @app.route("/api/permissions/multi")
    def api_permissions_multi():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        pids = request.args.get("pids", "")
        pid_list = [int(x) for x in pids.split(",") if x.strip().isdigit()]
        conn = get_db()
        out = {}
        for pid in pid_list:
            role = get_user_project_role(u["uid"], pid)
            folders = {}
            rows = conn.execute(
                """SELECT phase, doc_type, permission FROM folder_permissions
                   WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL""",
                (pid, u["uid"]),
            ).fetchall()
            for r in rows:
                ph = r["phase"] or ""
                dt = r["doc_type"] or ""
                key = (ph + ("|" + dt if dt else "")) if (ph or dt) else ""
                folders[key] = r["permission"] or ""
            out[str(pid)] = {"role_in_project": role, "folders": folders}
        return jsonify(out)

    @app.route("/api/admin/project-permissions/<int:pid>")
    def api_admin_project_perms(pid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        proj = conn.execute("SELECT id, project_no FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
        if not proj:
            return jsonify({"error": "项目不存在"}), 404
        rows = conn.execute(
            """SELECT user_id, role_in_project, allowed_phases, phase_permissions, created_at
               FROM project_permissions
               WHERE project_id=? AND user_id NOT IN (SELECT uid FROM users WHERE role='superAdmin')
               ORDER BY user_id""",
            (pid,),
        ).fetchall()
        fp_rows = conn.execute(
            """SELECT user_id, phase, permission FROM folder_permissions
               WHERE project_id=? AND part_id IS NULL AND doc_type IS NULL AND phase IS NOT NULL""",
            (pid,),
        ).fetchall()
        fp_map = {}
        for r in fp_rows:
            fp_map.setdefault(r["user_id"], {})[r["phase"]] = r["permission"]
        user_rows = conn.execute("SELECT uid, name FROM users").fetchall()
        user_name_map = {r["uid"]: r["name"] for r in user_rows}
        out = []
        for r in rows:
            try:
                pp = json.loads(r["phase_permissions"] or "{}")
            except Exception:
                pp = {}
            pp.update(fp_map.get(r["user_id"], {}))
            role = r["role_in_project"]
            summary = "项目管理员（全部可管理）" if role == "projectAdmin" else phase_summary(pp)
            out.append({
                "userId": r["user_id"],
                "userName": user_name_map.get(r["user_id"], r["user_id"]),
                "roleInProject": role,
                "phaseSummary": summary,
                "createdAt": r["created_at"],
            })
        return jsonify(out)

    @app.route("/api/admin/project-permissions/<int:pid>", methods=["POST"])
    def api_admin_project_perm_create(pid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        tuid = (data.get("userId") or "").strip()
        role = data.get("roleInProject") or "pd"
        if not tuid:
            return jsonify({"error": "缺少用户工号"}), 400
        if role not in ("pd", "projectAdmin"):
            return jsonify({"error": "项目角色非法"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        if not conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "项目不存在"}), 404
        if not conn.execute("SELECT uid FROM users WHERE uid=?", (tuid,)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "用户不存在"}), 404
        conn.execute(
            """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO project_permissions
               (project_id, user_id, role_in_project, allowed_phases, phase_permissions, created_at)
               VALUES (?, ?, ?, '*', '{}', datetime('now','localtime'))""",
            (pid, tuid, role),
        )
        conn.commit()
        from core.audit import write_audit
        write_audit(u["uid"], "grant_perm", "project %s grant %s %s" % (pid, tuid, role))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/admin/project-permissions/<int:pid>/<uid>", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_admin_project_perm_update(pid, uid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        role = data.get("roleInProject")
        if role not in ("pd", "projectAdmin"):
            return jsonify({"error": "项目角色非法"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        if not conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM project_permissions WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=?", (pid, uid)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "权限记录不存在"}), 404
        conn.execute(
            "UPDATE project_permissions SET role_in_project=? WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=?",
            (role, pid, uid),
        )
        conn.commit()
        from core.audit import write_audit
        write_audit(u["uid"], "update_perm", "project %s user %s -> %s" % (pid, uid, role))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/admin/project-permissions/<int:pid>/<uid>", methods=["DELETE"])
    def api_admin_project_perm_delete(pid, uid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM project_permissions WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=?", (pid, uid))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM folder_permissions WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=?", (pid, uid))
        conn.commit()
        from core.audit import write_audit
        write_audit(u["uid"], "revoke_perm", "project %s revoke %s" % (pid, uid))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/admin/project-permissions/<int:pid>/<uid>/folder-tree")
    def api_admin_folder_tree_get(pid, uid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        tree = build_folder_tree(conn, pid, uid)
        if tree is None:
            return jsonify({"error": "项目不存在"}), 404
        return jsonify(tree)

    @app.route("/api/admin/project-permissions/<int:pid>/<uid>/folder-tree", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_admin_folder_tree_put(pid, uid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        entries = data.get("entries") or []
        if not isinstance(entries, list):
            return jsonify({"error": "entries 必须是数组"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        if not conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "项目不存在"}), 404
        if not conn.execute("SELECT uid FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "用户不存在"}), 404
        conn.execute(
            """INSERT OR IGNORE INTO project_permissions
               (project_id, user_id, role_in_project, allowed_phases, phase_permissions, created_at)
               VALUES (?, ?, 'pd', '*', '{}', datetime('now','localtime'))""",
            (pid, uid),
        )
        conn.execute(
            "DELETE FROM folder_permissions WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL",
            (pid, uid),
        )
        valid = {"edit", "read", "none"}
        for e in entries:
            perm = e.get("permission")
            if perm not in valid and perm != "":
                continue
            phase = e.get("phase")
            doc_type = e.get("docType")
            if phase is None and doc_type is not None:
                continue
            if not perm:
                continue
            conn.execute(
                """INSERT INTO folder_permissions
                   (project_id, user_id, part_id, phase, doc_type, permission, created_at, updated_at)
                   VALUES (?,?,NULL,?,?,?,datetime('now','localtime'),datetime('now','localtime'))""",
                (pid, uid, phase, doc_type, perm),
            )
        conn.commit()
        from core.audit import write_audit
        write_audit(u["uid"], "save_folder_perm", "project %s user %s folder perm" % (pid, uid))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/permissions/batch", methods=["POST"])
    def api_permissions_batch():
        u = get_request_user()
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        project_ids = [int(x) for x in (data.get("projectIds") or []) if str(x).isdigit()]
        user_ids = data.get("userIds") or []
        role = data.get("role") or "pd"
        if role not in ("pd", "projectAdmin"):
            return jsonify({"error": "项目角色非法"}), 400
        # 初始权限：支持两种模式
        # 1) 新：entries=[{phase, docType, permission}, ...] 按阶段/文档类型矩阵设置
        # 2) 旧：initPermission=edit/read/none 统一设置（兼容旧前端）
        entries = data.get("entries")
        if not isinstance(entries, list):
            entries = None
        init_perm = data.get("initPermission")
        if init_perm not in ("edit", "read", "none"):
            init_perm = "edit" if role == "projectAdmin" else "read"
        # 新：userPerms = {uid: initPermission|entries} —— 多职位混勾时每位用户按自身 initPermission 分别赋权
        user_perms_raw = data.get("userPerms")
        if isinstance(user_perms_raw, list):
            user_perms_raw = {x.get("uid"): x.get("initPermission") for x in user_perms_raw if isinstance(x, dict)}
        if not isinstance(user_perms_raw, dict):
            user_perms_raw = None
        # 新：按职位分组的矩阵覆盖 posEntries={posKey:[{phase,docType,permission}]}
        #     + userPos={uid:posKey} —— 每位用户只套用自己职位那一套手动覆盖
        user_pos_raw = data.get("userPos")
        if not isinstance(user_pos_raw, dict):
            user_pos_raw = {}
        pos_entries_raw = data.get("posEntries")
        pos_entries = {}
        if isinstance(pos_entries_raw, dict):
            for k, lst in pos_entries_raw.items():
                if not isinstance(lst, list):
                    continue
                cleaned = []
                for e in lst:
                    if not isinstance(e, dict):
                        continue
                    perm = e.get("permission")
                    if perm not in ("edit", "read", "none"):
                        continue
                    phase = e.get("phase")
                    doc_type = e.get("docType")
                    if phase is None and doc_type is not None:
                        continue
                    cleaned.append((phase, doc_type, perm))
                if cleaned:
                    pos_entries[str(k or "").strip().lower()] = cleaned
        if not project_ids or not user_ids:
            return jsonify({"error": "请选择项目和用户"}), 400
        # superAdmin 可授权任意项目；projectAdmin 只能授权自己管理的项目
        is_super = u and u["role"] == "superAdmin"
        if not is_super:
            for pid in project_ids:
                if not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
                    return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()

        # 检查重复授权
        placeholders = ",".join("?" * len(project_ids))
        existing = []
        for uid in user_ids:
            rows = conn.execute(
                "SELECT project_id FROM project_permissions WHERE project_id IN (%s) AND user_id=?" % placeholders,
                project_ids + [uid]
            ).fetchall()
            for r in rows:
                existing.append((int(r["project_id"]), uid))
        overwrite = data.get("overwrite") is True
        if existing and not overwrite:
            proj_rows = conn.execute(
                "SELECT id, project_no FROM projects WHERE id IN (%s)" % placeholders, project_ids
            ).fetchall()
            proj_map = {r["id"]: r["project_no"] for r in proj_rows}
            user_rows = conn.execute(
                "SELECT uid, name FROM users WHERE uid IN (%s)" % ",".join("?" * len(user_ids)), user_ids
            ).fetchall()
            user_map = {r["uid"]: r["name"] for r in user_rows}
            return jsonify({
                "error": "以下项目与用户已存在授权关系，确认后将覆盖原权限",
                "existing": [
                    {
                        "projectId": pid,
                        "projectNo": proj_map.get(pid, str(pid)),
                        "userId": uid,
                        "userName": user_map.get(uid, uid)
                    }
                    for pid, uid in existing
                ]
            }), 409

        # 确认覆盖：先删除已有授权记录
        if existing and overwrite:
            for pid, uid in existing:
                conn.execute(
                    "DELETE FROM project_permissions WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=?",
                    (pid, uid)
                )
                conn.execute(
                    "DELETE FROM folder_permissions WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL",
                    (pid, uid)
                )

        # 准备阶段/文档类型模板（user_perms 与 entries/init_perm 都需要）
        templates = conn.execute(
            "SELECT phase, doc_type FROM phase_doc_templates ORDER BY phase, doc_type"
        ).fetchall()
        phase_dt = {}
        for r in templates:
            phase_dt.setdefault(r["phase"], set()).add(r["doc_type"])

        # 过滤合法的 entries（矩阵覆盖：userPerms 默认 + entries 覆盖）
        valid = {"edit", "read", "none"}
        clean_entries = None
        if entries:
            clean_entries = []
            for e in entries:
                perm = e.get("permission")
                if perm not in valid:
                    continue
                phase = e.get("phase")
                doc_type = e.get("docType")
                if phase is None and doc_type is not None:
                    continue
                clean_entries.append((phase, doc_type, perm))

        def _apply_overrides(items, over):
            """把覆盖项合并进默认条目，并做级联：
            阶段级 (phase,None) → 该阶段下未被显式覆盖的子文件夹跟随；
            根级 (None,None) → 未被任何显式阶段覆盖的全部跟随。
            单点子文件夹 (phase,dt) 优先级最高。
            """
            merged = {}
            for phase, doc_type, perm in items:
                merged[(phase, doc_type)] = perm
            for phase, doc_type, perm in over:
                merged[(phase, doc_type)] = perm
            phase_overrides = {p: perm for p, dt, perm in over if p is not None and dt is None}
            explicit_dt = {(p, dt) for p, dt, _ in over if p is not None and dt is not None}
            for (p, dt) in list(merged.keys()):
                if p is not None and dt is not None and (p, dt) not in explicit_dt and p in phase_overrides:
                    merged[(p, dt)] = phase_overrides[p]
            root_perm = next((perm for p, dt, perm in over if p is None and dt is None), None)
            if root_perm is not None:
                phase_explicit = {p for p, dt, _ in over if p is not None}
                for (p, dt) in list(merged.keys()):
                    if p not in phase_explicit:
                        merged[(p, dt)] = root_perm
            return [(p, dt, perm) for (p, dt), perm in merged.items()]

        def _entries_for_user(tuid):
            """每位用户的 folder_permissions 初始化条目。

            优先级：
              1) userPerms（按职位） → 默认填满所有 (phase, None) + (phase, dt)
              2) 该用户职位对应的 posEntries 覆盖（无则用「全部」标签的通用覆盖）
              3) 退回旧的全局 entries 覆盖
              4) 仅 initPermission → 按 init_perm 填满所有
            """
            # 该用户所属职位的覆盖集：优先职位专属，其次「全部」标签的通用覆盖
            upos = str(user_pos_raw.get(tuid) or "").strip().lower()
            over = pos_entries.get(upos)
            if over is None:
                over = pos_entries.get("")
            if over is None and clean_entries is not None:
                over = clean_entries

            items = []
            if user_perms_raw is not None:
                u_perm = user_perms_raw.get(tuid)
                if u_perm not in valid:
                    u_perm = init_perm
                items = [(phase, None, u_perm) for phase in phase_dt]
                for phase, dts in phase_dt.items():
                    for dt in dts:
                        items.append((phase, dt, u_perm))
            elif over is not None:
                items = list(over)
            else:
                items = [(phase, None, init_perm) for phase in phase_dt]
                for phase, dts in phase_dt.items():
                    for dt in dts:
                        items.append((phase, dt, init_perm))
            if over is not None and user_perms_raw is not None:
                items = _apply_overrides(items, over)
            return items

        for pid in project_ids:
            for tuid in user_ids:
                conn.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO project_permissions
                       (project_id, user_id, role_in_project, allowed_phases, phase_permissions, created_at)
                       VALUES (?, ?, ?, '*', '{}', datetime('now','localtime'))""",
                    (pid, tuid, role),
                )
                # 初始化该用户在该项目上的阶段/子文件夹权限
                conn.execute(
                    "DELETE FROM folder_permissions WHERE project_id=? AND user_id=? AND part_id IS NULL",
                    (pid, tuid)
                )
                for phase, doc_type, perm in _entries_for_user(tuid):
                    conn.execute(
                        """INSERT INTO folder_permissions
                           (project_id, user_id, part_id, phase, doc_type, permission, created_at, updated_at)
                           VALUES (?, ?, NULL, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now','localtime'), datetime('now','localtime'))""",
                        (pid, tuid, phase, doc_type, perm),
                    )
        conn.commit()
        from core.audit import write_audit
        if user_perms_raw is not None:
            write_audit(u["uid"], "batch_grant",
                        "batch project %s users %s role %s userPerms=%s" % (
                            project_ids, user_ids, role, user_perms_raw))
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "granted": len(project_ids) * len(user_ids), "role": role,
                            "mode": "userPerms"})
        elif entries:
            write_audit(u["uid"], "batch_grant", "batch project %s users %s role %s entries=%s" % (project_ids, user_ids, role, len(clean_entries or [])))
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "granted": len(project_ids) * len(user_ids), "role": role, "entries": len(clean_entries or [])})
        else:
            write_audit(u["uid"], "batch_grant", "batch project %s users %s role %s init %s" % (project_ids, user_ids, role, init_perm))
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "granted": len(project_ids) * len(user_ids), "role": role, "initPermission": init_perm})

6. 项目 / 零件 / 阶段 / 文档类型 core/projects.py

项目、零件、阶段、自定义文档类型的 CRUD 与计棵树构建；含新建项目时批量生成零件+阶段、项目级联删除（清子表+磁盘目录）、重命名时同步迁移目录与多表 phase 字段，保证树节点与文件归类一致；并支持子文件夹（标准模板与自定义）的重命名与删除，级联同步 files / folder_permissions / custom_doc_types 三表。新建项目权限放宽：系统管理员或项目管理员均可创建；项目创建成功后自动把所有角色为 projectAdmin 的用户写入 project_permissions（role_in_project='projectAdmin'），即所有项目管理员默认拥有该新项目的项目管理员权限，superAdmin 不写入。新建零件弹窗支持选择 NPI 阶段：前端从 /api/phase-templates 动态加载阶段模板，按业务分组（模具/治具阶段、开发验证阶段 P1-PVT、其他阶段）渲染，组内/全局均可全选/取消，并显示目录预览；创建时把所选阶段数组传给后端；后端 api_part_create 接受可选 phases 数组——不传回退「按模板全阶段」旧行为，显式空数组返回 400 提示至少选一个。树形目录仅展示零件实际拥有的阶段（part_phases），不再强制显示全部模板阶段。

方法路径处理函数
函数_proj_with_counts()
函数register_project_routes()
查看 / 复制 源代码（1081 行） 
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
项目 / 零件 / 阶段 / 文档类型 API（复刻主服务核心）
"""
import json
import os
import re
import shutil

from flask import request, jsonify
from core.db import get_db, get_upload_roots, locate_file, locate_overview
from core.auth import get_request_user, is_admin
from core.permissions import get_user_project_role, get_visible_projects, is_project_admin, can_access_project, can_edit_project_content
from core.audit import write_audit

# 零件信息卡默认字段（当项目未自定义字段时回退使用）
# 注：NO 是 SQLite 关键字，故列名为 no_
DEFAULT_PART_INFO_FIELDS = [
    {"key": "no_", "label": "NO.", "sort_order": 0},
    {"key": "rev", "label": "REV", "sort_order": 1},
    {"key": "model", "label": "模号", "sort_order": 2},
    {"key": "description", "label": "Part description", "sort_order": 3},
    {"key": "process", "label": "制程", "sort_order": 4},
    {"key": "pd", "label": "PD", "sort_order": 5},
    {"key": "dqe", "label": "DQE", "sort_order": 6},
    {"key": "stamp_loc", "label": "冲压地点", "sort_order": 7},
]

_KEY_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,40}$")


def _get_part_info_fields(conn, project_id):
    """读取项目自定义字段；若无配置则返回默认 8 字段。"""
    rows = conn.execute(
        "SELECT field_key, label, sort_order FROM part_info_fields "
        "WHERE project_id=? ORDER BY sort_order, field_key",
        (project_id,),
    ).fetchall()
    if rows:
        return [
            {"key": r["field_key"], "label": r["label"], "sort_order": r["sort_order"]}
            for r in rows
        ]
    return list(DEFAULT_PART_INFO_FIELDS)


def _proj_with_counts(conn):
    cols = [r[1] for r in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(projects)")]
    hidden_col = ", hidden" if "hidden" in cols else ""
    projects = conn.execute(
        "SELECT id, project_no, created_by, created_at%s FROM projects ORDER BY project_no" % hidden_col
    ).fetchall()
    parts = conn.execute(
        "SELECT id, project_id, name FROM parts ORDER BY project_id, name"
    ).fetchall()
    counts = conn.execute(
        "SELECT project_id, part_id, COUNT(*) AS c FROM files GROUP BY project_id, part_id"
    ).fetchall()
    proj_total = {}
    part_count = {}
    for r in counts:
        proj_total[r["project_id"]] = proj_total.get(r["project_id"], 0) + r["c"]
        if r["part_id"] is not None:
            part_count[(r["project_id"], r["part_id"])] = r["c"]
    # 零件下的阶段数（用于目录状态：空目录判定）
    phase_rows = conn.execute(
        "SELECT part_id, COUNT(*) AS c FROM part_phases GROUP BY part_id"
    ).fetchall()
    phase_count = {r["part_id"]: r["c"] for r in phase_rows}
    # 零件实际拥有的阶段列表（按 sort_order 排序，供树形仅显示已选阶段）
    pp_rows = conn.execute(
        """SELECT pp.part_id, pp.phase, MIN(COALESCE(t.sort_order, 0)) AS so
           FROM part_phases pp
           LEFT JOIN phase_doc_templates t ON t.phase = pp.phase
           GROUP BY pp.part_id, pp.phase
           ORDER BY pp.part_id, so, pp.phase"""
    ).fetchall()
    part_phases = {}
    for r in pp_rows:
        part_phases.setdefault(r["part_id"], []).append(r["phase"])
    # 每个（project_id, part_id, phase, doc_type）的文件计数（用于树节点角标）
    dt_rows = conn.execute(
        "SELECT project_id, part_id, phase, doc_type, COUNT(*) AS c FROM files "
        "GROUP BY project_id, part_id, phase, doc_type"
    ).fetchall()
    dt_count = {}
    for r in dt_rows:
        if r["part_id"] is None:
            continue
        dt_count[(r["project_id"], r["part_id"], r["phase"], r["doc_type"])] = r["c"]
    part_by_proj = {}
    for pt in parts:
        d = dict(pt)
        d["file_count"] = part_count.get((pt["project_id"], pt["id"]), 0)
        d["phase_count"] = phase_count.get(pt["id"], 0)
        d["phases"] = part_phases.get(pt["id"], [])
        doc_types = []
        for (ppid, ptid, ph, dt), c in dt_count.items():
            if ppid == pt["project_id"] and ptid == pt["id"]:
                doc_types.append({"phase": ph, "doc_type": dt, "file_count": c})
        d["doc_types"] = doc_types
        part_by_proj.setdefault(pt["project_id"], []).append(d)
    out = []
    for p in projects:
        d = dict(p)
        d["hidden"] = int(d.get("hidden") or 0)
        d["file_count"] = proj_total.get(p["id"], 0)
        d["part_count"] = len(part_by_proj.get(p["id"], []))
        d["parts"] = part_by_proj.get(p["id"], [])
        out.append(d)
    return out


def _seed_custom_doc_types(conn, project_id, part_id, phases, created_by):
    """根据阶段模板，把 (phase, doc_type) 快照到零件的 custom_doc_types。
    保证新建零件不依赖全局模板实时渲染，已有零件独立。"""
    if not phases:
        return
    placeholders = ",".join("?" * len(phases))
    rows = conn.execute(
        "SELECT phase, doc_type FROM phase_doc_templates WHERE phase IN (%s)" % placeholders,
        tuple(phases),
    ).fetchall()
    for r in rows:
        if not conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM custom_doc_types WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (project_id, part_id, r["phase"], r["doc_type"]),
        ).fetchone():
            conn.execute(
                """INSERT INTO custom_doc_types (project_id, part_id, phase, doc_type, created_by, created_at)
                   VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,datetime('now','localtime'))""",
                (project_id, part_id, r["phase"], r["doc_type"], created_by),
            )


def register_project_routes(app):

    @app.route("/api/projects")
    def api_projects():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        visible = get_visible_projects(u["uid"])
        all_projects = _proj_with_counts(conn)
        # superAdmin 看到全部；其他仅可见项目，且隐藏项目对普通用户不可见
        if u["role"] == "superAdmin":
            out = all_projects
        else:
            if u["role"] == "projectAdmin":
                admin_pids = {p["id"] for p in all_projects}
            else:
                rows = conn.execute(
                    "SELECT project_id FROM project_permissions WHERE user_id=? "
                    "AND role_in_project IN ('projectAdmin','superAdmin')",
                    (u["uid"],),
                ).fetchall()
                admin_pids = {r["project_id"] for r in rows}
            out = [
                p for p in all_projects
                if p["id"] in visible and (not p["hidden"] or p["id"] in admin_pids)
            ]
        for p in out:
            p["canManage"] = (u["role"] == "superAdmin") or (p["id"] in visible and
                           get_user_project_role(u["uid"], p["id"]) in ("superAdmin", "projectAdmin"))
        return jsonify(out)

    @app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/parts")
    def api_project_parts(pid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        if not can_access_project(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        parts = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, project_id, name FROM parts WHERE project_id=? ORDER BY name", (pid,)
        ).fetchall()
        return jsonify([dict(p) for p in parts])

    @app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/phase-tree")
    def api_project_phase_tree(pid):
        """返回项目实际已有的阶段及对应文档类型（基于零件 part_phases + phase_doc_templates）。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        if not can_access_project(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        # 取该项目下所有零件实际存在的阶段，并按阶段模板 sort_order 排序
        phase_rows = conn.execute(
            """SELECT pp.phase,
                      MIN(CASE WHEN COALESCE(t.sort_order,0)=0 THEN NULL ELSE t.sort_order END) AS so
               FROM part_phases pp
               JOIN parts pt ON pt.id = pp.part_id
               LEFT JOIN phase_doc_templates t ON t.phase = pp.phase
               WHERE pt.project_id = ?
               GROUP BY pp.phase
               ORDER BY CASE WHEN MIN(CASE WHEN COALESCE(t.sort_order,0)=0 THEN NULL ELSE t.sort_order END) IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
                        MIN(CASE WHEN COALESCE(t.sort_order,0)=0 THEN NULL ELSE t.sort_order END),
                        pp.phase""",
            (pid,),
        ).fetchall()
        tree = {}
        for r in phase_rows:
            ph = r["phase"]
            dts = [
                dt["doc_type"]
                for dt in conn.execute(
                    """SELECT DISTINCT doc_type, COALESCE(sort_order, 0) AS so
                       FROM phase_doc_templates
                       WHERE phase = ?
                       ORDER BY so, doc_type""",
                    (ph,),
                ).fetchall()
            ]
            tree[ph] = dts
        return jsonify(tree)

    @app.route("/api/parts/<int:ptid>")
    def api_part_detail(ptid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        pt = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM parts WHERE id=?", (ptid,)).fetchone()
        if not pt:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件不存在"}), 404
        if not can_access_project(u["uid"], pt["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目"}), 403
        return jsonify(dict(pt))

    @app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/info_fields")
    def api_project_info_fields_get(pid):
        """项目信息卡字段配置：项目成员可查看。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        if not can_access_project(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        fields = _get_part_info_fields(conn, pid)
        return jsonify(fields)

    @app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/info_fields", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_project_info_fields_put(pid):
        """保存项目信息卡字段配置：系统管理员 / 项目管理员可维护。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        payload = request.get_json(silent=True) or []
        if not isinstance(payload, list):
            return jsonify({"error": "字段列表应为数组"}), 400

        cleaned = []
        seen = set()
        for idx, item in enumerate(payload):
            key = (item.get("key") or "").strip()
            label = (item.get("label") or "").strip()
            if not key or not label:
                return jsonify({"error": "第 %d 项字段 key/label 不能为空" % (idx + 1)}), 400
            if not _KEY_RE.match(key):
                return jsonify(
                    {"error": "第 %d 项字段 key 只能包含字母/数字/下划线" % (idx + 1)}
                ), 400
            if key in seen:
                return jsonify({"error": "字段 key '%s' 重复" % key}), 400
            seen.add(key)
            try:
                sort = int(item.get("sort_order", idx))
            except Exception:
                sort = idx
            cleaned.append((pid, key, label, sort))

        conn = get_db()
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM part_info_fields WHERE project_id=?", (pid,))
        conn.executemany(
            "INSERT INTO part_info_fields (project_id, field_key, label, sort_order) VALUES (?,?,?,?)",
            cleaned,
        )
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "update_part_info_fields", "project %s" % pid)
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "count": len(cleaned)})

    @app.route("/api/parts/<int:ptid>/info")
    def api_part_info_get(ptid):
        """零件信息卡：项目成员可查看，返回 data + _canEdit 供前端决定只读/可编辑。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        pt = conn.execute(
            "SELECT project_id, name FROM parts WHERE id=?", (ptid,)
        ).fetchone()
        if not pt:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件不存在"}), 404
        if not can_access_project(u["uid"], pt["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目"}), 403
        fields = _get_part_info_fields(conn, pt["project_id"])
        allowed = {f["key"] for f in fields}
        out = {"data": {k: "" for k in allowed}, "updated_by": "", "updated_by_name": "", "updated_at": ""}
        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT pi.data, pi.updated_by, u.name AS updated_by_name, pi.updated_at "
            "FROM part_info pi LEFT JOIN users u ON pi.updated_by=u.uid WHERE pi.part_id=?", (ptid,)
        ).fetchone()
        if row:
            stored = {}
            try:
                stored = json.loads(row["data"] or "{}") if row["data"] else {}
                if not isinstance(stored, dict):
                    stored = {}
            except Exception:
                stored = {}
            for k in allowed:
                v = stored.get(k)
                out["data"][k] = "" if v is None else str(v)
            out["updated_by"] = row["updated_by"] or ""
            out["updated_by_name"] = row["updated_by_name"] or ""
            out["updated_at"] = row["updated_at"] or ""
        out["_canEdit"] = can_edit_project_content(u["uid"], pt["project_id"])
        return jsonify(out)

    @app.route("/api/parts/<int:ptid>/info", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_part_info_put(ptid):
        """保存零件信息卡：项目/系统管理员或本人在该项目任一文件夹可编辑的成员可写，
        仅接受该项目配置的字段。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        pt = conn.execute("SELECT project_id, name FROM parts WHERE id=?", (ptid,)).fetchone()
        if not pt:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件不存在"}), 404
        if not can_edit_project_content(u["uid"], pt["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无该项目内容编辑权限"}), 403

        fields = _get_part_info_fields(conn, pt["project_id"])
        allowed = {f["key"] for f in fields}

        payload = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        if not isinstance(payload, dict) or "data" not in payload:
            return jsonify({"error": "请求体需包含 data 对象"}), 400
        incoming = payload["data"]

        def _clean(v):
            v = "" if v is None else str(v).strip()
            return v[:500]

        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT data FROM part_info WHERE part_id=?", (ptid,)
        ).fetchone()
        stored = {}
        if row and row["data"]:
            try:
                stored = json.loads(row["data"])
                if not isinstance(stored, dict):
                    stored = {}
            except Exception:
                stored = {}
        # 仅覆盖本次提交的允许字段，未提交字段保持原值
        for k in allowed:
            if k in incoming:
                stored[k] = _clean(incoming[k])
        data_json = json.dumps(stored, ensure_ascii=False)

        if row:
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE part_info SET data=?, updated_by=?, updated_at=datetime('now','localtime') "
                "WHERE part_id=?",
                (data_json, u["uid"], ptid),
            )
        else:
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO part_info (part_id, data, updated_by, updated_at) "
                "VALUES (?,?,?,datetime('now','localtime'))",
                (ptid, data_json, u["uid"]),
            )
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "update_part_info", "part %s (%s)" % (ptid, pt["name"]))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/parts/<int:ptid>/phases")
    def api_part_phases(ptid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        pt = conn.execute("SELECT project_id FROM parts WHERE id=?", (ptid,)).fetchone()
        if not pt:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件不存在"}), 404
        if not can_access_project(u["uid"], pt["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目"}), 403
        rows = conn.execute(
            """SELECT pp.phase FROM part_phases pp
               LEFT JOIN phase_doc_templates t ON t.phase = pp.phase
               WHERE pp.part_id=?
               GROUP BY pp.phase
               ORDER BY MIN(COALESCE(t.sort_order, 0)), pp.phase""", (ptid,)
        ).fetchall()
        return jsonify([r["phase"] for r in rows])

    @app.route("/api/phase-docs")
    def api_phase_docs():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        pid = request.args.get("project_id")
        ptid = request.args.get("part_id")
        phase = request.args.get("phase")
        conn = get_db()
        # 仅返回当前用户可访问项目的自定义文档类型，避免越权枚举其他项目配置
        visible = get_visible_projects(u["uid"])
        # 阶段文档类型模板
        tmpl = conn.execute(
            "SELECT phase, doc_type FROM phase_doc_templates ORDER BY sort_order, phase, doc_type"
        ).fetchall()
        # 自定义文档类型（需包含 id，前端在右键菜单使用）
        custom_rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, project_id, part_id, phase, doc_type FROM custom_doc_types"
        ).fetchall()
        custom = [dict(r) for r in custom_rows
                  if (r["project_id"] in visible) or (r["project_id"] is None)]
        return jsonify({
            "templates": [dict(r) for r in tmpl],
            "custom": custom,
        })

    # ---------- 自定义文档类型 ----------
    # 注：/api/phase-templates（返回 {phase:[doc_types]} 分组结构）由
    # core/phase_templates.py 统一提供，此处不再重复注册，避免路由冲突。
    @app.route("/api/custom-doc-types", methods=["POST"])
    def api_custom_doc_type_create():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        pid = data.get("project_id")
        if not pid:
            return jsonify({"error": "缺少 project_id"}), 400

            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        ptid = data.get("part_id")
        phase = (data.get("phase") or "").strip()
        doc_type = (data.get("doc_type") or "").strip()
        if not phase or not doc_type:
            return jsonify({"error": "阶段与文档类型均必填"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        conn.execute(
            """INSERT INTO custom_doc_types (project_id, part_id, phase, doc_type, created_by, created_at)

            (pid, ptid, phase, doc_type, u["uid"]),
        )
