    @app.route("/api/custom-doc-types/<int:ctid>", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_custom_doc_type_rename(ctid):
        """重命名自定义子文件夹（文档类型）。

        会级联同步 files / folder_permissions 中的 doc_type 字段，
        保证已上传文件与权限记录继续归属新名称。
        """
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT project_id, part_id, phase, doc_type FROM custom_doc_types WHERE id=?", (ctid,)
        ).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return jsonify({"error": "记录不存在"}), 404
        pid = row["project_id"]
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403

        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        new_dt = (data.get("doc_type") or "").strip()
        if not new_dt:
            return jsonify({"error": "新文件夹名称不能为空"}), 400
        if new_dt == row["doc_type"]:
            return jsonify({"ok": True})

        # 避免与同阶段下其他名称重复（含标准模板中的名称）
        existing = conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM custom_doc_types WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=? AND id!=?",
            (pid, row["part_id"], row["phase"], new_dt, ctid),
        ).fetchone()
        if not existing:
            existing = conn.execute(
                "SELECT 1 FROM phase_doc_templates WHERE phase=? AND doc_type=?",
                (row["phase"], new_dt),
            ).fetchone()
        if existing:
            return jsonify({"error": "该阶段下已存在同名文件夹"}), 409

        # 同步更新三处关联数据
        conn.execute(
            "UPDATE custom_doc_types SET doc_type=? WHERE id=?",
            (new_dt, ctid),
        )
        conn.execute(
            "UPDATE files SET doc_type=? WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (new_dt, pid, row["part_id"], row["phase"], row["doc_type"]),
        )
        conn.execute(
            "UPDATE folder_permissions SET doc_type=? WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (new_dt, pid, row["part_id"], row["phase"], row["doc_type"]),
        )
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(
            u["uid"], "rename_doc_type",
            "phase=%s old=%s new=%s" % (row["phase"], row["doc_type"], new_dt),
        )
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/custom-doc-types/rename", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_custom_doc_type_rename_by_key():
        """重命名项目/零件/阶段下的子文件夹（支持标准模板文件夹）。

        标准模板文件夹在 custom_doc_types 中可能不存在记录；重命名时会自动
        插入一条项目级自定义记录，并把 files / folder_permissions 中的旧
        doc_type 迁移到新名称。
        """
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        pid = data.get("project_id")
        ptid = data.get("part_id")
        phase = (data.get("phase") or "").strip()
        old_dt = (data.get("old_doc_type") or "").strip()
        new_dt = (data.get("new_doc_type") or "").strip()
        if not pid or not phase or not old_dt:
            return jsonify({"error": "缺少 project_id / phase / old_doc_type"}), 400
        if not new_dt:
            return jsonify({"error": "新文件夹名称不能为空"}), 400
        if new_dt == old_dt:
            return jsonify({"ok": True})
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], int(pid)):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403

        conn = get_db()
        # 检查是否已有其他自定义记录占用新名称
        existing_custom = conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM custom_doc_types WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (pid, ptid, phase, new_dt),
        ).fetchone()
        if existing_custom:
            return jsonify({"error": "该阶段下已存在同名文件夹"}), 409

        old_in_template = conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM phase_doc_templates WHERE phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (phase, old_dt),
        ).fetchone()
        new_in_template = conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM phase_doc_templates WHERE phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (phase, new_dt),
        ).fetchone()
        # 两个都是标准模板名时，会生成同名标准节点冲突，禁止
        if old_in_template and new_in_template:
            return jsonify({"error": "不能直接将一个标准文件夹重命名为另一个标准文件夹"}), 409

        # 迁移 files / folder_permissions
        conn.execute(
            "UPDATE files SET doc_type=? WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (new_dt, pid, ptid, phase, old_dt),
        )
        conn.execute(
            "UPDATE folder_permissions SET doc_type=? WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (new_dt, pid, ptid, phase, old_dt),
        )

        # 维护 custom_doc_types：目标是自定义名则保留/创建记录；目标是标准名则删除自定义映射
        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id FROM custom_doc_types WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (pid, ptid, phase, old_dt),
        ).fetchone()
        if new_in_template:
            # 恢复为标准模板：删除旧的自定义映射
            if row:
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM custom_doc_types WHERE id=?", (row["id"],))
        else:
            # 自定义名：查找或创建记录
            if row:
                conn.execute(
                    "UPDATE custom_doc_types SET doc_type=? WHERE id=?",
                    (new_dt, row["id"]),
                )
            else:
                conn.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO custom_doc_types (project_id, part_id, phase, doc_type, created_by, created_at)
                       VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,datetime('now','localtime'))""",
                    (pid, ptid, phase, new_dt, u["uid"]),
                )
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(
            u["uid"], "rename_doc_type",
            "project=%s part=%s phase=%s old=%s new=%s" % (pid, ptid, phase, old_dt, new_dt),
        )
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/custom-doc-types/delete", methods=["POST"])
    def api_custom_doc_type_delete_by_key():
        """删除项目/零件/阶段下的子文件夹（支持标准模板文件夹）。

        会同步删除该文件夹下的所有文件（含物理文件）、文件夹权限记录以及
        custom_doc_types 自定义映射。
        """
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        pid = data.get("project_id")
        ptid = data.get("part_id")
        phase = (data.get("phase") or "").strip()
        doc_type = (data.get("doc_type") or "").strip()
        if not pid or not phase or not doc_type:
            return jsonify({"error": "缺少 project_id / phase / doc_type"}), 400
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], int(pid)):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403

        conn = get_db()
        # 获取待删除文件列表，用于物理删除
        files = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, project_id, part_id, phase, doc_type, storage_name, storage_root FROM files "
            "WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (pid, ptid, phase, doc_type),
        ).fetchall()
        proj_no = conn.execute("SELECT project_no FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
        proj_no = proj_no["project_no"] if proj_no else str(pid)
        for f in files:
            path = locate_file(proj_no, f["storage_name"], f["storage_root"] if "storage_root" in f.keys() else None)
            if path and os.path.exists(path):
                try:
                    os.remove(path)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        # 删除数据库记录
        conn.execute(
            "DELETE FROM files WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (pid, ptid, phase, doc_type),
        )
        conn.execute(
            "DELETE FROM folder_permissions WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (pid, ptid, phase, doc_type),
        )
        conn.execute(
            "DELETE FROM custom_doc_types WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (pid, ptid, phase, doc_type),
        )
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(
            u["uid"], "delete_doc_type",
            "project=%s part=%s phase=%s doc=%s files=%s" % (pid, ptid, phase, doc_type, len(files)),
        )
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    # ---------- 项目 / 零件 / 阶段 CRUD（仅项目管理员/系统管理员） ----------
    @app.route("/api/projects", methods=["POST"])
    def api_project_create():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        # 新建项目：系统管理员或项目管理员均可创建；创建者默认成为该项目的项目管理员
        if u["role"] not in ("superAdmin", "projectAdmin"):
            return jsonify({"error": "仅系统管理员或项目管理员可创建新项目"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        project_no = (data.get("project_no") or "").strip().upper()
        if not project_no:
            return jsonify({"error": "项目编号必填"}), 400
        parts_raw = data.get("parts") or []
        phases = data.get("phases") or []
        # parts 支持列表或 \n 分隔字符串
        if isinstance(parts_raw, str):
            parts = [p.strip() for p in parts_raw.splitlines() if p.strip()]
        else:
            parts = [str(p).strip() for p in parts_raw if str(p).strip()]
        if not parts:
            return jsonify({"error": "至少需要填写一个零件名称"}), 400
        if not phases:
            return jsonify({"error": "至少需要选择一个 NPI 阶段"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        if conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM projects WHERE project_no=?", (project_no,)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "项目编号已存在"}), 409
        try:
            cur = conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO projects (project_no, created_by, created_at) VALUES (?,?,datetime('now','localtime'))",
                (project_no, u["uid"]),
            )
            pid = cur.lastrowid
            # 批量创建零件与阶段
            for part_name in parts:
                if conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM parts WHERE project_id=? AND name=?", (pid, part_name)).fetchone():
                    continue
                pcur = conn.execute("INSERT INTO parts (project_id, name) VALUES (?,?)", (pid, part_name))
                ptid = pcur.lastrowid
                for ph in phases:
                    if not conn.execute(
                        "SELECT 1 FROM part_phases WHERE part_id=? AND phase=?", (ptid, ph)
                    ).fetchone():
                        conn.execute("INSERT INTO part_phases (part_id, phase) VALUES (?,?)", (ptid, ph))
                _seed_custom_doc_types(conn, pid, ptid, phases, u["uid"])
            # 所有角色为 projectAdmin 的用户默认自动成为该项目的项目管理员
            # superAdmin 已有全局管理权限，无需写入 project_permissions 表
            pa_users = conn.execute("SELECT uid FROM users WHERE role='projectAdmin'").fetchall()
            for pa in pa_users:
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO project_permissions (project_id, user_id, role_in_project) VALUES (?,?,?)",
                    (pid, pa["uid"], "projectAdmin"),
                )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            conn.rollback()
            return jsonify({"error": "创建失败：" + str(e)}), 500
        write_audit(u["uid"], "create_project", "%s parts=%s phases=%s" % (project_no, len(parts), len(phases)))
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "id": pid})

    @app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>", methods=["DELETE"])
    def api_project_delete(pid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        proj = conn.execute("SELECT project_no FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
        if not proj:
            return jsonify({"error": "项目不存在"}), 404
        # 级联清理：先删子表，再删主表，避免产生孤儿记录与悬空文件
        conn.execute(
            "DELETE FROM part_phases WHERE part_id IN (SELECT id FROM parts WHERE project_id=?)",
            (pid,),
        )
        conn.execute(
            "DELETE FROM part_info WHERE part_id IN (SELECT id FROM parts WHERE project_id=?)",
            (pid,),
        )
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM files WHERE project_id=?", (pid,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM custom_doc_types WHERE project_id=?", (pid,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM folder_permissions WHERE project_id=?", (pid,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM project_permissions WHERE project_id=?", (pid,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM parts WHERE project_id=?", (pid,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,))
        conn.commit()
        # 清理磁盘所有存储路径下的 <project_no> 目录
        for root in get_upload_roots():
            proj_dir = os.path.join(root, proj["project_no"])
            if os.path.isdir(proj_dir):
                try:
                    shutil.rmtree(proj_dir)
                except Exception as _e:
                    write_audit(u["uid"], "delete_project_warn",
                                "清理目录失败 %s: %s" % (proj_dir, _e))
        write_audit(u["uid"], "delete_project", "id=%s %s" % (pid, proj["project_no"]))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/parts", methods=["POST"])
    def api_part_create(pid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}

        # 支持批量：优先 names 数组；兼容旧版单 name
        names = []
        if isinstance(data.get("names"), list):
            names = [str(n).strip() for n in data["names"] if str(n).strip()]
        elif isinstance(data.get("name"), str):
            names = [data["name"].strip()]
        if not names:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件名称必填"}), 400
        if len(names) > 50:
            return jsonify({"error": "一次最多创建 50 个零件"}), 400

        conn = get_db()
        if not conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "项目不存在"}), 404

        # 检查重复
        existing = [r["name"] for r in conn.execute(
            "SELECT name FROM parts WHERE project_id=? AND name IN (%s)" % ",".join(["?"] * len(names)),
            (pid,) + tuple(names),
        ).fetchall()]
        if existing:
            return jsonify({"error": "该项目下已存在同名零件: %s" % ", ".join(existing)}), 409

        # 阶段选择
        requested_phases = data.get("phases")
        if isinstance(requested_phases, list):
            phases = [str(p).strip() for p in requested_phases if str(p).strip()]
            if not phases:
                return jsonify({"error": "请至少选择一个 NPI 阶段"}), 400
        else:
            phases = [r["phase"] for r in conn.execute(
                "SELECT DISTINCT phase FROM phase_doc_templates ORDER BY sort_order, phase"
            ).fetchall()]
        if not phases:
            return jsonify({"error": "当前没有可用的 NPI 阶段，请先配置阶段模板"}), 400

        created_ids = []
        for name in names:
            cur = conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO parts (project_id, name) VALUES (?,?)",
                (pid, name),
            )
            ptid = cur.lastrowid
            created_ids.append(ptid)
            for ph in phases:
                if not conn.execute(
                    "SELECT 1 FROM part_phases WHERE part_id=? AND phase=?", (ptid, ph)
                ).fetchone():
                    conn.execute("INSERT INTO part_phases (part_id, phase) VALUES (?,?)", (ptid, ph))
            _seed_custom_doc_types(conn, pid, ptid, phases, u["uid"])
            write_audit(u["uid"], "create_part", "project %s part %s phases=%s" % (pid, name, ",".join(phases)))
        conn.commit()
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "created": len(created_ids), "ids": created_ids})

    @app.route("/api/parts/<int:ptid>", methods=["DELETE"])
    def api_part_delete(ptid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        pt = conn.execute("SELECT project_id, name FROM parts WHERE id=?", (ptid,)).fetchone()
        if not pt:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件不存在"}), 404
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pt["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        # 级联清理：先删子表与磁盘文件，再删主表，避免产生孤儿记录与悬空文件
        proj_no = conn.execute("SELECT project_no FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pt["project_id"],)).fetchone()["project_no"]
        # 1) 删除该零件下的文件及磁盘实体
        for r in conn.execute("SELECT storage_name, storage_root FROM files WHERE part_id=?", (ptid,)).fetchall():
            try:
                p = locate_file(proj_no, r["storage_name"], r["storage_root"] if "storage_root" in r.keys() else None)
                if p and os.path.exists(p):
                    os.remove(p)
            except Exception:
                pass
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM files WHERE part_id=?", (ptid,))
        # 2) 删除零件级概览图及磁盘实体
        for r in conn.execute("SELECT storage_name, storage_root FROM project_overviews WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=?", (pt["project_id"], ptid)).fetchall():
            try:
                p = locate_overview(r["storage_name"], r["storage_root"] if "storage_root" in r.keys() else None)
                if p and os.path.exists(p):
                    os.remove(p)
            except Exception:
                pass
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM project_overviews WHERE project_id=? AND part_id=?", (pt["project_id"], ptid))
        # 3) 清理其他子表
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM part_phases WHERE part_id=?", (ptid,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM folder_permissions WHERE part_id=?", (ptid,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM custom_doc_types WHERE part_id=?", (ptid,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM part_info WHERE part_id=?", (ptid,))
        # 4) 删除零件主表
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM parts WHERE id=?", (ptid,))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "delete_part", "project %s part %s id=%s" % (pt["project_id"], pt["name"], ptid))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/parts/<int:ptid>/phases", methods=["POST"])
    def api_phase_create(ptid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        pt = conn.execute("SELECT project_id FROM parts WHERE id=?", (ptid,)).fetchone()
        if not pt:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件不存在"}), 404
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pt["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        # 支持批量：phases: [] 优先；兼容旧 phase: str
        requested = data.get("phases")
        if isinstance(requested, list):
            phases = [str(p).strip() for p in requested if str(p).strip()]
        else:
            one = (data.get("phase") or "").strip()
            phases = [one] if one else []
        if not phases:
            return jsonify({"error": "请至少输入一个阶段名称"}), 400
        # 去重（按提交顺序保留首次出现）
        seen = set()
        uniq = []
        for p in phases:
            if p not in seen:
                seen.add(p)
                uniq.append(p)
        phases = uniq
        created = []
        skipped = []
        for ph in phases:
            if conn.execute(
                "SELECT 1 FROM part_phases WHERE part_id=? AND phase=?", (ptid, ph)
            ).fetchone():
                skipped.append(ph)
                continue
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO part_phases (part_id, phase) VALUES (?,?)", (ptid, ph)
            )
            created.append(ph)
        # 模板里有 doc_type 的阶段，自动写入 custom_doc_types 形成预设子文件夹；
        # 手动输入且不在模板里的阶段，模板查不到，自然不建子文件夹。
        _seed_custom_doc_types(conn, pt["project_id"], ptid, phases, u["uid"])
        conn.commit()
        if created:
            write_audit(
                u["uid"], "create_phase",
                "part %s phases=%s" % (ptid, ",".join(created)),
            )
        msg_parts = ["已创建 %d 个阶段" % len(created)]
        if skipped:
            msg_parts.append("已存在跳过 %d 个（%s）" % (len(skipped), ", ".join(skipped)))
        return jsonify({
            "ok": True,
            "created": created,
            "skipped": skipped,
            "message": "；".join(msg_parts),
        })

    @app.route("/api/parts/<int:ptid>/phases/<phase>", methods=["DELETE"])
    def api_phase_delete(ptid, phase):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        pt = conn.execute("SELECT project_id FROM parts WHERE id=?", (ptid,)).fetchone()
        if not pt:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件不存在"}), 404
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pt["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        # 阶段下存在文件时不允许直接删除，避免数据孤立
        if conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM files WHERE part_id=? AND phase=?", (ptid, phase)
        ).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "该阶段下存在文件，无法删除"}), 409
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM part_phases WHERE part_id=? AND phase=?", (ptid, phase))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "delete_phase", "part %s phase %s" % (ptid, phase))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    # ---------- 重命名（项目 / 零件 / 阶段） ----------
    @app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_project_rename(pid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        new_no = (data.get("project_no") or "").strip()
        if not new_no:
            return jsonify({"error": "项目编号必填"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        existing = conn.execute("SELECT project_no FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
        if not existing:
            return jsonify({"error": "项目不存在"}), 404
        if new_no != existing["project_no"] and \
           conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM projects WHERE project_no=? AND id<>?", (new_no, pid)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "项目编号已被其他项目使用"}), 409
        conn.execute("UPDATE projects SET project_no=? WHERE id=?", (new_no, pid))
        conn.commit()
        # 迁移磁盘所有存储路径下的项目目录，避免历史文件下载失败
        for root in get_upload_roots():
            old_dir = os.path.join(root, existing["project_no"])
            new_dir = os.path.join(root, new_no)
            if os.path.isdir(old_dir) and old_dir != new_dir:
                try:
                    shutil.move(old_dir, new_dir)
                except Exception as _e:
                    write_audit(u["uid"], "rename_project_warn",
                                "迁移目录失败 %s -> %s: %s" % (old_dir, new_dir, _e))
        write_audit(u["uid"], "rename_project", "%s -> %s" % (existing["project_no"], new_no))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/hide", methods=["POST"])
    def api_project_toggle_hide(pid):
        """隐藏 / 显示项目：系统管理员或项目管理员可切换。

        隐藏后普通用户在项目树中看不到该项目；管理员仍可见并可继续管理。
        """
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        proj = conn.execute("SELECT project_no, hidden FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
        if not proj:
            return jsonify({"error": "项目不存在"}), 404
        new_hidden = 0 if (proj["hidden"] or 0) else 1
        conn.execute("UPDATE projects SET hidden=? WHERE id=?", (new_hidden, pid))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(
            u["uid"], "toggle_project_hidden",
            "id=%s %s hidden=%s" % (pid, proj["project_no"], new_hidden),
        )
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "hidden": new_hidden})

    @app.route("/api/parts/<int:ptid>", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_part_rename(ptid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        pt = conn.execute("SELECT project_id, name FROM parts WHERE id=?", (ptid,)).fetchone()
        if not pt:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件不存在"}), 404
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pt["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        new_name = (data.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not new_name:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件名称必填"}), 400
        if new_name != pt["name"] and \
           conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM parts WHERE project_id=? AND name=? AND id<>?",
                        (pt["project_id"], new_name, ptid)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "该项目下已存在同名零件"}), 409
        conn.execute("UPDATE parts SET name=? WHERE id=?", (new_name, ptid))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "rename_part", "id=%s %s -> %s" % (ptid, pt["name"], new_name))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/parts/<int:ptid>/phases/<path:old_phase>", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_phase_rename(ptid, old_phase):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        pt = conn.execute("SELECT project_id FROM parts WHERE id=?", (ptid,)).fetchone()
        if not pt:
            return jsonify({"error": "零件不存在"}), 404
        if u["role"] != "superAdmin" and not is_project_admin(u["uid"], pt["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要项目管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        new_phase = (data.get("phase") or "").strip()
        if not new_phase:
            return jsonify({"error": "阶段名称必填"}), 400
        if new_phase == old_phase:
            return jsonify({"ok": True})
        if conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM part_phases WHERE part_id=? AND phase=? AND phase<>?",
                        (ptid, new_phase, old_phase)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "该零件下已存在同名阶段"}), 409
        # 阶段是该零件级 part_phases 中的具名记录；同时在多个文件 / 自定义 / 权限表中
        # 通过 phase 字段引用，因此重命名需一并更新（保证树节点 + 文件归类一致）。
        conn.execute("UPDATE part_phases SET phase=? WHERE part_id=? AND phase=?",
                     (new_phase, ptid, old_phase))
        conn.execute("UPDATE files SET phase=? WHERE part_id=? AND phase=?",
                     (new_phase, ptid, old_phase))
        conn.execute("UPDATE custom_doc_types SET phase=? WHERE part_id=? AND phase=?",
                     (new_phase, ptid, old_phase))
        conn.execute("""UPDATE folder_permissions SET phase=?
                          WHERE project_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type IS NOT NULL""",
                     (new_phase, pt["project_id"], old_phase))
        conn.execute("""UPDATE folder_permissions SET phase=?
                          WHERE project_id=? AND phase=? AND doc_type IS NULL""",
                     (new_phase, pt["project_id"], old_phase))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "rename_phase",
                    "part=%s %s -> %s" % (ptid, old_phase, new_phase))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

7. 文件管理 core/files.py

文件的上传 / 列表 / 下载 / 删除。磁盘按 项目/零件/阶段/文档类型 四级目录存放于 uploads/<project_no>/<part_no>/<phase>/<doc_type>/；磁盘文件名保持上传时的原始文件名（重名时自动加 (1)/(2) 序号，不覆盖历史文件），便于在资源管理器中直接按原名查找。上传/删除受文件夹权限（edit）与上传者身份约束；列表与下载做读权限过滤，防止越权枚举。上传白名单 ALLOWED_EXT 共 29 种：图片/PDF/Office/压缩包，以及工程格式 stp/step/dwg/dxf/igs/iges/catpart/catproduct/stl/sldprt/sldasm/x_t/x_b/sat，后端校验拦截不在白名单的扩展名。

方法路径处理函数
函数_proj_no()
函数_part_no()
函数_safe_storage_name()
函数_unique_name()
函数_can_write()
函数_can_read()
函数register_file_routes()
查看 / 复制 源代码（232 行） 
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
文件管理 API（上传 / 下载 / 删除），复用现有 uploads/<project_no>/ 目录
"""
import os
import re

from flask import request, jsonify, send_file
from core.db import get_db, get_current_upload_root, locate_file, build_subpath
from core.auth import get_request_user, is_admin
from core.permissions import get_user_project_role, get_folder_perm
from core.audit import write_audit

ALLOWED_EXT = {"png", "jpg", "jpeg", "gif", "webp", "bmp", "pdf", "xlsx", "xls",
               "doc", "docx", "txt", "zip", "ppt", "pptx",
               # 工程 / CAD / 三维格式
               "stp", "step", "dwg", "dxf", "igs", "iges",
               "catpart", "catproduct", "stl", "sldprt", "sldasm",
               "x_t", "x_b", "sat"}


def _proj_no(conn, pid):
    r = conn.execute("SELECT project_no FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
    return r["project_no"] if r else None


def _part_no(conn, ptid):
    if not ptid:
        return None
    try:
        r = conn.execute("SELECT name FROM parts WHERE id=?", (int(ptid),)).fetchone()
        return r["name"] if r else None
    except Exception:
        return None


_WIN_INVALID = re.compile(r'[\\/:*?"<>|]')


def _safe_storage_name(original):
    """把原始文件名清理为安全的磁盘文件名（保留原文件名，只去除非法字符）。"""
    name = os.path.basename(original or "file").strip()
    if not name:
        name = "file"
    # 去除 Windows 非法字符及控制字符
    name = _WIN_INVALID.sub("", name)
    name = "".join(c for c in name if ord(c) >= 32)
    # 处理连续空格/点号，避免末尾点号导致 Windows 创建失败
    name = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", name).strip()
    name = name.rstrip(".")
    if not name:
        name = "file"
    return name


def _unique_name(folder, name):
    """若 folder 下已存在同名文件，自动加 (1)/(2) 序号，不覆盖旧文件。"""
    if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(folder, name)):
        return name
    base, ext = name, ""
    if "." in base:
        base, ext = base.rsplit(".", 1)
        ext = "." + ext
    i = 1
    while True:
        candidate = "%s (%d)%s" % (base, i, ext)
        if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(folder, candidate)):
            return candidate
        i += 1


def _can_write(u, pid, phase, doc_type):
    phase = phase or None
    doc_type = doc_type or None
    if u["role"] == "superAdmin":
        return True
    if get_user_project_role(u["uid"], pid) in ("superAdmin", "projectAdmin"):
        return True
    perm = get_folder_perm(u["uid"], pid, phase, doc_type)
    return perm in ("edit",)


def _can_read(u, pid, phase, doc_type):
    phase = phase or None
    doc_type = doc_type or None
    if u["role"] == "superAdmin":
        return True
    if get_user_project_role(u["uid"], pid) in ("superAdmin", "projectAdmin"):
        return True
    perm = get_folder_perm(u["uid"], pid, phase, doc_type)
    return perm in ("edit", "read")


def register_file_routes(app):

    @app.route("/api/files")
    def api_files():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        pid = request.args.get("project_id")
        ptid = request.args.get("part_id")
        phase = request.args.get("phase")
        doc_type = request.args.get("doc_type")
        conn = get_db()
        sql = "SELECT * FROM files WHERE 1=1"
        params = []
        if pid:
            sql += " AND project_id=?"
            params.append(pid)
        if ptid:
            sql += " AND part_id=?"
            params.append(ptid)
        if phase:
            sql += " AND phase=?"
            params.append(phase)
        if doc_type:
            sql += " AND doc_type=?"
            params.append(doc_type)
        sql += " ORDER BY uploaded_at DESC"
        rows = conn.execute(sql, params).fetchall()
        items = [dict(r) for r in rows]
        # 权限过滤：仅返回当前用户可读取的文件，防止越权列出项目文件
        items = [it for it in items
                 if _can_read(u, it["project_id"], it.get("phase"), it.get("doc_type"))]
        return jsonify(items)

    @app.route("/api/upload", methods=["POST"])
    def api_upload():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        pid = request.form.get("project_id") or request.args.get("project_id")
        ptid = request.form.get("part_id") or request.args.get("part_id")
        phase = request.form.get("phase") or request.args.get("phase") or None
        doc_type = request.form.get("doc_type") or request.args.get("doc_type") or None
        if not pid:
            return jsonify({"error": "缺少 project_id"}), 400
        # 文件必须归属于具体文档类型文件夹（files.doc_type 为 NOT NULL）
        if not doc_type:
            return jsonify({"error": "缺少文档类型，请选择具体文档类型文件夹后再上传"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        if not _can_write(u, int(pid), phase, doc_type):
            return jsonify({"error": "无上传权限"}), 403
        files = request.files.getlist("file")
        if not files:
            return jsonify({"error": "未选择文件"}), 400
        # 先批量校验文件类型，避免在保存部分文件后报错导致磁盘产生孤立文件
        for f in files:
            original = f.filename or "file"
            ext = original.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower() if "." in original else ""
            if ext and ext not in ALLOWED_EXT:
                return jsonify({"error": "不支持的文件类型: %s" % ext}), 400
        proj_no = _proj_no(conn, int(pid))
        if not proj_no:
            return jsonify({"error": "项目不存在"}), 404
        current_root = get_current_upload_root()
        # 按 零件/阶段/分类 分目录存放（空值用占位符 _）
        part_no = _part_no(conn, ptid)
        subpath = build_subpath(part_no, phase, doc_type)
        folder = os.path.join(current_root, proj_no, subpath)
        os.makedirs(folder, exist_ok=True)
        saved = []
        for f in files:
            original = f.filename or "file"
            # 磁盘存储名保持原文件名；重名时自动加序号，不覆盖历史文件
            storage = _unique_name(folder, _safe_storage_name(original))
            full_path = os.path.join(folder, storage)
            f.save(full_path)
            # storage_name 保存相对子目录 + 文件名，便于按分类定位
            storage_name = os.path.join(subpath, storage)
            conn.execute(
                """INSERT INTO files (project_id, part_id, phase, doc_type, original_name, storage_name, storage_root, uploader_id, uploader_name, size_str, uploaded_at)
                   VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,datetime('now','localtime'))""",
                (pid, ptid, phase, doc_type, original, storage_name, current_root, u["uid"], u["name"],
                 str(os.path.getsize(full_path)),),
            )
            saved.append(original)
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "upload", "project %s phase %s doc %s files %s" % (pid, phase, doc_type, saved))
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "count": len(saved)})

    @app.route("/api/files/<int:fid>/download")
    def api_file_download(fid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        r = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM files WHERE id=?", (fid,)).fetchone()
        if not r:
            return jsonify({"error": "文件不存在"}), 404
        if not _can_read(u, r["project_id"], r["phase"], r["doc_type"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无下载权限"}), 403
        proj_no = _proj_no(conn, r["project_id"])
        path = locate_file(proj_no, r["storage_name"], r["storage_root"] if "storage_root" in r.keys() else None)
        if not path:
            return jsonify({"error": "文件实体缺失"}), 404
        write_audit(u["uid"], "download", "file %s" % r["original_name"])
        return send_file(path, as_attachment=True, download_name=r["original_name"])

    def _can_delete_file(u, file_row):
        # 上传者本人、系统管理员、项目管理员可删除
        if u["role"] == "superAdmin":
            return True
        if file_row["uploader_id"] == u["uid"]:
            return True
        if get_user_project_role(u["uid"], file_row["project_id"]) in ("superAdmin", "projectAdmin"):
            return True
        return False

    @app.route("/api/files/<int:fid>", methods=["DELETE"])
    def api_file_delete(fid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        r = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM files WHERE id=?", (fid,)).fetchone()
        if not r:
            return jsonify({"error": "文件不存在"}), 404
        if not _can_delete_file(u, r):
            return jsonify({"error": "仅上传者、项目管理员或系统管理员可删除"}), 403
        proj_no = _proj_no(conn, r["project_id"])
        path = locate_file(proj_no, r["storage_name"], r["storage_root"] if "storage_root" in r.keys() else None)
        try:
            if path and os.path.exists(path):
                os.remove(path)
        except Exception:
            pass
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM files WHERE id=?", (fid,))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "delete_file", "file %s" % r["original_name"])
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

8. 概览图管理 core/overviews.py

项目级 / 零件级概览图（project_overviews 表，按 rowid 引用）。支持上传（覆盖式，每项目+零件仅一条）、下载预览、删除，以及 Excel(xlsx/xls) 概览图的二维表格解析供前端渲染。

方法路径处理函数
函数_can_view_overview()
函数_can_manage_overview()
函数register_overview_routes()
查看 / 复制 源代码（201 行） 
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
概览图 API（项目级 / 零件级），复用 project_overviews 表与 uploads/overviews/

说明：
  - project_overviews 表使用 (project_id, part_id) 复合主键，业务上每个零件只存一条。
    表本身没有 id 列，路由按 SQLite 行内 ROWID (alias id) 作为前端引用。
  - 查看权限：项目成员及以上；管理权限：项目管理员 / 系统管理员。
"""
import os
import time
import uuid

from flask import request, jsonify, send_file
from core.db import get_db, get_overview_dir, get_current_upload_root, locate_overview
from core.auth import get_request_user, is_admin
from core.permissions import get_user_project_role, is_project_admin, can_edit_project_content
from core.audit import write_audit

import openpyxl

ALLOWED_EXT = {"png", "jpg", "jpeg", "gif", "webp", "bmp", "pdf", "xlsx", "xls"}


def _can_view_overview(u, pid):
    """查看权限：系统管理员 / 项目成员均可查看概览图。"""
    if u["role"] == "superAdmin":
        return True
    return get_user_project_role(u["uid"], int(pid)) is not None


def _can_manage_overview(u, pid, ptid=None):
    """概览图管理权限。

    项目级概览图（不带 part_id）：仅项目管理员 / 系统管理员。
    零件级概览图（带 part_id）：项目管理员，或本人在该项目任一文件夹
    被授予『可编辑』的成员（与文档权限联动）。
    """
    if str(ptid) in ("", "0", "null", "None"):
        return is_project_admin(u["uid"], int(pid))
    return can_edit_project_content(u["uid"], int(pid))


def register_overview_routes(app):

    @app.route("/api/overviews")
    def api_overviews():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        pid = request.args.get("project_id")
        ptid = request.args.get("part_id")
        if not pid:
            return jsonify({"error": "缺少 project_id"}), 400
        if not _can_view_overview(u, pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目概览图"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        sql = ("SELECT o.rowid AS id, o.project_id, o.part_id, o.storage_name, o.original_name, "
               "o.mime, o.size, o.uploader_id, u.name AS uploader_name, o.uploaded_at "
               "FROM project_overviews o LEFT JOIN users u ON o.uploader_id=u.uid "
               "WHERE o.project_id=?")
        params = [pid]
        if ptid:
            sql += " AND part_id=?"
            params.append(ptid)
        rows = conn.execute(sql, params).fetchall()
        return jsonify([dict(r) for r in rows])

    @app.route("/api/overviews", methods=["POST"])
    def api_overview_upload():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        pid = request.form.get("project_id") or request.args.get("project_id")
        ptid = request.form.get("part_id") or request.args.get("part_id") or None
        if not pid:
            return jsonify({"error": "缺少 project_id"}), 400
        if not _can_manage_overview(u, pid, ptid):
            return jsonify({"error": "仅项目管理员可管理项目概览图，或需具备该项目内容编辑权限"}), 403
        f = request.files.get("file")
        if not f:
            return jsonify({"error": "未选择文件"}), 400
        original = f.filename or "overview"
        ext = original.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower() if "." in original else ""
        if ext not in ALLOWED_EXT:
            return jsonify({"error": "不支持的文件类型"}), 400
        current_root = get_current_upload_root()
        ov_dir = get_overview_dir(current_root)
        os.makedirs(ov_dir, exist_ok=True)
        ts = time.strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S")
        storage = "%s_%s_%s" % (ts, uuid.uuid4().hex[:6], original)
        path = os.path.join(ov_dir, storage)
        f.save(path)
        mime = f.mimetype or "application/octet-stream"
        size = os.path.getsize(path)
        # 仅保留该项目+零件的一条概览图（覆盖式）
        conn = get_db()
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM project_overviews WHERE project_id=? AND part_id IS ?",
                     (pid, ptid))
        conn.execute(
            """INSERT INTO project_overviews
               (project_id, part_id, storage_name, storage_root, original_name, mime, size, uploader_id, uploaded_at)
               VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,datetime('now','localtime'))""",
            (pid, ptid, storage, current_root, original, mime, size, u["uid"]),
        )
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "upload_overview",
                    "project %s part %s file %s" % (pid, ptid, original))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/overviews/<int:oid>")
    def api_overview_file(oid):
        """下载 / 内联预览概览图实体（项目成员及以上）。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        r = conn.execute(
            "SELECT rowid AS id, * FROM project_overviews WHERE rowid=?",
            (oid,),
        ).fetchone()
        if not r:
            return jsonify({"error": "概览图不存在"}), 404
        if not _can_view_overview(u, r["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目概览图"}), 403
        path = locate_overview(r["storage_name"], r["storage_root"] if "storage_root" in r.keys() else None)
        if not path:
            return jsonify({"error": "文件实体缺失"}), 404
        return send_file(path)

    @app.route("/api/overviews/<int:pid>/<ptid>", methods=["DELETE"])
    def api_overview_delete(pid, ptid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        # ptid 可能为空串 / null 表示项目级
        if str(ptid) in ("", "null", "0", "None"):
            ptid_val = None
        else:
            try:
                ptid_val = int(ptid)
            except Exception:
                ptid_val = ptid
        if not _can_manage_overview(u, pid, ptid_val):
            return jsonify({"error": "仅项目管理员可管理项目概览图，或需具备该项目内容编辑权限"}), 403
        r = conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM project_overviews WHERE project_id=? AND part_id IS ?",
            (pid, ptid_val),
        ).fetchone()
        if not r:
            return jsonify({"error": "概览图不存在"}), 404
        try:
            p = locate_overview(r["storage_name"], r["storage_root"] if "storage_root" in r.keys() else None)
            if p and os.path.exists(p):
                os.remove(p)
        except Exception:
            pass
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM project_overviews WHERE project_id=? AND part_id IS ?",
                     (pid, ptid_val))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "delete_overview", "project %s part %s" % (pid, ptid_val))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/overviews/<int:oid>/rows")
    def api_overview_rows(oid):
        """解析 Excel 概览图（xlsx/xls），返回每个 sheet 的二维表格数据供前端渲染。

        权限：项目成员及以上（避免外泄），不要求管理员。
        """
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        r = conn.execute(
            "SELECT rowid AS id, * FROM project_overviews WHERE rowid=?",
            (oid,),
        ).fetchone()
        if not r:
            return jsonify({"error": "概览图不存在"}), 404
        if not _can_view_overview(u, r["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目概览图"}), 403
        path = locate_overview(r["storage_name"], r["storage_root"] if "storage_root" in r.keys() else None)
        if not path:
            return jsonify({"error": "文件实体缺失"}), 404
        ext = (r["original_name"] or "").rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower()
        if ext not in ("xlsx", "xls"):
            return jsonify({"error": "该文件不是 Excel"}), 400
        try:
            wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(path, data_only=True)
        except Exception as e:
            return jsonify({"error": "Excel 解析失败: %s" % e}), 400
        sheets_out = []
        for ws in wb.worksheets:
            rows = []
            for row in ws.iter_rows(values_only=True):
                rows.append([("" if v is None else str(v)) for v in row])
                if len(rows) >= 200:
                    break
            sheets_out.append({"name": ws.title, "rows": rows})
        return jsonify({"name": r["original_name"], "sheets": sheets_out})

9. 用户管理 + 审计日志接口 core/users.py

用户增删改查、角色调整（防止降级最后一位超级管理员）、冻结/解冻、重置密码（强制下次改密）；并提供审计日志查询接口（带中文动作标签与操作用户名）。

方法路径处理函数
函数register_user_routes()
GET/api/users/exportapi_users_export() 导出用户 Excel（含职位列）
GET/api/users/import/templateapi_users_import_template() 下载导入模板（含职位列）
POST/api/users/importapi_users_import() 批量导入用户（按职位名自动映射 position_id）
查看 / 复制 源代码（548 行） 
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
用户管理 + 审计日志 API（复刻主服务系统管理功能）
"""
import io

import bcrypt
import openpyxl
from flask import request, jsonify, send_file
from core.db import get_db
from core.auth import get_request_user, is_admin
from core.audit import write_audit, get_action_label


def register_user_routes(app):

    @app.route("/api/users")
    def api_users():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        if not is_admin(u, allow_project_admin=True):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT u.uid, u.name, u.role, u.frozen, u.dept, u.position_id, "
            "p.name AS position_name, u.created_at "
            "FROM users u LEFT JOIN positions p ON u.position_id=p.id "
            "ORDER BY u.role, u.name"
        ).fetchall()
        return jsonify([dict(r) for r in rows])

    @app.route("/api/users/all")
    def api_users_all():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        if not is_admin(u, allow_project_admin=True):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT u.uid, u.name, u.role, u.dept, u.position_id, p.name AS position_name "
            "FROM users u LEFT JOIN positions p ON u.position_id=p.id "
            "WHERE u.frozen=0 ORDER BY u.name"
        ).fetchall()
        return jsonify([dict(r) for r in rows])

    @app.route("/api/users/create", methods=["POST"])
    def api_user_create():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_admin(u):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        uid = (data.get("uid") or "").strip()
        name = (data.get("name") or "").strip()
        role = data.get("role") or "pd"
        dept = data.get("dept") or ""
        pw = data.get("password") or "123456"
        position_id = data.get("position_id")
        if not uid or not name:
            return jsonify({"error": "工号与姓名必填"}), 400
        if role not in ("superAdmin", "projectAdmin", "pd"):
            return jsonify({"error": "角色非法"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        if conn.execute("SELECT uid FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "工号已存在"}), 400
        if position_id is not None:
            position_id = int(position_id) if position_id != "" else None
            if position_id is not None and not conn.execute(
                "SELECT id FROM positions WHERE id=?", (position_id,)
            ).fetchone():
                position_id = None
        nh = bcrypt.hashpw(pw.encode("utf-8"), bcrypt.gensalt()).decode("utf-8")
        conn.execute(
            """INSERT INTO users (uid, name, dept, password_hash, role, frozen, must_change_password, position_id, created_at)
               VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,0,1,?,datetime('now','localtime'))""",
            (uid, name, dept, nh, role, position_id),
        )
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "create_user", "user %s %s" % (uid, name))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/users/<uid>/role", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_user_role(uid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_admin(u):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        role = data.get("role")
        if role not in ("superAdmin", "projectAdmin", "pd"):
            return jsonify({"error": "角色非法"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        if not conn.execute("SELECT uid FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "用户不存在"}), 404
        # 防止降级系统中最后一位超级管理员，否则将无管理员可管理用户/项目
        if role != "superAdmin":
            cur = conn.execute("SELECT role FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone()
            if cur and cur["role"] == "superAdmin":
                cnt = conn.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users WHERE role='superAdmin'"
                ).fetchone()["c"]
                if cnt <= 1:
                    return jsonify({"error": "不能降级最后一位超级管理员"}), 409
        conn.execute("UPDATE users SET role=? WHERE uid=?", (role, uid))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "update_user_role", "user %s -> %s" % (uid, role))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/users/<uid>/freeze", methods=["POST"])
    def api_user_freeze(uid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_admin(u):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        frozen = 1 if data.get("frozen") else 0
        conn = get_db()
        if not conn.execute("SELECT uid FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "用户不存在"}), 404
        conn.execute("UPDATE users SET frozen=? WHERE uid=?", (frozen, uid))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "freeze_user", "user %s frozen=%s" % (uid, frozen))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/users/<uid>/password", methods=["POST"])
    def api_user_password(uid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_admin(u):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        new_pw = data.get("password") or "123456"
        conn = get_db()
        if not conn.execute("SELECT uid FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone():
            return jsonify({"error": "用户不存在"}), 404
        nh = bcrypt.hashpw(new_pw.encode("utf-8"), bcrypt.gensalt()).decode("utf-8")
        conn.execute("UPDATE users SET password_hash=?, must_change_password=1 WHERE uid=?",
                     (nh, uid))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "reset_password", "user %s" % uid)
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/users/<uid>", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_user_update(uid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_admin(u):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        name = (data.get("name") or "").strip()
        dept = data.get("dept") or ""
        role = data.get("role")
        password = data.get("password")  # 可选，非空则重置密码
        position_id = data.get("position_id")
        if not name:
            return jsonify({"error": "姓名必填"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        row = conn.execute("SELECT uid, role FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return jsonify({"error": "用户不存在"}), 404
        # 角色变更时保护最后一位超级管理员
        if role and role != row["role"]:
            if role not in ("superAdmin", "projectAdmin", "pd"):
                return jsonify({"error": "角色非法"}), 400
            if row["role"] == "superAdmin" and role != "superAdmin":
                cnt = conn.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users WHERE role='superAdmin'"
                ).fetchone()["c"]
                if cnt <= 1:
                    return jsonify({"error": "不能降级最后一位系统管理员"}), 409
        upd = "UPDATE users SET name=?, dept=?"
        params = [name, dept]
        if role and role != row["role"]:
            upd += ", role=?"
            params.append(role)
        if position_id is not None:
            pid = int(position_id) if position_id != "" else None
            if pid is not None and not conn.execute(
                "SELECT id FROM positions WHERE id=?", (pid,)
            ).fetchone():
                pid = None
            upd += ", position_id=?"
            params.append(pid)
        if password:
            nh = bcrypt.hashpw(password.encode("utf-8"), bcrypt.gensalt()).decode("utf-8")
            upd += ", password_hash=?, must_change_password=1"
            params.append(nh)
        upd += " WHERE uid=?"
        params.append(uid)
        conn.execute(upd, params)
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "update_user", "user %s" % uid)
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/users/<uid>", methods=["DELETE"])
    def api_user_delete(uid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_admin(u):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        row = conn.execute("SELECT uid, role FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return jsonify({"error": "用户不存在"}), 404
        # 不能删除当前登录账号
        if uid == u["uid"]:
            return jsonify({"error": "不能删除当前登录账号"}), 409
        # 保护最后一位超级管理员
        if row["role"] == "superAdmin":
            cnt = conn.execute(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users WHERE role='superAdmin'"
            ).fetchone()["c"]
            if cnt <= 1:
                return jsonify({"error": "不能删除最后一位系统管理员"}), 409
        # 级联清理该用户的权限记录（项目权限 CASCADE 也会删，这里显式清理更稳妥）
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM project_permissions WHERE user_id=?", (uid,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM folder_permissions WHERE user_id=?", (uid,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "delete_user", "user %s" % uid)
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    def _norm_role(v):
        if not v:
            return "pd", True
        s = str(v).strip().lower()
        if s in ("superadmin", "系统管理员", "sa", "超级管理员"):
            return "superAdmin", True
        if s in ("projectadmin", "项目管理员", "pa"):
            return "projectAdmin", True
        if s in ("pd", "一般用户", "普通用户", "user"):
            return "pd", True
        return None, False

    def _norm_frozen(v):
        if v is None or v == "":
            return 0, True
        s = str(v).strip()
        if s in ("0", "正常", "启用", "激活", "false", "no"):
            return 0, True
        if s in ("1", "冻结", "禁用", "停用", "true", "yes"):
            return 1, True
        return None, False

    def _load_users_dict(conn):
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT uid, name, dept, role, frozen, position_id FROM users"
        ).fetchall()
        return {r["uid"]: dict(r) for r in rows}

    @app.route("/api/users/export")
    def api_users_export():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_admin(u):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT u.uid, u.name, u.dept, u.role, u.frozen, p.name AS position_name "
            "FROM users u LEFT JOIN positions p ON u.position_id=p.id "
            "ORDER BY u.role, u.name"
        ).fetchall()
        wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "用户列表"
        ws.append(["工号", "姓名", "部门", "职位", "角色", "状态"])
        role_disp = {"superAdmin": "系统管理员", "projectAdmin": "项目管理员", "pd": "一般用户"}
        for r in rows:
            ws.append([
                r["uid"],
                r["name"],
                r["dept"] or "",
                r["position_name"] or "",
                role_disp.get(r["role"], r["role"]),
                "冻结" if r["frozen"] else "正常",
            ])
        for col in ws.columns:
            max_len = 0
            col_letter = openpyxl.utils.get_column_letter(col[0].column)
            for cell in col:
                try:
                    val = str(cell.value) if cell.value is not None else ""
                    max_len = max(max_len, len(val))
                except Exception:
                    pass
            ws.column_dimensions[col_letter].width = min(max_len + 4, 40)
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        wb.save(buf)
        buf.seek(0)
        write_audit(u["uid"], "export_users", "count=%s" % len(rows))
        return send_file(
            buf,
            mimetype="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
            as_attachment=True,
            download_name="NPI用户列表.xlsx",
        )

    @app.route("/api/users/import/template")
    def api_users_import_template():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_admin(u):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "用户导入模板"
        headers = ["工号", "姓名", "部门", "职位", "角色", "状态"]
        ws.append(headers)
        # 表头加粗
        from openpyxl.styles import Font, PatternFill, Alignment
        for c in ws[1]:
            c.font = Font(bold=True, color="FFFFFF")
            c.fill = PatternFill("solid", fgColor="4472C4")
            c.alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center")
        # 示例行
        ws.append(["EMP001", "张三", "研发部", "PD", "一般用户", "正常"])
        # 下拉验证：角色 / 状态
        role_dv = openpyxl.worksheet.datavalidation.DataValidation(
            type="list", formula1='"系统管理员,项目管理员,一般用户"', allow_blank=True
        )
        status_dv = openpyxl.worksheet.datavalidation.DataValidation(
            type="list", formula1='"正常,冻结"', allow_blank=True
        )
        ws.add_data_validation(role_dv)
        ws.add_data_validation(status_dv)
        role_dv.add("E2:E1000")
        status_dv.add("F2:F1000")
        # 列宽
        for col_letter, w in zip("ABCDEF", (12, 12, 14, 12, 12, 10)):
            ws.column_dimensions[col_letter].width = w
        # 填写说明 sheet
        note = wb.create_sheet("填写说明")
        notes = [
            ["列名", "是否必填", "说明"],
            ["工号", "必填", "用户的唯一工号，导入后作为登录账号，不能与已有用户重复"],
            ["姓名", "必填", "用户真实姓名"],
            ["部门", "选填", "用户所属部门"],
            ["职位", "选填", "填写职位字典中已存在的职位名称（如 PD / PQE），留空则不设置职位"],
            ["角色", "选填", "可选：系统管理员 / 项目管理员 / 一般用户（留空默认一般用户）"],
            ["状态", "选填", "可选：正常 / 冻结（留空默认正常）"],
            ["", "", "新增用户默认密码为 123456，首次登录需修改"],
            ["", "", "请勿修改表头行，从第二行开始填写；空行会自动跳过"],
        ]
        for r in notes:
            note.append(r)
        for c in note[1]:
            c.font = Font(bold=True)
        for col_letter, w in zip("ABC", (10, 10, 60)):
            note.column_dimensions[col_letter].width = w
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        wb.save(buf)
        buf.seek(0)
        return send_file(
            buf,
            mimetype="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
            as_attachment=True,
            download_name="NPI用户导入模板.xlsx",
        )

    @app.route("/api/users/import", methods=["POST"])
    def api_users_import():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_admin(u):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        dry = request.args.get("dry_run", "1") not in ("0", "false", "False")
        if "file" not in request.files:
            return jsonify({"error": "请上传 Excel 文件"}), 400
        f = request.files["file"]
        if not f.filename:
            return jsonify({"error": "未选择文件"}), 400
        try:
            wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(io.BytesIO(f.read()))
        except Exception as e:
            return jsonify({"error": "无法解析 Excel：" + str(e)}), 400
        ws = wb.active
        conn = get_db()
        existing = _load_users_dict(conn)
        role_disp = {"superAdmin": "系统管理员", "projectAdmin": "项目管理员", "pd": "一般用户"}
        # 职位名称 -> id 映射（用于导入）
        pos_rows = conn.execute("SELECT id, name FROM positions WHERE disabled=0 OR disabled IS NULL").fetchall()
        pos_name_to_id = {r["name"]: r["id"] for r in pos_rows}
        header_map = {}
        rows_out = []
        errors = []
        to_create = []
        to_update = []
        # 解析表头（首行）
        header = [str(c.value or "").strip() for c in next(ws.iter_rows(min_row=1, max_row=1))]
        for idx, h in enumerate(header):
            if h in ("工号", "uid", "用户工号"):
                header_map["uid"] = idx
            elif h in ("姓名", "name", "用户名"):
                header_map["name"] = idx
            elif h in ("部门", "dept", "部门名称"):
                header_map["dept"] = idx
            elif h in ("角色", "role", "权限"):
                header_map["role"] = idx
            elif h in ("状态", "status", "冻结", "frozen"):
                header_map["status"] = idx
            elif h in ("职位", "position", "职位名称"):
                header_map["position"] = idx
        if "uid" not in header_map or "name" not in header_map:
            return jsonify({"error": "Excel 缺少必要列：工号、姓名"}), 400
        uid_idx = header_map["uid"]
        name_idx = header_map["name"]
        dept_idx = header_map.get("dept")
        role_idx = header_map.get("role")
        status_idx = header_map.get("status")
        position_idx = header_map.get("position")
        for row_idx, row in enumerate(ws.iter_rows(min_row=2, values_only=True), start=2):
            uid = str(row[uid_idx] or "").strip() if uid_idx < len(row) else ""
            name = str(row[name_idx] or "").strip() if name_idx < len(row) else ""
            dept = ""
            if dept_idx is not None and dept_idx < len(row) and row[dept_idx] is not None:
                dept = str(row[dept_idx]).strip()
            role_raw = ""
            if role_idx is not None and role_idx < len(row) and row[role_idx] is not None:
                role_raw = str(row[role_idx]).strip()
            status_raw = ""
            if status_idx is not None and status_idx < len(row) and row[status_idx] is not None:
                status_raw = str(row[status_idx]).strip()
            position_raw = ""
            if position_idx is not None and position_idx < len(row) and row[position_idx] is not None:
                position_raw = str(row[position_idx]).strip()
            position_id = None
            if position_raw:
                position_id = pos_name_to_id.get(position_raw)
                if position_id is None:
                    rec = {"row": row_idx, "uid": uid, "name": name, "dept": dept,
                           "role_raw": role_raw, "status_raw": status_raw, "position_raw": position_raw}
                    rec["action"] = "错误"
                    rec["action_detail"] = "职位不存在：%s" % position_raw
                    errors.append("第 %s 行（%s）：职位不存在：%s" % (row_idx, uid, position_raw))
                    rows_out.append(rec)
                    continue
            if not uid and not name and not dept and not role_raw and not status_raw and not position_raw:
                continue  # 跳过空行
            rec = {"row": row_idx, "uid": uid, "name": name, "dept": dept, "role_raw": role_raw,
                   "status_raw": status_raw, "position_raw": position_raw, "position_id": position_id}
            if not uid:
                rec["action"] = "错误"
                rec["action_detail"] = "工号为空"
                errors.append("第 %s 行：工号为空" % row_idx)
                rows_out.append(rec)
                continue
            if not name:
                rec["action"] = "错误"
                rec["action_detail"] = "姓名为空"
                errors.append("第 %s 行（%s）：姓名为空" % (row_idx, uid))
                rows_out.append(rec)
                continue
            role, role_ok = _norm_role(role_raw)
            if not role_ok:
                rec["action"] = "错误"
                rec["action_detail"] = "角色无法识别：%s" % role_raw
                errors.append("第 %s 行（%s）：角色无法识别：%s" % (row_idx, uid, role_raw))
                rows_out.append(rec)
                continue
            frozen, frozen_ok = _norm_frozen(status_raw)
            if not frozen_ok:
                rec["action"] = "错误"
                rec["action_detail"] = "状态无法识别：%s" % status_raw
                errors.append("第 %s 行（%s）：状态无法识别：%s" % (row_idx, uid, status_raw))
                rows_out.append(rec)
                continue
            rec["role"] = role
            rec["role_text"] = role_disp.get(role, role)
            rec["frozen"] = frozen
            rec["status_text"] = "冻结" if frozen else "正常"
            old = existing.get(uid)
            if old:
                changed = (
                    old["name"] != name
                    or (old["dept"] or "") != dept
                    or old["role"] != role
                    or old["frozen"] != frozen
                    or (old.get("position_id") or "") != (position_id or "")
                )
                if old["role"] == "superAdmin" and (role != "superAdmin" or frozen == 1):
                    cnt = conn.execute(
                        "SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users WHERE role='superAdmin'"
                    ).fetchone()["c"]
                    if cnt <= 1:
                        rec["action"] = "错误"
                        rec["action_detail"] = "不能变更/冻结最后一位系统管理员"
                        errors.append("第 %s 行（%s）：不能变更/冻结最后一位系统管理员" % (row_idx, uid))
                        rows_out.append(rec)
                        continue
                if changed:
                    rec["action"] = "更新"
                    rec["action_detail"] = ""
                    to_update.append((uid, name, dept, role, frozen, position_id))
                else:
                    rec["action"] = "跳过"
                    rec["action_detail"] = "与系统一致"
            else:
                rec["action"] = "新增"
                rec["action_detail"] = "默认密码 123456"
                to_create.append((uid, name, dept, role, frozen, position_id))
            rows_out.append(rec)
        stats = {
            "total": len(rows_out),
            "create": len(to_create),
            "update": len(to_update),
            "skip": sum(1 for r in rows_out if r["action"] == "跳过"),
            "error": len(errors),
        }
        if dry:
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "dry_run": True, "rows": rows_out, "errors": errors, "stats": stats})
        # 真正执行
        created = 0
        updated = 0
        for uid, name, dept, role, frozen, position_id in to_create:
            if conn.execute("SELECT uid FROM users WHERE uid=?", (uid,)).fetchone():
                continue
            nh = bcrypt.hashpw("123456".encode("utf-8"), bcrypt.gensalt()).decode("utf-8")
            conn.execute(
                """INSERT INTO users (uid, name, dept, password_hash, role, frozen, must_change_password, position_id, created_at)
                   VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,datetime('now','localtime'))""",
                (uid, name, dept, nh, role, frozen, position_id),
            )
            created += 1
        for uid, name, dept, role, frozen, position_id in to_update:
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE users SET name=?, dept=?, role=?, frozen=?, position_id=? WHERE uid=?",
                (name, dept, role, frozen, position_id, uid),
            )
            updated += 1
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "import_users", "create=%s update=%s" % (created, updated))
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "created": created, "updated": updated, "errors": errors, "stats": stats})

    @app.route("/api/audit")
    def api_audit():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not is_admin(u):
            return jsonify({"error": "需要管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        limit = request.args.get("limit", 200)
        try:
            limit = int(limit)
        except Exception:
            limit = 200
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM audit_log ORDER BY ts DESC LIMIT ?", (limit,)
        ).fetchall()
        out = []
        for r in rows:
            d = dict(r)
            d["action_label"] = get_action_label(r["action"])
            u2 = conn.execute("SELECT name FROM users WHERE uid=?", (r["uid"],)).fetchone()
            d["user_name"] = u2["name"] if u2 else r["uid"]
            out.append(d)
        return jsonify(out)

10. 职位管理 core/positions.py

仅系统管理员可维护的职位字典（名称 / 描述 / 启用停用）。被 users 表的 position_id 关联引用。

方法路径处理函数
函数register_position_routes()
查看 / 复制 源代码（102 行） 
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
职位管理 API（仅 superAdmin 可维护）
"""
from flask import request, jsonify
from core.db import get_db
from core.auth import get_request_user, is_admin
from core.audit import write_audit


def register_position_routes(app):

    @app.route("/api/admin/positions")
    def api_positions_list():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, name, description, active, created_at FROM positions ORDER BY id"
        ).fetchall()
        return jsonify([dict(r) for r in rows])

    @app.route("/api/admin/positions", methods=["POST"])
    def api_position_create():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        name = (data.get("name") or "").strip()
        description = (data.get("description") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            return jsonify({"error": "职位名称必填"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        try:
            cur = conn.execute(
                """INSERT INTO positions (name, description, active, created_at)
                   VALUES (?,?,1,datetime('now','localtime'))""",
                (name, description),
            )
            conn.commit()
            write_audit(u["uid"], "create_position", name)
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "id": cur.lastrowid})
        except Exception:
            return jsonify({"error": "职位名称已存在"}), 409

    @app.route("/api/admin/positions/<int:pid>", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_position_update(pid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        name = (data.get("name") or "").strip()
        description = (data.get("description") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            return jsonify({"error": "职位名称必填"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        try:
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE positions SET name=?, description=? WHERE id=?",
                (name, description, pid),
            )
            conn.commit()
            write_audit(u["uid"], "update_position", "id=%s name=%s" % (pid, name))
            return jsonify({"ok": True})
        except Exception:
            return jsonify({"error": "职位名称已存在"}), 409

    @app.route("/api/admin/positions/<int:pid>/toggle", methods=["POST"])
    def api_position_toggle(pid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        r = conn.execute("SELECT active, name FROM positions WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
        if not r:
            return jsonify({"error": "职位不存在"}), 404
        new_state = 0 if r["active"] else 1
        conn.execute("UPDATE positions SET active=? WHERE id=?", (new_state, pid))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "toggle_position", "id=%s name=%s active=%s" % (pid, r["name"], new_state))
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "active": new_state})

    @app.route("/api/admin/positions/<int:pid>", methods=["DELETE"])
    def api_position_delete(pid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        r = conn.execute("SELECT name FROM positions WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
        if not r:
            return jsonify({"error": "职位不存在"}), 404
        # 若已有用户关联该职位，拒绝删除并提示
        used = conn.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users WHERE position_id=?", (pid,)
        ).fetchone()["c"]
        if used:
            return jsonify({"error": "该职位已被 %s 位用户关联，无法删除，请先调整用户职位" % used}), 409
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM positions WHERE id=?", (pid,))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "delete_position", "id=%s name=%s" % (pid, r["name"]))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

11. 阶段模板 core/phase_templates.py

维护「阶段 → 文档类型」默认结构（phase_doc_templates）。新建零件/阶段、批量授权初始化权限时，依据此处生成文件夹节点与权限骨架。新增 sort_order 字段控制阶段显示与创建顺序：模具/治具阶段（Tooling/Fixture）→ 开发验证阶段（P1/P2/EVT/DVT/PVT）→ MP；API 返回按 sort_order 排序，Flask JSON_SORT_KEYS=False 保持响应顺序。

方法路径处理函数
函数_group_templates()
函数register_phase_template_routes()
查看 / 复制 源代码（98 行） 
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
阶段模板 API：维护「阶段 → 文档类型」的默认结构
新建零件 / 阶段时依据此处生成文件夹与权限节点。
"""
from flask import request, jsonify
from core.db import get_db
from core.auth import get_request_user
from core.permissions import is_project_admin
from core.audit import write_audit


def _group_templates(rows):
    groups = {}
    for r in rows:
        groups.setdefault(r["phase"], []).append(r["doc_type"])
    return groups


def register_phase_template_routes(app):

    @app.route("/api/phase-templates")
    def api_phase_template_list():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, phase, doc_type FROM phase_doc_templates ORDER BY sort_order, phase, doc_type"
        ).fetchall()
        return jsonify(_group_templates(rows))

    @app.route("/api/admin/phase-templates", methods=["POST"])
    def api_phase_template_create():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        phase = (data.get("phase") or "").strip()
        doc_type = (data.get("doc_type") or "").strip()
        if not phase or not doc_type:
            return jsonify({"error": "阶段与文档类型均必填"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        try:
            # 新增行继承同阶段现有 sort_order，保证阶段内所有 doc_type 排序一致
            so_row = conn.execute(
                """SELECT MIN(CASE WHEN COALESCE(sort_order,0)=0 THEN NULL ELSE sort_order END) AS so
                   FROM phase_doc_templates WHERE phase=?""",
                (phase,),
            ).fetchone()
            sort_order = so_row["so"]
            DEFAULT_PHASE_ORDER = {
                "Tooling": 10, "Fixture": 20,
                "P1": 30, "P2": 40, "EVT": 50, "DVT": 60, "PVT": 70,
                "MP": 80,
            }
            if sort_order is None:
                sort_order = DEFAULT_PHASE_ORDER.get(phase)
            if sort_order is None:
                max_so = conn.execute(
                    "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sort_order),0) FROM phase_doc_templates"
                ).fetchone()[0]
                sort_order = max_so + 10
            cur = conn.execute(
                """INSERT INTO phase_doc_templates
                       (phase, doc_type, sort_order, created_by, created_at)
                   VALUES (?,?,?,?,datetime('now','localtime'))""",
                (phase, doc_type, sort_order, u["uid"]),
            )
            # 顺手把同阶段其它可能为 0 的行也统一成该 sort_order，保持归一
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE phase_doc_templates SET sort_order=? WHERE phase=? AND COALESCE(sort_order,0)=0",
                (sort_order, phase),
            )
            conn.commit()
            write_audit(u["uid"], "create_phase_template", "%s/%s" % (phase, doc_type))
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "id": cur.lastrowid})
        except Exception:
            return jsonify({"error": "该阶段下已存在同名文档类型"}), 409

    @app.route("/api/admin/phase-templates", methods=["DELETE"])
    def api_phase_template_delete():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        phase = (data.get("phase") or "").strip()
        doc_type = (data.get("doc_type") or "").strip()
        if not phase or not doc_type:
            return jsonify({"error": "阶段与文档类型均必填"}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        conn.execute(
            "DELETE FROM phase_doc_templates WHERE phase=? AND doc_type=?",
            (phase, doc_type),
        )
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "delete_phase_template", "%s/%s" % (phase, doc_type))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

12. 系统设置 core/system_config.py

键值对系统配置（system_config 表）的读写；重点维护存储路径列表 upload_roots（支持配置多个存储路径、绝对/相对路径自动解析、状态/剩余空间展示与一键打开目录；新文件写入列表最后一个路径，历史路径文件仍可正常读取/下载/删除）。

方法路径处理函数

函数set_setting()
函数register_system_config_routes()
