# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
系统设置 API（仅 superAdmin 可维护）
"""
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
from flask import request, jsonify
from core.db import get_db, get_upload_roots, get_current_upload_root
from core.auth import get_request_user
from core.audit import write_audit


def get_setting(conn, key, default=""):
    r = conn.execute("SELECT value FROM system_config WHERE key=?", (key,)).fetchone()
    return r["value"] if r else default


def set_setting(conn, key, value):
    conn.execute(
        "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO system_config (key, value) VALUES (?, ?)",
        (key, value),
    )


def register_system_config_routes(app):

    @app.route("/api/admin/system-config")
    def api_system_config_list():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        rows = conn.execute("SELECT key, value FROM system_config ORDER BY key").fetchall()
        return jsonify({r["key"]: r["value"] for r in rows})

    @app.route("/api/admin/system-config/<key>", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_system_config_update(key):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        value = (data.get("value") if isinstance(data.get("value"), str) else "").strip()
        conn = get_db()
        set_setting(conn, key, value)
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "update_system_config", "%s=%s" % (key, value))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/admin/system-config/upload-roots", methods=["GET"])
    def api_system_config_upload_roots():
        """返回存储路径列表及当前写入路径。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        roots = get_upload_roots()
        current = get_current_upload_root()
        import shutil
        items = []
        for idx, p in enumerate(roots):
            display = p.replace(os.sep, "/")
            st = {"path": display, "exists": False, "writable": False, "total": 0, "free": 0, "current": p == current}
            try:
                st["exists"] = os.path.isdir(p)
                if st["exists"]:
                    st["writable"] = os.access(p, os.W_OK)
                    usage = shutil.disk_usage(p)
                    st["total"] = usage.total
                    st["free"] = usage.free
            except Exception:
                pass
            items.append(st)
        return jsonify({"roots": items, "current": current.replace(os.sep, "/")})

    @app.route("/api/admin/system-config/upload-roots", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_system_config_upload_roots_update():
        """保存完整存储路径列表；新文件将写入列表最后一个路径。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        roots = data.get("roots") or []
        if not isinstance(roots, list):
            return jsonify({"error": "roots 必须是路径数组"}), 400
        from core.db import DATA_DIR
        cleaned = []
        for p in roots:
            if not isinstance(p, str):
                continue
            p = p.strip()
            if not p:
                continue
            # 允许相对路径：基于数据目录解析为绝对路径
            if not os.path.isabs(p):
                p = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, p)
            p = os.path.abspath(os.path.normpath(p))
            cleaned.append(p)
        if not cleaned:
            cleaned = [get_current_upload_root()]
        # 去重并保持顺序
        seen = set()
        uniq = []
        for p in cleaned:
            if p.lower() not in seen:
                seen.add(p.lower())
                uniq.append(p)
        # 尝试创建每个路径
        for p in uniq:
            try:
                os.makedirs(p, exist_ok=True)
                os.makedirs(os.path.join(p, "overviews"), exist_ok=True)
            except Exception as e:
                return jsonify({"error": "无法创建目录 %s: %s" % (p, e)}), 400
        conn = get_db()
        set_setting(conn, "upload_roots", json.dumps(uniq))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "update_upload_roots", json.dumps(uniq))
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "roots": [p.replace(os.sep, "/") for p in uniq], "current": uniq[-1].replace(os.sep, "/")})

    @app.route("/api/admin/system-config/upload-root", methods=["PUT"])
    def api_system_config_upload_root():
        """兼容旧单路径设置：将传入路径追加为新的当前写入路径（若已存在则置顶）。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        from core.db import DATA_DIR
        path = (data.get("path") or "").strip()
        if path:
            if not os.path.isabs(path):
                path = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, path)
            path = os.path.abspath(os.path.normpath(path))
            try:
                os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
            except Exception as e:
                return jsonify({"error": "无法创建目录: %s" % e}), 400
        roots = get_upload_roots()
        norm = os.path.normpath(path) if path else ""
        if norm and norm not in roots:
            roots.append(norm)
        conn = get_db()
        set_setting(conn, "upload_roots", json.dumps(roots))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "update_upload_root", path)
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/admin/system-config/open-upload-root", methods=["POST"])
    def api_system_config_open_upload_root():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u or u["role"] != "superAdmin":
            return jsonify({"error": "需要系统管理员权限"}), 403
        data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        roots = get_upload_roots()
        idx = data.get("index")
        if isinstance(idx, int) and 0 <= idx < len(roots):
            path = roots[idx]
        else:
            path = get_current_upload_root()
        if not os.path.isdir(path):
            return jsonify({"error": "目录不存在: %s" % path}), 400
        try:
            if os.name == "nt":
                os.startfile(path)
            elif os.uname().sysname == "Darwin":
                subprocess.Popen(["open", path])
            else:
                subprocess.Popen(["xdg-open", path])
        except Exception as e:
            return jsonify({"error": "无法打开目录: %s" % e}), 500
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "path": path})

13. 零件 3D 模型 core/models3d.py

零件 3D 图直接读取该零件文件夹下的 3D 源文件（STEP/IGES/STL/BREP），无需单独上传；首次预览时由独立可执行 npi_occt_cli.exe（OpenCASCADE）按需转换为 GLB + 几何元数据 JSON 并缓存（源文件变更按 mtime/size 自动重转），前端 three.js 渲染；一个零件可有多个 3D 文件，下拉切换，默认显示最新上传的。权限与零件概览图一致（查看=项目成员，管理=项目管理员或具备编辑权成员）；CLI 缺失时仅无法生成预览，不影响其它业务。转换按文件加并发锁（懒创建、键为含 mtime/size 的缓存路径）：同一模型只启动一次转换，其余请求等待并复用结果，避免重复计算与并发写坏产物；不同文件之间仍并行转换，缓存命中路径不加锁。产物先写临时文件（保持 .glb / .json 后缀，因为转换器按输出扩展名判定二进制 glTF）再原子改名，避免其他请求读到写了一半的文件。转换超时 300 秒，子进程 TEMP 重定向到部署目录 runtime_tmp 以规避 C 盘解压空间不足。

方法路径处理函数
GET/api/models3dapi_models3d_list
GET/api/models3d/cli-statusapi_models3d_cli_status
POST/api/models3d/uploadapi_models3d_upload
GET/api/models3d/<id>/glbapi_models3d_glb
GET/api/models3d/<id>/sourceapi_models3d_source
DELETE/api/models3d/<id>api_models3d_delete
POST/api/models3d/<id>/retryapi_models3d_retry
查看 / 复制 源代码（646 行） 
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
零件 3D 模型 API（STEP/IGES/STL → GLB 预览）

设计要点：
  - 转换由**独立的 C++ 可执行** npi_occt_cli.exe 完成（OpenCASCADE），
    Python 端只负责调度与存储，OCCT 的 DLL 不会被打进 NPI_Server.exe。
  - CLI 不存在时功能降级：仍可上传/下载/删除原始模型，只是无法生成预览，
    不会阻塞其它业务。
  - 权限与「零件概览图」保持一致：
      查看 = 项目成员及以上；管理 = 项目管理员，或在该项目任一文件夹有编辑权的成员。
  - 每个零件可有多个 3D 模型（不同于概览图的覆盖式单条）。

目录：uploads/models/            存放上传的原始模型
      uploads/models/glb/        存放转换产物 GLB 与元数据 JSON
"""
import os
import re
import json
import time
import uuid
import shutil
import subprocess
import threading

from flask import request, jsonify, send_file
from core.db import get_db, get_current_upload_root, get_upload_roots, locate_file
from core.auth import get_request_user
from core.permissions import get_user_project_role, is_project_admin, can_edit_project_content
from core.audit import write_audit

# 支持的源格式
ALLOWED_EXT = {"step", "stp", "iges", "igs", "stl", "brep", "brp"}
EXT_LABEL = {
    "step": "STEP", "stp": "STEP",
    "iges": "IGES", "igs": "IGES",
    "stl": "STL",
    "brep": "BREP", "brp": "BREP",
}

# CLI 可执行名（会依次在以下位置查找）
CLI_NAME = "npi_occt_cli.exe" if os.name == "nt" else "npi_occt_cli"
CONVERT_TIMEOUT = 300  # 秒

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 转换并发锁
# 转换是CPU 密集且耗时的（最长 CONVERT_TIMEOUT 秒）。若不加锁，多人同时首次打开
# 同一个未转换的源文件时，会各自启动一个 CLI 进程重复做同一份计算，白白吃掉多个
# CPU 核；更严重的是多个进程会并发写同一个 .glb 产物，可能把文件写坏。
# 故按缓存键（源文件及其 mtime/size）加锁：同一模型串行转换，不同模型仍可并行。
_CONVERT_LOCKS = {}
_CONVERT_LOCKS_GUARD = threading.Lock()


def _convert_lock_for(key):
    """取得某个缓存键对应的转换锁（懒创建，永不删除）。"""
    with _CONVERT_LOCKS_GUARD:
        lk = _CONVERT_LOCKS.get(key)
        if lk is None:
            lk = threading.Lock()
            _CONVERT_LOCKS[key] = lk
        return lk



# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 路径工具

def _models_dir(root=None):
    return os.path.join(root or get_current_upload_root(), "models")


def _glb_dir(root=None):
    return os.path.join(_models_dir(root), "glb")


def _locate_model(storage_name, storage_root=None):
    """定位已上传的原始模型文件（兼容多存储根）。"""
    cands = []
    if storage_root:
        cands.append(os.path.join(storage_root, "models", storage_name))
    for root in get_upload_roots():
        cands.append(os.path.join(root, "models", storage_name))
    for p in cands:
        if p and os.path.exists(p):
            return p
    return None


def _locate_glb(glb_name, storage_root=None):
    if not glb_name:
        return None
    cands = []
    if storage_root:
        cands.append(os.path.join(storage_root, "models", "glb", glb_name))
    for root in get_upload_roots():
        cands.append(os.path.join(root, "models", "glb", glb_name))
    for p in cands:
        if p and os.path.exists(p):
            return p
    return None


def find_cli():
    """定位 npi_occt_cli 可执行文件。

    查找顺序：
      1) 环境变量 NPI_OCCT_CLI（显式指定）
      2) 运行目录（EXE 同目录，即 01_部署运行/）
      3) 源码目录（开发调试用）
    返回路径或 None。
    """
    env = os.environ.get("NPI_OCCT_CLI")
    if env and os.path.isfile(env):
        return env
    here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))          # 02_源码/core
    bases = [
        os.getcwd(),                                            # 运行目录
        os.path.dirname(here),                                  # 02_源码
        os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(here)),                 # 工程根
    ]
    for b in bases:
        for sub in ("", "01_部署运行", "bin"):
            # 优先 onedir 目录形式（npi_occt_cli/npi_occt_cli.exe，启动无需解压、快约 20 倍）
            p = os.path.join(b, sub, "npi_occt_cli", CLI_NAME)
            if os.path.isfile(p):
                return p
            p = os.path.join(b, sub, CLI_NAME)
            if os.path.isfile(p):
                return p
    # PATH 中查找
    which = shutil.which(CLI_NAME)
    return which


def cli_available():
    return find_cli() is not None


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 权限

def _can_view_model(u, pid):
    if u["role"] == "superAdmin":
        return True
    return get_user_project_role(u["uid"], int(pid)) is not None


def _can_manage_model(u, pid):
    """与零件级概览图一致：项目管理员，或在该项目任一文件夹有编辑权的成员。"""
    if u["role"] == "superAdmin":
        return True
    if is_project_admin(u["uid"], int(pid)):
        return True
    return can_edit_project_content(u["uid"], int(pid))


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 转换

def _runtime_tmp():
    """CLI 解压运行时目录：EXE 是 onefile 打包（含数百 MB OCCT DLL），
    每次启动要解压到 TEMP。C 盘 TEMP 空间紧张会导致解压失败
    （Failed to extract ... return code -1），故重定向到部署目录所在的大盘。"""
    base = os.path.dirname(get_current_upload_root())
    tmp = os.path.join(base, "runtime_tmp")
    try:
        os.makedirs(tmp, exist_ok=True)
    except Exception:
        tmp = None
    return tmp


def _cli_env():
    env = os.environ.copy()
    tmp = _runtime_tmp()
    if tmp:
        env["TEMP"] = env["TMP"] = tmp
    return env


def _run_convert(cli, src_path, glb_path, json_path, unit="mm"):
    """调用 CLI 转换，返回 (ok, meta_dict_or_None, error_msg)。"""
    cmd = [cli, "convert", src_path, glb_path, "--json", json_path, "--unit", unit]
    try:
        # 隐藏控制台窗口（Windows）
        startup = None
        if os.name == "nt":
            startup = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
            startup.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
            startup.wShowWindow = 0
        p = subprocess.run(
            cmd, capture_output=True, timeout=CONVERT_TIMEOUT,
            env=_cli_env(),
            startupinfo=startup if os.name == "nt" else None,
        )
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return False, None, "转换超时（超过 %d 秒），模型可能过于复杂" % CONVERT_TIMEOUT
    except Exception as e:
        return False, None, "无法启动转换工具：%s" % e

    meta = None
    if os.path.isfile(json_path):
        try:
            with open(json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                meta = json.load(f)
        except Exception:
            meta = None

    if p.returncode != 0:
        err = (p.stderr or b"").decode("utf-8", "ignore").strip()
        if not err:
            err = (p.stdout or b"").decode("utf-8", "ignore").strip()
        code_msg = {
            2: "无法读取该文件（格式不支持或文件损坏）",
            3: "文件中没有可显示的几何体",
            4: "GLB 写出失败（磁盘空间不足或路径无权限）",
        }.get(p.returncode)
        return False, meta, code_msg or ("转换失败（退出码 %d）%s" % (p.returncode, err[:200]))

    if not os.path.isfile(glb_path):
        return False, meta, "转换未生成 GLB 文件"
    return True, meta, ""


# ------------------------------------------------- 零件文件夹里的 3D 文件（3D 图数据来源）

# 视为「可做 3D 预览」的文件扩展名（与文件上传允许的类型取交集）
DOC_3D_EXT = ("step", "stp", "iges", "igs", "stl", "brep", "brp")


def _ext_of(name):
    name = name or ""
    return name.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower() if "." in name else ""


def _file_src_path(row):
    """定位 files 表中某条记录对应的磁盘文件。"""
    conn = get_db()
    pr = conn.execute("SELECT project_no FROM projects WHERE id=?",
                      (row["project_id"],)).fetchone()
    if not pr:
        return None
    root = row["storage_root"] if "storage_root" in row.keys() else None
    return locate_file(pr["project_no"], row["storage_name"], root)


def _file_cache_paths(fid, src_path):
    """按「文件 id + 源文件 mtime/size」生成缓存名：源文件被替换后会自动重转。"""
    try:
        st = os.stat(src_path)
        key = "f%d_%d_%d" % (fid, int(st.st_mtime_ns), int(st.st_size))
    except Exception:
        key = "f%d" % fid
    d = _glb_dir()
    return os.path.join(d, key + ".glb"), os.path.join(d, key + ".json")


def _ensure_file_glb(fid, row):
    """确保该文件已转换为 GLB（带缓存）。返回 (glb_path, meta, err)。"""
    src = _file_src_path(row)
    if not src:
        return None, None, "源文件已丢失（可能已被删除或移动）"
    glb, js = _file_cache_paths(fid, src)

    def _read_cached():
        """产物已存在则返回 (glb, meta)，否则 None。"""
        if not os.path.isfile(glb):
            return None
        meta = None
        if os.path.isfile(js):
            try:
                with open(js, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    meta = json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                meta = None
        return glb, meta

    # 快路径：已有缓存，直接返回且不加锁（读缓存极廉价，避免把命中流量全部串行化）
    hit = _read_cached()
    if hit:
        return hit[0], hit[1], ""

    cli = find_cli()
    if not cli:
        return None, None, "未找到 3D 转换工具 npi_occt_cli.exe"
    try:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(glb), exist_ok=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 慢路径：按缓存键加锁串行转换。
    # 锁键用 glb 路径（内含源文件 mtime/size），源文件被替换后键会变化，
    # 新旧两份缓存可各自独立转换，互不阻塞。
    with _convert_lock_for(glb):
        # 双检：等锁期间可能已被另一个请求转好
        hit = _read_cached()
        if hit:
            return hit[0], hit[1], ""
        # 先写临时产物，成功后再原子改名。
        # 临时名必须保持 .glb / .json 后缀 —— CLI 会按输出文件名的扩展名判断
        # 要不要写二进制 glTF（见 npi_occt_cli.cpp 中对 ".glb" 的比较），
        # 若用 xxx.glb.part 会导致写出 ASCII glTF 内容却命名为 .glb，产物损坏。
        tmp_glb = glb + ".part.glb"
        tmp_js = js + ".part.json"

        def _cleanup():
            for p in (tmp_glb, tmp_js):
                try:
                    os.remove(p)
                except OSError:
                    pass

        try:
            ok, meta, err = _run_convert(cli, src, tmp_glb, tmp_js, "mm")
        except Exception as e:
            _cleanup()
            return None, None, "转换异常：%s" % e
        if not ok or not os.path.isfile(tmp_glb):
            _cleanup()
            return None, meta, err or "转换失败"
        try:
            os.replace(tmp_glb, glb)
            if os.path.isfile(tmp_js):
                os.replace(tmp_js, js)
        except OSError as e:
            _cleanup()
            return None, meta, "转换结果落盘失败：%s" % e
        return glb, meta, ""


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 路由

def register_model3d_routes(app):

    @app.route("/api/models3d/cli-status")
    def api_models3d_cli_status():
        """前端据此判断是否显示「无法预览」提示。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        cli = find_cli()
        info = {"available": bool(cli), "path": cli or "", "version": ""}
        if cli:
            try:
                startup = None
                if os.name == "nt":
                    startup = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
                    startup.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
                # onefile EXE 首次启动要解压数百 MB，超时给足
                p = subprocess.run([cli, "version"], capture_output=True, timeout=120,
                                   env=_cli_env(), startupinfo=startup)
                info["version"] = (p.stdout or b"").decode("utf-8", "ignore").strip()
            except Exception:
                pass
        return jsonify(info)

    @app.route("/api/models3d")
    def api_models3d_list():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        pid = request.args.get("project_id")
        ptid = request.args.get("part_id")
        if not pid or not ptid:
            return jsonify({"error": "缺少 project_id / part_id"}), 400
        if not _can_view_model(u, pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目"}), 403
        conn = get_db()
        out = []

        # 数据来源：该零件文件夹里已有的 3D 文件（STEP/IGES/STL/BREP），无需再单独上传
        frows = conn.execute(
            """SELECT f.*, u.name AS uploader_name
               FROM files f LEFT JOIN users u ON f.uploader_id=u.uid
               WHERE f.project_id=? AND f.part_id=?
               ORDER BY f.uploaded_at DESC, f.id DESC""", (pid, ptid)
        ).fetchall()
        for r in frows:
            ext = _ext_of(r["original_name"])
            if ext not in DOC_3D_EXT:
                continue
            fid = r["id"]
            src = _file_src_path(r)
            # 源文件已不在零件文件夹里（被删除/移动）→ 不再作为 3D 图展示
            if not src:
                continue
            meta, converted = {}, False
            glb, js = _file_cache_paths(fid, src)
            if os.path.isfile(glb):
                converted = True
                if os.path.isfile(js):
                    try:
                        with open(js, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fp:
                            meta = json.load(fp)
                    except Exception:
                        meta = {}
            out.append({
                "id": "f%d" % fid,
                "fid": fid,
                "kind": "file",
                "original_name": r["original_name"],
                "src_format": EXT_LABEL.get(ext, ext.upper()),
                "uploader_name": r["uploader_name"] or r["uploader_id"] or "",
                "uploaded_at": r["uploaded_at"],
                "size_str": r["size_str"] or "",
                "status": "ready",          # 首次预览时按需转换（前端显示“正在加载模型…”）
                "converted": converted,     # 是否已有转换缓存
                "glb_url": "/api/models3d/file/%d/glb" % fid,
                "meta_url": "/api/models3d/file/%d/meta" % fid,
                "meta": meta,
            })

        # 只展示零件文件夹里的 3D 文件；默认第一条即最新上传的
        out.sort(key=lambda x: (str(x.get("uploaded_at") or ""), str(x.get("id") or "")),
                 reverse=True)
        return jsonify(out)

    @app.route("/api/models3d/upload", methods=["POST"])
    def api_models3d_upload():
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        pid = request.form.get("project_id")
        ptid = request.form.get("part_id")
        if not pid or not ptid:
            return jsonify({"error": "缺少 project_id / part_id"}), 400
        if not _can_manage_model(u, pid):
            return jsonify({"error": "仅项目管理员或具备该项目编辑权限的成员可上传 3D 模型"}), 403

        f = request.files.get("file")
        if not f:
            return jsonify({"error": "未选择文件"}), 400
        original = f.filename or "model"
        ext = original.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower() if "." in original else ""
        if ext not in ALLOWED_EXT:
            return jsonify({"error": "不支持的格式：.%s（支持 STEP/IGES/STL/BREP）" % ext}), 400

        unit = (request.form.get("unit") or "mm").strip().lower()
        if unit not in ("mm", "cm", "m", "in", "ft"):
            unit = "mm"

        root = get_current_upload_root()
        m_dir = _models_dir(root)
        g_dir = _glb_dir(root)
        os.makedirs(m_dir, exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(g_dir, exist_ok=True)

        ts = time.strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S")
        safe = re.sub(r'[\\/:*?"<>|]+', "_", original)
        storage = "%s_%s_%s" % (ts, uuid.uuid4().hex[:6], safe)
        src_path = os.path.join(m_dir, storage)
        f.save(src_path)
        size = os.path.getsize(src_path)

        conn = get_db()
        cur = conn.execute(
            """INSERT INTO part_models
               (project_id, part_id, storage_name, glb_name, original_name,
                src_format, size, status, uploader_id, uploaded_at)
               VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,datetime('now','localtime'))""",
            (pid, ptid, storage, "", original, EXT_LABEL.get(ext, ext.upper()),
             size, "pending", u["uid"]),
        )
        mid = cur.lastrowid
        conn.commit()

        # 转换
        cli = find_cli()
        if not cli:
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE part_models SET status='failed', error_msg=? WHERE id=?",
                ("未找到 3D 转换工具 npi_occt_cli.exe，请将其放入服务运行目录", mid))
            conn.commit()
            write_audit(u["uid"], "upload_model3d",
                        "project %s part %s file %s (no cli)" % (pid, ptid, original))
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "id": mid, "status": "failed",
                            "error": "未找到 3D 转换工具，仅已保存原始文件"})

        glb_name = "%d.glb" % mid
        json_name = "%d.json" % mid
        glb_path = os.path.join(g_dir, glb_name)
        json_path = os.path.join(g_dir, json_name)
        ok, meta, err = _run_convert(cli, src_path, glb_path, json_path, unit)

        if ok:
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE part_models SET status='ready', glb_name=?, meta_json=?, error_msg='' WHERE id=?",
                (glb_name, json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False) if meta else "", mid))
            conn.commit()
            write_audit(u["uid"], "upload_model3d",
                        "project %s part %s file %s -> %s" % (pid, ptid, original, glb_name))
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "id": mid, "status": "ready", "meta": meta})
        else:
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE part_models SET status='failed', error_msg=? WHERE id=?",
                (err, mid))
            conn.commit()
            write_audit(u["uid"], "upload_model3d",
                        "project %s part %s file %s failed: %s" % (pid, ptid, original, err))
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "id": mid, "status": "failed", "error": err})

    @app.route("/api/models3d/file/<int:fid>/meta")
    def api_models3d_file_meta(fid):
        """零件文件（STEP/IGES/STL/BREP）的几何元数据；首次调用时按需转换。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM files WHERE id=?", (fid,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return jsonify({"error": "文件不存在"}), 404
        if not _can_view_model(u, row["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目"}), 403
        glb, meta, err = _ensure_file_glb(fid, row)
        if not glb:
            return jsonify({"error": err or "转换失败"}), 500
        return jsonify(meta or {})

    @app.route("/api/models3d/file/<int:fid>/glb")
    def api_models3d_file_glb(fid):
        """零件文件的 GLB 预览；首次调用时按需转换并缓存。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM files WHERE id=?", (fid,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return jsonify({"error": "文件不存在"}), 404
        if not _can_view_model(u, row["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问该项目"}), 403
        glb, meta, err = _ensure_file_glb(fid, row)
        if not glb:
            return jsonify({"error": err or "转换失败"}), 500
        base = (row["original_name"] or "model")
        name = base.rsplit(".", 1)[0] + ".glb"
        resp = send_file(glb, mimetype="model/gltf-binary",
                         as_attachment=False, download_name=name)
        resp.headers["Cache-Control"] = "private, max-age=0, must-revalidate"
        return resp

    @app.route("/api/models3d/<int:mid>/glb")
    def api_models3d_glb(mid):
        """供 three.js 加载的 GLB。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM part_models WHERE id=?", (mid,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return jsonify({"error": "模型不存在"}), 404
        if not _can_view_model(u, row["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问"}), 403
        if row["status"] != "ready" or not row["glb_name"]:
            return jsonify({"error": "该模型暂无可预览的转换结果"}), 404
        path = _locate_glb(row["glb_name"])
        if not path:
            return jsonify({"error": "转换结果文件已丢失"}), 404
        resp = send_file(path, mimetype="model/gltf-binary",
                         as_attachment=False,
                         download_name=row["glb_name"])
        # 模型 id 稳定、内容仅在 retry 时重生成：交给浏览器缓存并强制重校验，
        # 未变化时服务端只回 304，避免每个用户每次查看都整份重下 GLB（并发浏览更省带宽/IO）。
        resp.headers["Cache-Control"] = "private, max-age=0, must-revalidate"
        return resp

    @app.route("/api/models3d/<int:mid>/source")
    def api_models3d_source(mid):
        """下载原始模型文件。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM part_models WHERE id=?", (mid,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return jsonify({"error": "模型不存在"}), 404
        if not _can_view_model(u, row["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权访问"}), 403
        path = _locate_model(row["storage_name"])
        if not path:
            return jsonify({"error": "源文件已丢失"}), 404
        return send_file(path, as_attachment=True, download_name=row["original_name"])

    @app.route("/api/models3d/<int:mid>", methods=["DELETE"])
    def api_models3d_delete(mid):
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM part_models WHERE id=?", (mid,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return jsonify({"error": "模型不存在"}), 404
        if not _can_manage_model(u, row["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权删除"}), 403
        # 删除实体文件（尽力而为）
        try:
            p = _locate_model(row["storage_name"])
            if p:
                os.remove(p)
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            if row["glb_name"]:
                g = _locate_glb(row["glb_name"])
                if g:
                    os.remove(g)
                j = g[:-4] + ".json" if g and g.endswith(".glb") else None
                if j and os.path.exists(j):
                    os.remove(j)
        except Exception:
            pass
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM part_models WHERE id=?", (mid,))
        conn.commit()
        write_audit(u["uid"], "delete_model3d", "model %s (%s)" % (mid, row["original_name"]))
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @app.route("/api/models3d/<int:mid>/retry", methods=["POST"])
    def api_models3d_retry(mid):
        """转换失败后重试（例如刚装好 CLI）。"""
        u = get_request_user()
        if not u:
            return jsonify({"error": "未登录"}), 401
        conn = get_db()
        row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM part_models WHERE id=?", (mid,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return jsonify({"error": "模型不存在"}), 404
        if not _can_manage_model(u, row["project_id"]):
            return jsonify({"error": "无权操作"}), 403
        cli = find_cli()
        if not cli:
            return jsonify({"error": "未找到 3D 转换工具 npi_occt_cli.exe"}), 400
        src = _locate_model(row["storage_name"])
        if not src:
            return jsonify({"error": "源文件已丢失，请重新上传"}), 404
        root = get_current_upload_root()
        g_dir = _glb_dir(root)
        os.makedirs(g_dir, exist_ok=True)
        glb_name = "%d.glb" % mid
        json_name = "%d.json" % mid
        ok, meta, err = _run_convert(cli, src, os.path.join(g_dir, glb_name),
                                     os.path.join(g_dir, json_name), "mm")
        if ok:
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE part_models SET status='ready', glb_name=?, meta_json=?, error_msg='' WHERE id=?",
                (glb_name, json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False) if meta else "", mid))
            conn.commit()
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "status": "ready", "meta": meta})
        conn.execute("UPDATE part_models SET status='failed', error_msg=? WHERE id=?", (err, mid))
        conn.commit()
        return jsonify({"error": err}), 400

14. 前端单页应用 templates/main.html

原生 HTML/CSS/JS 实现的单页前端（无框架，自绘 Element UI 风格组件）。包含登录页、左侧项目/零件/阶段/文档类型树、文件列表与上传、系统管理（用户/职位/项目权限/阶段模板/审计/设置）、权限设置弹窗、批量授权弹窗等全部交互逻辑；子文件夹支持右键重命名/删除，顶部工具栏删除按钮亦支持子文件夹。

查看 / 复制 源代码（3618 行）

<!-- 自定义输入对话框 -->
<div class="confirm-mask" id="promptMask">
  <div class="confirm-box">
    <div class="confirm-head" id="promptTitle">输入</div>
    <div class="confirm-body">
      <div class="prompt-msg" id="promptMsg"></div>
      <input type="text" id="promptInput" class="prompt-input" maxlength="120" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="请输入..." onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();onPromptOk();}else if(event.key==='Escape'){event.preventDefault();closePrompt();}">
      <div class="prompt-err" id="promptErr"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="confirm-foot">
      <button class="btn" onclick="closePrompt()">取消</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" id="promptOk" onclick="onPromptOk()">确定</button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
const API = '';  // 同源
let CURRENT = {user:null, projects:[], treeFilter:''};
// 批量授权：按职位分组的矩阵覆盖集 {posKey: {'阶段|子文件夹': perm}}，posKey 为小写职位名，'' 表示「全部」标签
let BATCH_POS_PERM = {};
let BATCH_CUR_POS = '';

function api(path, opts){
  opts = opts||{};
  const o = Object.assign({credentials:'same-origin'}, opts);
  if(o.body && typeof o.body!=='string' && !(o.body instanceof FormData)){ o.body = JSON.stringify(o.body); }
  if(o.body && !(o.body instanceof FormData) && !o.headers) o.headers = {'Content-Type':'application/json'};
  return fetch(API+path, o).then(r=>{
    if(r.status===401){ return r.json().catch(()=>null); }
    return r.json().catch(()=>({error:'解析失败'}));
  });
}
function toast(msg, isErr){
  const t=document.getElementById('toast'); t.textContent=msg;
  t.className='toast'+(isErr?' err':'');
  void t.offsetWidth;
  t.classList.add('show'); clearTimeout(t._t);
  t._t=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'), 2400);
}
let confirmCallback=null;
function askConfirm(title, body, cb, okText, okClass, boxClass){
  document.getElementById('confirmTitle').textContent=title||'确认';
  document.getElementById('confirmBody').innerHTML=body||'';
  const okBtn=document.getElementById('confirmOk');
  okBtn.textContent=okText||'确定';
  okBtn.className='btn '+(okClass||'btn-primary');
  document.getElementById('confirmBox').className='confirm-box '+(boxClass||'');
  confirmCallback=cb;
  const el=document.getElementById('confirmMask');
  el.style.zIndex = ++overlayZ;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function onConfirmOk(){
  const cb=confirmCallback; confirmCallback=null; closeConfirm();
  if(cb) cb();
}
function closeConfirm(){ document.getElementById('confirmMask').classList.remove('show'); confirmCallback=null; }

/* 自定义输入弹窗（替代浏览器原生 prompt） */
let promptCallback=null, promptResolve=null;
function askPrompt(title, msg, defaultValue, cb, okText, okClass){
  document.getElementById('promptTitle').textContent=title||'输入';
  document.getElementById('promptMsg').textContent=msg||'';
  const inp=document.getElementById('promptInput');
  inp.value=defaultValue||'';
  document.getElementById('promptErr').textContent='';
  const okBtn=document.getElementById('promptOk');
  okBtn.textContent=okText||'确定';
  okBtn.className='btn '+(okClass||'btn-primary');
  promptCallback=cb;
  const el=document.getElementById('promptMask');
  el.style.zIndex=++overlayZ;
  el.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>{ inp.focus(); inp.select(); },60);
}
function onPromptOk(){
  const v=(document.getElementById('promptInput').value||'').trim();
  if(!v){ document.getElementById('promptErr').textContent='请输入内容'; return; }
  const cb=promptCallback; promptCallback=null;
  if(cb) cb(v);       // 先回调（内部会清 promptResolve 并 resolve 值）
  closePrompt();      // 再关闭，避免误触发 null
}
function closePrompt(){
  document.getElementById('promptMask').classList.remove('show');
  promptCallback=null;
  if(promptResolve){ const r=promptResolve; promptResolve=null; r(null); }
}
function askPromptAsync(title, msg, defaultValue, okText){
  return new Promise(resolve=>{
    promptResolve=resolve;
    askPrompt(title, msg, defaultValue, v=>{ const r=promptResolve; promptResolve=null; if(r) r(v); }, okText);
  });
}

async function doLogin(){
  const uid=document.getElementById('loginUid').value.trim();
  const pw=document.getElementById('loginPw').value;
  const err=document.getElementById('loginErr'); err.textContent='';
  const btn=document.getElementById('loginBtn');
  const card=document.getElementById('loginCard');
  if(!uid||!pw){ err.textContent='工号和密码均必填'; card.classList.add('shake'); setTimeout(()=>card.classList.remove('shake'),400); return; }
  btn.disabled=true; btn.innerHTML='<span class="spinner"></span>登录中…';
  const r=await api('/api/login',{method:'POST',body:{uid,password:pw}});
  btn.disabled=false; btn.innerHTML='登 录';
  if(r.error){ err.textContent=r.error; card.classList.add('shake'); setTimeout(()=>card.classList.remove('shake'),400); return; }
  if(r.must_change){ CURRENT.user={uid:r.uid,name:r.name,role:r.role}; enforceForceChange(); return; }
  await initApp();
}
function doLogout(){ api('/api/logout',{method:'POST'}).then(()=>location.reload()); }

async function initApp(me){
  if(!me) me = await api('/api/me');
  if(!me || !me.uid || me.error){ CURRENT.user=null; return; }
  CURRENT.user=me;
  document.getElementById('loginWrap').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('header').style.display='flex';
  document.getElementById('layout').style.display='flex';
  document.getElementById('hdrUser').textContent=me.name;
  const rb=document.getElementById('hdrRole');
  const map={superAdmin:['sa','SA'],projectAdmin:['pa','PA'],pd:['pd','PD']};
  rb.className='role-badge '+(map[me.role]?map[me.role][0]:'pd');
  rb.textContent=map[me.role]?map[me.role][1]:'PD';
  renderHeaderNav();
  document.getElementById('sbActions').style.display=(me.role==='superAdmin'||me.role==='projectAdmin')?'flex':'none';
  await loadTree();
}

function renderHeaderNav(){
  const u=CURRENT.user;
  const el=document.getElementById('hdrNav');
  if(el) el.innerHTML='';
  const dd=document.getElementById('hdrDropdown');
  const menu=document.getElementById('mgmtMenu');
  if(!dd || !menu) return;
  const canAdmin=u.role==='superAdmin'||u.role==='projectAdmin';
  const canSys=u.role==='superAdmin';
  dd.style.display='block';
  const roleName={superAdmin:'系统管理员',projectAdmin:'项目管理员',pd:'一般用户'}[u.role]||'一般用户';
  let html='<div class="hdr-menu-user">'
    +'<div class="u-name">'+esc(u.name)+' <span style="color:var(--t3);font-weight:400">('+esc(u.uid)+')</span></div>'
    +'<div class="u-role">'+esc(roleName)+'</div>'
    +'</div>';
  if(canAdmin || canSys){
    html+='<div class="hdr-menu-group-title">系统管理</div>';
    if(canAdmin) html+='<button onclick="openMgmt(\'projperms\')"><span class="ic">⚙</span>项目权限</button>';
    if(canSys){
      html+='<button onclick="openMgmt(\'users\')"><span class="ic">👥</span>用户管理</button>';
      html+='<button onclick="openMgmt(\'positions\')"><span class="ic">🏢</span>职位管理</button>';
      html+='<button onclick="openMgmt(\'audit\')"><span class="ic">📋</span>审计日志</button>';
      html+='<button onclick="openMgmt(\'phasetmpl\')"><span class="ic">📁</span>阶段模板</button>';
      html+='<button onclick="openMgmt(\'settings\')"><span class="ic">🔧</span>系统设置</button>';
    }
    html+='<div class="sep"></div>';
  }
  html+='<div class="hdr-menu-group-title">个人</div>';
  html+='<button onclick="openMgmtPw()"><span class="ic">🔒</span>修改密码</button>';
  html+='<button onclick="openMgmtLogout()"><span class="ic">🚪</span>退出</button>';
  menu.innerHTML=html;
}
function openMgmt(tab){ closeMgmtMenu(); openModal('adminMask'); openAdminTab(tab); }
function openMgmtPw(){ closeMgmtMenu(); showChangePw(); }
function openMgmtLogout(){ closeMgmtMenu(); doLogout(); }
function toggleMgmtMenu(e){ if(e) e.stopPropagation(); const m=document.getElementById('mgmtMenu'); if(m) m.classList.toggle('hidden'); }
function closeMgmtMenu(){ const m=document.getElementById('mgmtMenu'); if(m) m.classList.add('hidden'); }
document.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if(!e.target.closest('.hdr-dropdown')) closeMgmtMenu(); });

// 侧边栏宽度拖拽调整
(function initSidebarResizer(){
  const sidebar=document.getElementById('sidebar');
  const resizer=document.getElementById('sbResizer');
  if(!sidebar || !resizer) return;
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('npisidebarWidth');
  if(saved) sidebar.style.width = saved + 'px';
  let dragging=false, startX=0, startW=0;
  resizer.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e){
    dragging=true;
    startX=e.clientX;
    startW=sidebar.offsetWidth;
    resizer.classList.add('dragging');
    document.body.style.cursor='col-resize';
    document.body.style.userSelect='none';
    e.preventDefault();
  });
  document.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e){
    if(!dragging) return;
    let w = startW + (e.clientX - startX);
    const min = 200, max = 500;
    if(w < min) w = min;
    if(w > max) w = max;
    sidebar.style.width = w + 'px';
  });
  document.addEventListener('mouseup', function(){
    if(!dragging) return;
    dragging=false;
    resizer.classList.remove('dragging');
    document.body.style.cursor='';
    document.body.style.userSelect='';
    localStorage.setItem('npisidebarWidth', sidebar.offsetWidth);
  });
})();

let TREE=[]; // 扁平树数据
async function loadTree(){
  const r=await api('/api/projects');
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
  CURRENT.projects=r;
  // 载入各项目内角色，供树节点权限判断
  const ids=r.map(p=>p.id).join(',');
  const multi=await api('/api/permissions/multi?pids='+ids);
  CURRENT.roles=multi;
  // 预载阶段文档模板
  const docs=await api('/api/phase-docs');
  CURRENT.templates=docs.templates||[];
  CURRENT.custom=docs.custom||[];
  buildTreeData(r);
  renderTree();
}

function buildTreeData(projects){
  TREE=[];
  projects.forEach(p=>{
    TREE.push({level:0,type:'project',id:p.id,projId:p.id,name:p.project_no,canManage:p.canManage,hidden:!!p.hidden,file_count:p.file_count||0,part_count:(p.parts||[]).length});
    (p.parts||[]).forEach(pt=>{
      // 提取 (phase, doc_type) → file_count 映射（来自后端）
      const dtMap = {};
      (pt.doc_types||[]).forEach(x=>{ if(x.doc_type!=null) dtMap[(x.phase||'')+'|'+(x.doc_type||'')] = x.file_count||0; });
      TREE.push({level:1,type:'part',id:pt.id,partId:pt.id,projId:p.id,name:pt.name,file_count:pt.file_count||0,phase_count:pt.phase_count||0});
      // 阶段：仅展示该零件实际拥有的阶段（由 part_phases 决定），不再强制显示全部模板阶段
      const phases = pt.phases || [];
      phases.forEach(ph=>{
        const phaseFc = Object.keys(dtMap).filter(k=>k.startsWith(ph+'|')).reduce((s,k)=>s+(dtMap[k]||0),0);
        TREE.push({level:2,type:'phase',projId:p.id,partId:pt.id,name:ph,phase:ph,file_count:phaseFc});
        const dts=new Set();
        const dtCtids={};
        // 1) 该零件该阶段实际有文件的分类（即使模板已删也显示）
        (pt.doc_types||[]).forEach(x=>{
          if(x.phase===ph && x.doc_type!=null) dts.add(x.doc_type);
        });
        // 2) 该零件/项目的自定义分类（创建时从模板快照，或后续手工添加）
        CURRENT.custom.forEach(c=>{
          if(c.phase===ph && (c.project_id==p.id || c.project_id==null)){
            if(!c.part_id || c.part_id==pt.id){
              dts.add(c.doc_type);
              dtCtids[c.doc_type]=c.id;
            }
          }
        });
        Array.from(dts).sort().forEach(dt=>{
          const fc = dtMap[ph+'|'+dt]||0;
          TREE.push({level:3,type:'doctype',projId:p.id,partId:pt.id,name:dt,phase:ph,ctid:dtCtids[dt]||'',file_count:fc});
        });
      });
    });
  });
}

function hasProjectAccess(projId){
  const u=CURRENT.user;
  if(u.role==='superAdmin') return true;
  const r=CURRENT.roles && CURRENT.roles[String(projId)];
  if(!r || !r.role_in_project) return false;
  return true;
}

function isProjAdmin(projId){
  const u=CURRENT.user;
  if(u.role==='superAdmin') return true;
  const role=CURRENT.roles && CURRENT.roles[String(projId)] && CURRENT.roles[String(projId)].role_in_project;
  return role==='superAdmin'||role==='projectAdmin';
}

// 项目内容（零件图片概览图、零件信息卡）编辑权：
// 项目管理员 或 本人在该项目任一文件夹被授予『可编辑』（与文档权限联动，只读人员仍只读）
function canEditProjectContent(projId){
  const u=CURRENT.user;
  if(u.role==='superAdmin') return true;
  const r=CURRENT.roles && CURRENT.roles[String(projId)];
  if(!r) return false;
  if(r.role_in_project==='superAdmin'||r.role_in_project==='projectAdmin') return true;
  const folders=r.folders||{};
  for(const k in folders){ if(folders[k]==='edit') return true; }
  return false;
}

// 零件信息面板的编辑能力见 canEditProjectContent。

function hasFolderAccess(projId, phase, docType){
  const u=CURRENT.user;
  if(u.role==='superAdmin') return true;
  const r=CURRENT.roles && CURRENT.roles[String(projId)];
  if(!r) return false;
  const folders=r.folders || {};
  // 阶段为 none 时，子文件夹强制不可访问
  if(phase && folders[phase]==='none') return false;
  const keys=[];
  if(phase && docType) keys.push(phase+'|'+docType);
  if(phase) keys.push(phase);
  keys.push('');
  for(const k of keys){
    const v=folders[k];
    if(v==='none') return false;
    if(v==='read' || v==='edit') return true;
  }
  // 子文件夹无独立记录时继承阶段权限
  if(phase && folders[phase]) return folders[phase] !== 'none';
  // 无阶段记录时回退到项目级权限
  return r.role_in_project==='superAdmin'||r.role_in_project==='projectAdmin';
}

function getFolderPerm(projId, phase, docType){
  const u=CURRENT.user;
  if(u.role==='superAdmin') return 'edit';
  const r=CURRENT.roles && CURRENT.roles[String(projId)];
  if(!r) return '';
  const folders=r.folders||{};
  // 阶段为 none 时，子文件夹强制无权限
  if(phase && folders[phase]==='none') return '';
  const keys=[];
  if(phase && docType) keys.push(phase+'|'+docType);
  if(phase) keys.push(phase);
  keys.push('');
  for(const k of keys){
    const v=folders[k];
    if(v==='none') return 'none';
    if(v==='read'||v==='edit') return v;
  }
  // 子文件夹无独立记录时继承阶段权限
  if(phase && folders[phase]) return folders[phase];
  return (r.role_in_project==='superAdmin'||r.role_in_project==='projectAdmin') ? 'edit' : '';
}

let COLLAPSED={}; // TREE 索引 -> true，表示折叠
function getTreeParent(i){
  if(i<=0) return -1;
  const lv=TREE[i].level;
  if(lv===0) return -1;
  for(let k=i-1;k>=0;k--){
    if(TREE[k].level===lv-1) return k;
  }
  return -1;
}
function renderTree(){
  const f=(document.getElementById('treeSearch').value||'').toLowerCase();
  const wrap=document.getElementById('tree');
  let html='';
  let skipLevel=-1;
  // 搜索可见性：自身匹配、匹配节点的祖先、匹配节点的子树 均显示
  const selfMatch=new Array(TREE.length).fill(false);
  const vis=new Array(TREE.length).fill(false);
  if(f){
    for(let i=0;i<TREE.length;i++){
      selfMatch[i]=TREE[i].name.toLowerCase().indexOf(f)>=0;
    }
    for(let i=0;i<TREE.length;i++){
      if(selfMatch[i]){
        let p=i;
        while(p>=0){ vis[p]=true; p=getTreeParent(p); }
        let j=i+1;
        while(j<TREE.length && TREE[j].level > TREE[i].level){ vis[j]=true; j++; }
      }
    }
  } else {
    vis.fill(true);
  }
  TREE.forEach((n,i)=>{
    if(skipLevel>=0){
      if(n.level>skipLevel) return;           // 仍在折叠子树内，跳过
      skipLevel=-1;                            // 遇到同级或更高级节点，退出子树
    }
    if(f && !vis[i]) return;
    const canAdmin=isProjAdmin(n.projId);
    const hasAccess=hasProjectAccess(n.projId);
    let dotClass='gray';
    if(n.type==='project'){
      if(!hasAccess) dotClass='gray';
      else if(n.file_count>0) dotClass='green';
      else if(n.part_count===0) dotClass='red';
      else dotClass='orange';
    } else if(n.type==='part'){
      if(!hasAccess) dotClass='gray';
      else if(n.file_count>0) dotClass='green';
      else if(n.phase_count===0) dotClass='red';
      else dotClass='orange';
    } else if(n.type==='phase'){
      // 阶段：按 folder_permissions 中 phase 级别判断
      const canSee=hasFolderAccess(n.projId, n.name||'', '');
      if(!canSee) dotClass='gray';
      else if(n.file_count>0) dotClass='green';
      else dotClass='red';
    } else if(n.type==='doctype'){
      // 文档类型：优先按 phase|docType，再按 phase 默认判断
      const canSee=hasFolderAccess(n.projId, n.phase||'', n.name||'');
      if(!canSee) dotClass='gray';
      else if(n.file_count>0) dotClass='green';
      else dotClass='red';
    } else {
      dotClass='gray';
    }
    const icon={project:'📁',part:'📦',phase:'📂',doctype:'📄'}[n.type];
    let count='';
    if(n.file_count!==undefined && n.file_count>0) count='<span class="count">'+n.file_count+'</span>';
    const isParent=(n.type!=='doctype');
    const collapsed=f ? false : (COLLAPSED[i]!==false); // 默认全部折叠；搜索时自动展开匹配节点
    const toggle=isParent
      ? '<span class="tn-toggle" onclick="toggleNode(event,'+i+')">▶</span>'
      : '<span class="tn-toggle" style="visibility:hidden">▶</span>';
    html+='<div class="tn lv'+n.level+(collapsed?' collapsed':'')+'" data-i="'+i+'" data-type="'+n.type+'" data-id="'+(n.id||'')+'" data-pid="'+n.projId+'" data-ptid="'+(n.partId||'')+'" data-phase="'+(n.phase||'')+'" data-name="'+esc(n.name)+'" data-ctid="'+(n.ctid||'')+'" data-hidden="'+(n.hidden?'1':'0')+'" onclick="selectNode(this)" oncontextmenu="showCtxMenu(event,this)">'
        +toggle
        +'<span class="status-dot '+dotClass+'"></span>'
        +'<span class="icon">'+(n.type==='project'&&n.hidden?'🙈':icon)+'</span>'
        +'<span class="label">'+esc(n.name)+'</span>'+count+'</div>';
    if(collapsed && !f) skipLevel=n.level;      // 折叠时隐藏其子树（搜索时忽略折叠）
  });
  wrap.innerHTML=html||'<div class="empty">无匹配项</div>';
}
function toggleNode(e,idx){ e.stopPropagation(); const cur=(COLLAPSED[idx]===false?false:true); COLLAPSED[idx]=!cur; renderTree(); }

/* 项目 / 零件 / 阶段 新建与删除 */
function createProject(){
  // 系统管理员或项目管理员可创建新项目；创建者默认成为该项目的项目管理员
  const u=CURRENT.user||{};
  if(u.role!=='superAdmin' && u.role!=='projectAdmin'){ toast('仅系统管理员或项目管理员可创建新项目',true); return; }
  document.getElementById('newProjNo').value='';
  document.getElementById('newProjParts').value='';
  document.querySelectorAll('#projCreateMask .phase-check input').forEach(c=>c.checked=false);
  updateProjPreview();
  openModal('projCreateMask');
}
function togglePhaseGroup(group, checked){
  const id = group==='tooling' ? 'phaseGroupTooling' : group==='dv' ? 'phaseGroupDv' : 'phaseGroupMp';
  const wrap = document.getElementById(id);
  if(!wrap) return;
  wrap.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]').forEach(c=>c.checked=checked);
  updateProjPreview();
}
function updateProjPreview(){
  const no=(document.getElementById('newProjNo').value||'').trim();
  const parts=(document.getElementById('newProjParts').value||'').split('\n').map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const phases=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#projCreateMask .phase-check input:checked')).map(c=>c.value);
  const box=document.getElementById('newProjPreview');
  if(!no && !parts.length && !phases.length){ box.innerHTML='<div class="preview-empty">请先填写项目编号和零件名称</div>'; return; }
  let html='<div class="root">📁 '+esc(no||'（未填写项目编号）')+'</div>';
  if(!parts.length){ html+='<div class="part">（未填写零件）</div>'; }
  else {
    parts.forEach(p=>{
      html+='<div class="part">📦 '+esc(p)+'</div>';
      if(!phases.length){ html+='<div class="phase">（未选择阶段）</div>'; }
      else { phases.forEach(ph=>{ html+='<div class="phase">📂 '+esc(ph)+'</div>'; }); }
    });
  }
  box.innerHTML=html;
}
function saveNewProject(){
  const project_no=(document.getElementById('newProjNo').value||'').trim();
  const parts=(document.getElementById('newProjParts').value||'').split('\n').map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const phases=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#projCreateMask .phase-check input:checked')).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!project_no){ toast('项目编号必填',true); return; }
  if(!parts.length){ toast('至少需要填写一个零件名称',true); return; }
  if(!phases.length){ toast('至少需要选择一个 NPI 阶段',true); return; }
  api('/api/projects',{method:'POST',body:{project_no,parts,phases}}).then(r=>{
    if(r.error) toast(r.error,true);
    else { toast('项目已创建'); closeModal('projCreateMask'); loadTree(); }
  });
}
function createPart(){
  let pid=SEL.projId;
  if(!pid){ toast('请先选择一个项目',true); return; }
  if(!isProjAdmin(pid)){ toast('需要项目管理员权限',true); return; }
  const projName=(TREE.find(n=>n.type==='project' && n.id==pid)||{}).name || ('项目#'+pid);
  document.getElementById('newPartProj').value=projName;
  document.getElementById('newPartName').value='';
  loadPartPhaseOptions();
  updatePartPreview();
  openModal('partCreateMask');
}
function loadPartPhaseOptions(){
  const wrap=document.getElementById('partPhaseList');
  wrap.innerHTML='<div class="preview-empty" style="padding:10px">加载中...</div>';
  api('/api/phase-templates').then(data=>{
    if(data.error){ wrap.innerHTML='<div class="preview-empty" style="padding:10px;color:var(--red)">加载失败</div>'; return; }
    const phases=Object.keys(data); // 后端已按 sort_order 排好序
    if(!phases.length){ wrap.innerHTML='<div class="preview-empty" style="padding:10px">暂无阶段模板</div>'; return; }
    // 按业务分组渲染，与截图一致：模具/治具 → 开发验证 → 其他
    const groups=[
      {title:'模具/治具阶段', list:['Tooling','Fixture']},
      {title:'开发验证阶段（P1-PVT）', list:['P1','P2','EVT','DVT','PVT']},
      {title:'量产阶段', list:['MP']},
      {title:'其他阶段', list:[]}
    ];
    const used=new Set();
    let html='';
    groups.forEach(g=>{
      let list=g.list.filter(ph=>phases.includes(ph));
      if(g.title==='其他阶段'){
        phases.forEach(ph=>{ if(!used.has(ph)) list.push(ph); });
      }
      if(!list.length) return;
      list.forEach(ph=>used.add(ph));
      html+='<div class="phase-sub-group" data-group="'+esc(g.title)+'" style="margin-bottom:12px">'
        +'<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:var(--t1);margin-bottom:8px">'+esc(g.title)
        +' <span class="link" onclick="togglePartPhaseGroup(\''+esc(g.title)+'\',true)">全选</span>'
        +' <span class="link" onclick="togglePartPhaseGroup(\''+esc(g.title)+'\',false)">取消全选</span></div>'
        +'<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px">'
        +list.map(ph=>`<label class="phase-check"><input type="checkbox" value="${esc(ph)}" checked onchange="updatePartPreview()"> <span>${esc(ph)}</span></label>`).join('')
        +'</div></div>';
    });
    wrap.innerHTML=html;
    updatePartPreview();
  }).catch(()=>{
    wrap.innerHTML='<div class="preview-empty" style="padding:10px;color:var(--red)">加载失败</div>';
  });
}
function toggleAllPartPhases(checked){
  document.querySelectorAll('#partPhaseList input[type=checkbox]').forEach(c=>c.checked=checked);
  updatePartPreview();
}
function togglePartPhaseGroup(title, checked){
  const g=document.querySelector('#partPhaseList .phase-sub-group[data-group="'+CSS.escape(title)+'"]');
  if(!g) return;
  g.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]').forEach(c=>c.checked=checked);
  updatePartPreview();
}
function updatePartPreview(){
  const raw=document.getElementById('newPartName').value||'';
  const names=raw.split(/\n|\r/).map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const phases=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#partPhaseList .phase-check input:checked')).map(c=>c.value);
  const box=document.getElementById('newPartPreview');
  if(!names.length && !phases.length){ box.innerHTML='<div class="preview-empty">请输入零件名称并选择阶段</div>'; return; }
  let html='';
  if(!names.length){ html='<div class="root">📦 （未填写零件名称）</div>'; }
  else {
    names.slice(0,8).forEach(name=>{ html+='<div class="root">📦 '+esc(name)+'</div>'; });
    if(names.length>8) html+='<div class="phase">… 还有 '+(names.length-8)+' 个零件</div>';
  }
  if(!phases.length){ html+='<div class="phase">（未选择阶段）</div>'; }
  else { phases.forEach(ph=>{ html+='<div class="phase">📂 '+esc(ph)+'</div>'; }); }
  box.innerHTML=html;
}
function saveNewPart(){
  const raw=document.getElementById('newPartName').value||'';
  const names=raw.split(/\n|\r/).map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  if(!names.length){ toast('零件名称必填',true); return; }
  if(names.length>50){ toast('一次最多创建 50 个零件',true); return; }
  const phases=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#partPhaseList .phase-check input:checked')).map(c=>c.value);
  if(!phases.length){ toast('请至少选择一个 NPI 阶段',true); return; }
  const btn=document.querySelector('#partCreateMask .btn-primary');
  if(btn){ btn.disabled=true; btn.textContent='创建中…'; }
  api('/api/projects/'+SEL.projId+'/parts',{method:'POST',body:{names, phases}}).then(r=>{
    if(btn){ btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent='确认创建'; }
    if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('已创建 '+r.created+' 个零件'); closeModal('partCreateMask'); loadTree(); }
  });
}
function createPhase(){
  let ptid=SEL.partId;
  if(!ptid){ toast('请先选择一个零件',true); return; }
  if(!isProjAdmin(SEL.projId)){ toast('需要项目管理员权限',true); return; }
  const projName=(TREE.find(n=>n.type==='project' && n.id==SEL.projId)||{}).name || ('项目#'+SEL.projId);
  const partName=(TREE.find(n=>n.type==='part' && n.id==ptid)||{}).name || ('零件#'+ptid);
  document.getElementById('newPhaseParent').value=projName+' / '+partName;
  // 重置：默认切回「模版」Tab，清空手动输入
  setPhaseSrc('template');
  const ta=document.getElementById('newPhaseNames'); if(ta) ta.value='';
  loadPhaseTemplateOptions();
  updatePhasePreview();
  openModal('phaseCreateMask');
}
function setPhaseSrc(v){
  const tabT=document.getElementById('phaseSrcTabTemplate');
  const tabM=document.getElementById('phaseSrcTabManual');
  const isTpl=(v==='template');
  tabT.classList.toggle('active', isTpl);
  tabM.classList.toggle('active', !isTpl);
  document.getElementById('phaseSrcTemplate').style.display = isTpl ? '' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('phaseSrcManual').style.display    = isTpl ? 'none' : '';
  document.getElementById('phaseSrcHintTemplate').style.display = isTpl ? '' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('phaseSrcHintManual').style.display   = isTpl ? 'none' : '';
  updatePhasePreview();
}
function loadPhaseTemplateOptions(){
  const wrap=document.getElementById('phaseTplList');
  wrap.innerHTML='<div class="preview-empty" style="padding:14px">加载中...</div>';
  api('/api/phase-templates').then(data=>{
    if(data.error){ wrap.innerHTML='<div class="preview-empty" style="padding:14px;color:var(--red)">加载失败</div>'; return; }
    const phases=Object.keys(data);
    if(!phases.length){ wrap.innerHTML='<div class="preview-empty" style="padding:14px">暂无阶段模板，请先到「阶段模板」中配置</div>'; return; }
    const groups=[
      {title:'模具 / 治具阶段', list:['Tooling','Fixture']},
      {title:'开发验证阶段（P1 → PVT）', list:['P1','P2','EVT','DVT','PVT']},
      {title:'量产阶段', list:['MP']},
      {title:'其他阶段', list:[]}
    ];
    const used=new Set();
    let html='';
    groups.forEach(g=>{
      let list=g.list.filter(ph=>phases.includes(ph));
      if(g.title==='其他阶段'){
        phases.forEach(ph=>{ if(!used.has(ph)) list.push(ph); });
      }
      if(!list.length) return;
      list.forEach(ph=>used.add(ph));
      const subNames = list.reduce((acc,ph)=>acc.concat(data[ph]||[]), []);
      const meta = list.length + ' 个可选 · 合计 ' + subNames.length + ' 个子文件夹';
      html+='<div class="phase-group">'
        +'<div class="phase-group-title"><span class="ic"></span>'+esc(g.title)
        +'<span class="meta">'+meta+'</span></div>'
        +'<div class="phase-pill-row">'
        +list.map(ph=>{
            const dts=data[ph]||[];
            const sub=dts.length ? '<span class="sub-count">'+dts.length+'</span>' : '';
            const tip = dts.length ? ' title="含 '+dts.length+' 个子文件夹：'+esc(dts.join('、'))+'"' : ' title="无预设子文件夹"';
            const subs = dts.length ? String(dts.length) : '0';
            const subAttr = dts.length ? dts.join('|').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;') : '';
            return '<span class="phase-pill" data-phase="'+esc(ph)+'" data-subcount="'+subs+'" data-subs="'+subAttr+'"'+tip+' onclick="togglePhasePill(this)"><span class="check">✓</span>'+esc(ph)+sub+'</span>';
          }).join('')
        +'</div></div>';
    });
    wrap.innerHTML=html;
    updatePhasePreview();
  }).catch(()=>{
    wrap.innerHTML='<div class="preview-empty" style="padding:14px;color:var(--red)">加载失败</div>';
  });
}
function togglePhasePill(el){
  el.classList.toggle('checked');
  updatePhasePreview();
}
function getSelectedPhases(){
  const tabTpl=document.getElementById('phaseSrcTabTemplate');
  if(!tabTpl.classList.contains('active')) return [];
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#phaseTplList .phase-pill.checked')).map(el=>el.dataset.phase);
}
function updatePhasePreview(){
  const phases=getSelectedPhases();
  document.getElementById('phasePreviewCount').textContent=phases.length;
  const list=document.getElementById('phasePreviewList');
  if(!phases.length){
    list.innerHTML='<span class="phase-preview-empty">尚未选择</span>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = phases.map(ph=>{
    const pill=document.querySelector('#phaseTplList .phase-pill[data-phase="'+CSS.escape(ph)+'"]');
    const sub=pill ? parseInt(pill.dataset.subcount||'0',10) : 0;
    const cls = sub>0 ? '' : 'muted';
    const subTxt = sub>0 ? ' · '+sub+' 子文件夹' : ' · 无预设';
    return '<span class="prev-tag '+cls+'">'+esc(ph)+'<span style="opacity:.7;font-size:10px">'+subTxt+'</span></span>';
  }).join('');
}
function saveNewPhase(){
  let phases=[];
  const tabTpl=document.getElementById('phaseSrcTabTemplate');
  if(tabTpl.classList.contains('active')){
    phases=getSelectedPhases();
    if(!phases.length){ toast('请至少选择一个 NPI 阶段',true); return; }
  } else {
    const ta=document.getElementById('newPhaseNames').value||'';
    phases=ta.split(/\n|\r/).map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if(!phases.length){ toast('请输入至少一个阶段名称',true); return; }
  }
  const btn=document.querySelector('#phaseCreateMask .btn-primary');
  if(btn){ btn.disabled=true; btn.textContent='创建中…'; }
  api('/api/parts/'+SEL.partId+'/phases',{method:'POST',body:{phases}}).then(r=>{
    if(btn){ btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent='确认创建'; }
    if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
    const msg=(r.message && r.message!=='已创建 0 个阶段') ? r.message : ('阶段已创建：'+r.created.join('、'));
    toast(msg);
    closeModal('phaseCreateMask');
    loadTree();
  });
}
function deleteSelectedNode(){
  const n=SEL;
  if(n.type==='project' && n.projId){
    if(!isProjAdmin(n.projId)){ toast('需要项目管理员权限',true); return; }
    askConfirm('删除项目','确认删除该项目及其下所有零件、文件？此操作不可恢复。',()=>{
      api('/api/projects/'+n.projId,{method:'DELETE'}).then(r=>{
        if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('项目已删除'); SEL={}; loadTree(); renderToolbar(); }
      });
    },'删除','btn-danger');
  } else if(n.type==='part' && n.partId){
    if(!isProjAdmin(n.projId)){ toast('需要项目管理员权限',true); return; }
    askConfirm('删除零件','确认删除该零件及其下所有文件？此操作不可恢复。',()=>{
      api('/api/parts/'+n.partId,{method:'DELETE'}).then(r=>{
        if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('零件已删除'); SEL={}; loadTree(); renderToolbar(); }
      });
    },'删除','btn-danger');
  } else if(n.type==='phase' && n.phase){
    if(!isProjAdmin(n.projId)){ toast('需要项目管理员权限',true); return; }
    askConfirm('删除阶段','确认删除该阶段？',()=>{
      api('/api/parts/'+n.partId+'/phases/'+encodeURIComponent(n.phase),{method:'DELETE'}).then(r=>{
        if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('阶段已删除'); loadTree(); }
      });
    },'删除','btn-danger');
  } else if(n.type==='doctype' && n.phase){
    if(!isProjAdmin(n.projId)){ toast('需要项目管理员权限',true); return; }
    askConfirm('删除文件夹','确认删除该文件夹？该文件夹下的所有文件将被永久删除，且无法恢复。',()=>{
      api('/api/custom-doc-types/delete',{
        method:'POST',
        body:{
          project_id:+n.projId,
          part_id:+n.partId||null,
          phase:n.phase,
          doc_type:n.docType||n.name
        }
      }).then(r=>{
        if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('文件夹已删除'); SEL={}; loadTree(); renderToolbar(); }
      });
    },'删除','btn-danger');
  }
}

function esc(s){ return (s||'').replace(/[&<>]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;'}[c])); }
function escAttr(s){ return (s||'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c])); }
function fmtTime(s){ if(!s) return ''; s=String(s); return s.length>16? s.slice(0,16): s; }

let SEL={};
function selectNode(el){
  p3dDestroy();   // 切换节点时销毁上一零件的内联 3D 渲染，释放 WebGL 上下文
  document.querySelectorAll('.tn').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('selected'));
  el.classList.add('selected');
  const type=el.dataset.type;
  SEL={projId:+el.dataset.pid, partId:el.dataset.ptid?+el.dataset.ptid:null, phase:el.dataset.phase||null, docType: type==='doctype'?el.dataset.name:null, ctid:el.dataset.ctid||'', name:el.dataset.name||'', type};
  renderToolbar();
  // 零件节点 → 在右侧渲染该零件的 3D 图面板（独立于普通文件区）
  if(type==='part'){ renderPartOverview(); return; }
  loadFiles();
}

function renderToolbar(){
  const tb=document.getElementById('toolbar');
  const proj=TREE.find(n=>n.projId===SEL.projId && n.type==='project');
  let crumb='<div class="crumb-group"><span class="crumb-item" onclick="goRoot()">项目</span>';
  if(proj) crumb+='<span class="crumb-sep">/</span><span class="crumb-item" onclick="selectByData('+SEL.projId+',\'project\')">'+esc(proj.name)+'</span>';
  if(SEL.partId){ const pt=TREE.find(n=>n.id==SEL.partId); if(pt) crumb+='<span class="crumb-sep">/</span><span class="crumb-current">'+esc(pt.name)+'</span>'; }
  if(SEL.phase) crumb+='<span class="crumb-sep">/</span><span class="crumb-current">'+esc(SEL.phase)+'</span>';
  if(SEL.docType) crumb+='<span class="crumb-sep">/</span><span class="crumb-current">'+esc(SEL.docType)+'</span>';
  crumb+='</div>';
  const canAdmin=isProjAdmin(SEL.projId);
  // 普通上传仅允许在「文档类型文件夹」节点执行（files.doc_type 为 NOT NULL，
  // 阶段/项目/零件节点无具体文件夹归属，上传会失败）
  // 零件 3D 图直接取该零件文件夹里的 3D 文件（STEP/IGES/STL/BREP）渲染，无需单独上传。
  // 项目节点不展示概览图（项目级概览图已取消，直接进入文件列表）。
  const folderPerm = getFolderPerm(SEL.projId, SEL.phase, SEL.docType);
  const canUpload = (SEL.type==='doctype') && folderPerm==='edit';
  const showDel=canAdmin && SEL.type;
  tb.innerHTML=crumb
    +'<span class="file-count" id="fc"></span>'
    +'<div class="spacer"></div>'
    +'<input type="text" id="fileSearch" placeholder="搜索文件名" oninput="loadFiles()">'
    +(canUpload ? '<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openUpload()">+ 上传文件</button>' : '<button class="btn btn-primary" disabled title="'+(SEL.type==='doctype'?'该文件夹为只读，无上传权限':'请选择具体文档类型文件夹后再上传文件')+'">+ 上传文件</button>')
    + (showDel?'<button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="deleteSelectedNode()">删除</button>':'');
  const banner=document.getElementById('secBanner');
  if(canAdmin) banner.textContent='● 当前项目您拥有管理权限';
  else banner.textContent='● 一般用户视图，仅显示您有权限的文件';
}

function goRoot(){ p3dDestroy(); SEL={}; document.querySelectorAll('.tn').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('selected')); renderToolbar(); document.getElementById('content').innerHTML='<div class="empty">请从左侧选择项目/零件/阶段查看文件</div>'; }
function selectByData(id,type){ const el=document.querySelector('.tn[data-id="'+id+'"][data-type="'+type+'"]'); if(el) selectNode(el); }

async function loadFiles(){
  p3dDestroy();   // 进入文件视图时销毁内联 3D 渲染
  const wrap=document.getElementById('content');
  wrap.innerHTML='<div class="loading-box"><div class="spinner"></div>正在加载文件…</div>';
  const q=new URLSearchParams();
  if(SEL.projId) q.set('project_id',SEL.projId);
  if(SEL.partId) q.set('part_id',SEL.partId);
  if(SEL.phase) q.set('phase',SEL.phase);
  if(SEL.docType) q.set('doc_type',SEL.docType);
  const r=await api('/api/files?'+q.toString());
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
  const kw=(document.getElementById('fileSearch').value||'').toLowerCase();
  let list=r;
  if(kw) list=list.filter(f=>f.original_name.toLowerCase().indexOf(kw)>=0);
  const fc=document.getElementById('fc');
  if(fc) fc.textContent=list.length+' 个文件';
  if(!list.length){ wrap.innerHTML='<div class="empty">暂无文件，点击右上角「上传文件」</div>'; return; }
  let html='<table class="files-tbl"><thead><tr><th>文件名</th><th>阶段</th><th>文件分类</th><th>上传人</th><th>上传时间</th><th>操作</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  const currentUid=(CURRENT.user||{}).uid;
  const isAdmin=isProjAdmin(SEL.projId);
  list.forEach(f=>{
    const canDelete=isAdmin || f.uploader_id===currentUid;
    html+='<tr><td class="file-name" title="'+esc(f.original_name)+'">'+esc(f.original_name)+'</td>'
      +'<td title="'+esc(f.phase||'')+'">'+esc(f.phase||'')+'</td>'
      +'<td title="'+esc(f.doc_type||'')+'">'+esc(f.doc_type||'')+'</td>'
      +'<td>'+esc(f.uploader_name||f.uploader_id)+'</td>'
      +'<td title="'+esc(f.uploaded_at||'')+'">'+fmtTime(f.uploaded_at)+'</td>'
      +'<td><div class="file-actions">'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="downloadFile('+f.id+')">下载</button>'
      +(canDelete?'<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="delFile('+f.id+')">删除</button>':'')
      +'</div></td></tr>';
  });
  html+='</tbody></table>';
  wrap.innerHTML=html;
}

function openUpload(){
  const inp=document.createElement('input'); inp.type='file'; inp.multiple=true;
  inp.accept='.png,.jpg,.jpeg,.gif,.webp,.bmp,.pdf,.xlsx,.xls,.doc,.docx,.txt,.zip,.ppt,.pptx,.stp,.step,.dwg,.dxf,.igs,.iges,.catpart,.catproduct,.stl,.sldprt,.sldasm,.x_t,.x_b,.sat';
  inp.onchange=async()=>{
    const files=inp.files; if(!files.length) return;
    const fd=new FormData();
    for(const f of files) fd.append('file',f);
    if(SEL.projId) fd.append('project_id',SEL.projId);
    if(SEL.partId) fd.append('part_id',SEL.partId);
    if(SEL.phase) fd.append('phase',SEL.phase);
    if(SEL.docType) fd.append('doc_type',SEL.docType);
    const r=await fetch('/api/upload',{method:'POST',body:fd,credentials:'same-origin'}).then(x=>x.json());
    if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
    toast('已上传 '+r.count+' 个文件');
    loadTree(); loadFiles();
  };
  inp.click();
}
function downloadFile(id){ window.open('/api/files/'+id+'/download','_blank'); }
function delFile(id){
  askConfirm('删除文件','确认删除该文件？删除后不可恢复。',()=>{
    api('/api/files/'+id,{method:'DELETE'}).then(r=>{
      if(r.error) toast(r.error,true);
      else { toast('已删除'); loadTree(); loadFiles(); }
    });
  },'删除','btn-danger');
}

/* ---------- 系统管理 ---------- */
const ADMIN_TABS=[
  {key:'users',label:'用户管理',render:'renderUsers',role:'superAdmin'},
  {key:'positions',label:'职位管理',render:'renderPositions',role:'superAdmin'},
  {key:'projperms',label:'项目权限',render:'renderProjPerms'},
  {key:'phasetmpl',label:'阶段模板',render:'renderPhaseTemplates',role:'superAdmin'},
  {key:'audit',label:'审计日志',render:'renderAudit',role:'superAdmin'},
  {key:'settings',label:'系统设置',render:'renderSettings',role:'superAdmin'},
];

function canAccessTab(tab){
  const u=CURRENT.user;
  if(!tab.role) return true;
  if(tab.role==='superAdmin') return u.role==='superAdmin';
  return false;
}

function openAdminTab(key){
  CURRENT.adminMode=key;
  renderAdminTabs();
  const tab=ADMIN_TABS.find(t=>t.key===key);
  if(tab && typeof window[tab.render]==='function') window[tab.render]();
}

function renderAdminTabs(){
  const u=CURRENT.user;
  const wrap=document.getElementById('adminTabs');
  let html='';
  ADMIN_TABS.forEach(tab=>{
    if(!canAccessTab(tab)) return;
    html+='<button class="modal-tab '+(CURRENT.adminMode===tab.key?'active':'')+'" onclick="openAdminTab(\''+tab.key+'\')">'+esc(tab.label)+'</button>';
  });
  wrap.innerHTML=html;
}

function openProjPerms(){ openModal('adminMask'); openAdminTab('projperms'); }
function openUserMgmt(){ openModal('adminMask'); openAdminTab('users'); }
function openAudit(){ openModal('adminMask'); openAdminTab('audit'); }
function openPositions(){ openModal('adminMask'); openAdminTab('positions'); }
function openPhaseTemplates(){ openModal('adminMask'); openAdminTab('phasetmpl'); }
function openSettings(){ openModal('adminMask'); openAdminTab('settings'); }

let PROJ_PERMS=[];
async function renderProjPerms(){
  const body=document.getElementById('adminBody');
  body.innerHTML='<div class="admin-toolbar">'
    +'<div class="proj-select-row"><label>选择项目</label><div class="custom-select"><select id="ppProj"></select></div></div>'
    +'<div class="spacer"></div>'
    +'<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openBatchForProj()">+ 授权用户</button>'
    +'<button class="btn" onclick="loadProjPerms()">🔄 刷新</button></div>'
    +'<div id="ppTable"></div>';
  const sel=document.getElementById('ppProj');
  CURRENT.projects.forEach(p=>{ const o=document.createElement('option'); o.value=p.id; o.textContent=p.project_no; sel.appendChild(o); });
  sel.onchange=loadProjPerms;
  loadProjPerms();
}
async function loadProjPerms(){
  const pid=document.getElementById('ppProj').value;
  const r=await api('/api/admin/project-permissions/'+pid);
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
  PROJ_PERMS=r;
  if(!r.length){ document.getElementById('ppTable').innerHTML='<div class="admin-empty">该项目暂无授权用户，点击「授权用户」添加。</div>'; return; }
  let html='<table class="admin-tbl"><thead><tr><th>用户</th><th>项目角色</th><th>权限概要</th><th style="width:140px">操作</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  r.forEach(pm=>{
    const roleTag = pm.roleInProject==='projectAdmin' ? '<span class="tag orange">项目管理员</span>' : '<span class="tag blue">一般用户</span>';
    html+='<tr><td>'+esc(pm.userName)+' <span style="color:var(--t3)">('+esc(pm.userId)+')</span></td>'
      +'<td><select onchange="changeRole('+pm.userId+',this.value)" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:12px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--radius-sm);background:var(--input-bg)">'
      +'<option value="pd" '+(pm.roleInProject==='pd'?'selected':'')+'>一般用户</option>'
      +'<option value="projectAdmin" '+(pm.roleInProject==='projectAdmin'?'selected':'')+'>项目管理员</option>'
      +'</select></td>'
      +'<td>'+esc(pm.phaseSummary)+'</td>'
      +'<td><div class="actions">'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="openPerm('+pid+',\''+pm.userId+'\',\''+esc(pm.userName)+'\',\''+pm.roleInProject+'\')">权限设置</button>'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="delPerm('+pid+',\''+pm.userId+'\')">删除</button></div></td></tr>';
  });
  html+='</tbody></table>';
  document.getElementById('ppTable').innerHTML=html;
}
function changeRole(uid,role){ api('/api/admin/project-permissions/'+document.getElementById('ppProj').value+'/'+uid,{method:'PUT',body:{roleInProject:role}}).then(r=>{ if(r.error)toast(r.error,true); else toast('角色已更新'); }); }
function delPerm(pid,uid){ askConfirm('撤销权限','确认撤销该用户的项目权限？',()=>{ api('/api/admin/project-permissions/'+pid+'/'+uid,{method:'DELETE'}).then(r=>{ if(r.error)toast(r.error,true); else {toast('已撤销');loadProjPerms();} }); },'撤销','btn-danger'); }
async function openBatchForProj(){
  await buildBatch();
  const currentPid=document.getElementById('ppProj').value;
  document.querySelectorAll('#batchProjList input').forEach(cb=>{ cb.checked=(cb.value===currentPid); });
  updateBatchHint('batchProjList','batchProjHint','个项目');
  renderBatchPermPanel();
  openModal('batchMask');
}
async function openPerm(pid,uid,uname,role){ CURRENT.fp={pid,uid,uname,role}; CURRENT.fpExpanded={}; const r=await api('/api/admin/project-permissions/'+pid+'/'+uid+'/folder-tree'); if(r.error){toast(r.error,true);return;} CURRENT.fpTree=r; renderPermTree(); openModal('permMask'); }
function renderPermTree(){
  const t=CURRENT.fpTree; const role=CURRENT.fp.role||'pd';
  let html='<div class="perm-info"><span>用户：</span><strong>'+esc(CURRENT.fp.uname)+' ('+esc(CURRENT.fp.uid)+')</strong></div>';
  const isAdmin = role==='projectAdmin';
  html+='<div class="perm-role-row"><label>项目角色</label><div class="custom-select"><select id="permRole" onchange="onPermRoleChange()">'
    +'<option value="pd" '+(role==='pd'?'selected':'')+'>一般用户</option>'
    +'<option value="projectAdmin" '+(isAdmin?'selected':'')+'>项目管理员</option>'
    +'</select></div></div>';
  if(isAdmin){
    html+='<div class="perm-admin-hint">项目管理员默认拥有该项目全部文件夹的可编辑权限，无需逐项设置。</div>';
  }
  html+='<div class="perm-btns" id="permBtns">'
    +'<button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="setPermAll(\'edit\')">全部设为可编辑</button>'
    +'<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="setPermAll(\'read\')">全部设为只读</button>'
    +'<button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="setPermAll(\'none\')">全部设为无权限</button>'
    +'</div>';
  html+='<div class="perm-tree" id="permTree">';
  const selDisabled = isAdmin ? ' disabled' : '';
  html+='<div class="perm-node root"><span class="perm-icon">📁</span><span class="perm-label">'+esc(t.projectNo)+'</span><select class="perm-sel" id="perm_root"'+selDisabled+'>'+permOpts(isAdmin?'edit':t.permission,true)+'</select></div>';
  html+='<div class="perm-hint">点击阶段可展开/折叠子文件夹；子文件夹未设置时继承阶段权限。</div>';
  (t.phases||[]).forEach(ph=>{
    const expanded = CURRENT.fpExpanded[ph.phase] === true;
    const hasKids = (ph.docTypes||[]).length > 0;
    html+='<div class="perm-node phase" data-phase="'+esc(ph.phase)+'" onclick="togglePermPhase(\''+esc(ph.phase)+'\')">'
      +'<span class="perm-toggle '+(hasKids?'':'empty')+(expanded?'':' collapsed')+'" onclick="event.stopPropagation();togglePermPhase(\''+esc(ph.phase)+'\')">▾</span>'
      +'<span class="perm-icon">📂</span><span class="perm-label">'+esc(ph.phase)+'</span>'
      +'<select class="perm-sel" data-phase="'+esc(ph.phase)+'" onclick="event.stopPropagation()" onchange="onPermPhaseChange(\''+esc(ph.phase)+'\')"'+selDisabled+'>'+permOpts(isAdmin?'edit':ph.permission,false)+'</select>'
      +'</div>';
    if(hasKids){
      html+='<div class="perm-children '+(expanded?'':'hidden')+'" id="permChildren_'+esc(ph.phase)+'">';
      ph.docTypes.forEach(dt=>{
        html+='<div class="perm-node doctype" data-phase="'+esc(ph.phase)+'" data-dt="'+esc(dt.docType)+'">'
          +'<span class="perm-toggle empty"></span><span class="perm-icon">📄</span>'
          +'<span class="perm-label">'+esc(dt.docType)+'</span>'
          +'<select class="perm-sel" data-phase="'+esc(ph.phase)+'" data-dt="'+esc(dt.docType)+'" onclick="event.stopPropagation()"'+selDisabled+'>'+permOpts(isAdmin?'edit':dt.permission,false)+'</select>'
          +'</div>';
      });
      html+='</div>';
    }
  });
  html+='</div>';
  document.getElementById('permBody').innerHTML=html;
  onPermRoleChange();
}
function permOpts(v,isRoot){
  const opts=isRoot
    ? [['','(不设，按阶段)'],['edit','可编辑'],['read','只读'],['none','无权限']]
    : [['','继承'],['edit','可编辑'],['read','只读'],['none','无权限']];
  return opts.map(o=>'<option value="'+o[0]+'" '+(o[0]===v?'selected':'')+'>'+o[1]+'</option>').join('');
}
function togglePermPhase(ph){
  const was=CURRENT.fpExpanded[ph]===true;
  CURRENT.fpExpanded[ph]=!was;
  const kids=document.getElementById('permChildren_'+ph);
  const toggle=document.querySelector('.perm-node.phase[data-phase="'+ph+'"] .perm-toggle');
  if(kids){ kids.classList.toggle('hidden',was); }
  if(toggle){ toggle.classList.toggle('collapsed',was); }
}
function setPermAll(val){
  document.getElementById('perm_root').value=val;
  document.querySelectorAll('#permBody .perm-sel').forEach(sel=>sel.value=val);
}
function onPermPhaseChange(ph){
  // 阶段权限变化时，子文件夹默认继承阶段权限（清空为继承）。
  // 如需单独授权，可在阶段设好后再展开单独调整。
  document.querySelectorAll('.perm-node.doctype[data-phase="'+ph+'"] .perm-sel').forEach(kid=>{
    kid.value='';
  });
}
function onPermRoleChange(){
  const role=document.getElementById('permRole').value;
  const isAdmin=role==='projectAdmin';
  // 项目管理员：全部设为可编辑并禁用；一般用户：保持当前值并启用
  document.querySelectorAll('#permBody .perm-sel').forEach(sel=>{
    sel.disabled=isAdmin;
    if(isAdmin) sel.value='edit';
  });
  const btns=document.getElementById('permBtns');
  if(btns) btns.style.display=isAdmin?'none':'flex';
  const tree=document.getElementById('permTree');
  if(tree) tree.classList.toggle('perm-admin',isAdmin);
}
function savePerm(){
  const pid=CURRENT.fp.pid, uid=CURRENT.fp.uid;
  const role=document.getElementById('permRole').value;
  // 严格按照用户当前选择的值保存；阶段与子文件夹可独立组合授权
  const entries=[];
  const root=document.getElementById('perm_root').value;
  if(root) entries.push({phase:null,permission:root});
  document.querySelectorAll('#permBody .perm-node.phase .perm-sel[data-phase]').forEach(sel=>{
    const v=sel.value; if(!v) return;
    entries.push({phase:sel.dataset.phase, docType:null, permission:v});
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#permBody .perm-node.doctype .perm-sel[data-dt]').forEach(sel=>{
    const v=sel.value; if(!v) return;
    entries.push({phase:sel.dataset.phase, docType:sel.dataset.dt, permission:v});
  });
  api('/api/admin/project-permissions/'+pid+'/'+uid,{method:'PUT',body:{roleInProject:role}}).then(r1=>{
    if(r1.error){ toast(r1.error,true); return; }
    api('/api/admin/project-permissions/'+pid+'/'+uid+'/folder-tree',{method:'PUT',body:{entries}}).then(r2=>{
      if(r2.error) toast(r2.error,true);
      else { toast('权限已保存'); closeModal('permMask'); loadProjPerms(); }
    });
  });
}

/* ---------- 批量授权 ---------- */
function buildBatch(){
  // 每次打开批量授权弹窗重置「按职位分组的矩阵覆盖集」
  BATCH_POS_PERM={}; BATCH_CUR_POS='';
  const p1 = api('/api/projects').then(ps=>{
    const box=document.getElementById('batchProjList'); box.innerHTML='';
    (ps||[]).forEach(p=>{
      const item=document.createElement('label'); item.className='batch-check-item'; item.dataset.search=p.project_no.toLowerCase();
      item.innerHTML='<input type="checkbox" value="'+p.id+'"> <span>'+esc(p.project_no)+'</span>';
      item.querySelector('input').onchange=()=>{ updateBatchHint('batchProjList','batchProjHint','个项目'); renderBatchPermPanel(); };
      box.appendChild(item);
    });
    updateBatchHint('batchProjList','batchProjHint','个项目');
    filterBatchItems('batchProjList','batchProjFilter');
  });
  const p2 = api('/api/users/all').then(us=>{
