    const box=document.getElementById('batchUserList'); box.innerHTML='';
    const posMap={}; // name -> count
    (us||[]).forEach(u=>{
      if(u.role==='superAdmin' || u.role==='projectAdmin') return; // 管理员无需批量授权
      const posName=(u.position_name||u.dept||'未设置职位').trim();
      const item=document.createElement('label');
      item.className='batch-check-item';
      item.dataset.search=((u.name||'')+' '+(u.uid||'')+' '+posName).toLowerCase();
      item.dataset.position=posName.toLowerCase();
      item.dataset.positionRaw=posName;
      item.innerHTML='<input type="checkbox" value="'+esc(u.uid)+'"> <span>'+esc(u.name)+' ('+esc(u.uid)+') <span style="color:var(--t3);font-size:11px">['+esc(posName)+']</span></span>';
      item.querySelector('input').onchange=()=>{
        updateBatchHint('batchUserList','batchUserHint','个用户');
      };
      box.appendChild(item);
      posMap[posName]=(posMap[posName]||0)+1;
    });
    buildBatchPosBar(posMap);
    updateBatchHint('batchUserList','batchUserHint','个用户');
    filterBatchItems('batchUserList','batchUserFilter');
  });
  return Promise.all([p1,p2]);
}
function buildBatchPosBar(posMap){
  const bar=document.getElementById('batchUserPosBar');
  const names=Object.keys(posMap).sort();
  if(!names.length){ bar.style.display='none'; return; }
  bar.style.display='flex';
  let h='<span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3)">按职位：</span>'
      +'<button class="btn btn-xs batch-pos-btn active" data-pos="" onclick="filterBatchByPos(this)\">全部</button>';
  names.forEach(name=>{
    h+='<button class="btn btn-xs batch-pos-btn" data-pos="'+escAttr(name.toLowerCase())+'" onclick="filterBatchByPos(this)">'+esc(name)+' ('+posMap[name]+')</button>';
  });
  bar.innerHTML=h;
}
function filterBatchByPos(btn){
  const pos=(btn.dataset.pos||'').toLowerCase();
  document.querySelectorAll('#batchUserPosBar .batch-pos-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active', b===btn));
  // 切标签前先保存当前职位下手动改过的覆盖项，再加载目标职位自己的覆盖集。
  // 这样「PD 标签下改 Fixture 只读」只作用于 PD 用户，切到 PQE 时矩阵是 PQE 自己的设置。
  _saveCurPosPerms();
  BATCH_CUR_POS=pos;
  const box=document.getElementById('batchUserList');
  const q=(document.getElementById('batchUserFilter').value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  Array.from(box.children).forEach(item=>{
    const text=item.dataset.search||'';
    const matchText= !q || text.indexOf(q)!==-1;
    const matchPos= !pos || item.dataset.position===pos;
    item.classList.toggle('hidden', !(matchText && matchPos));
  });
  _loadPosPerms(pos);
}
function filterBatchItems(listId, inputId){
  const box=document.getElementById(listId), inp=document.getElementById(inputId);
  const q=(inp.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  if(listId==='batchUserList'){
    const activeBtn=document.querySelector('#batchUserPosBar .batch-pos-btn.active');
    const pos=activeBtn?activeBtn.dataset.pos:'';
    Array.from(box.children).forEach(item=>{
      const text=item.dataset.search||'';
      const matchText= !q || text.indexOf(q)!==-1;
      const matchPos= !pos || item.dataset.position===pos;
      item.classList.toggle('hidden', !(matchText && matchPos));
    });
  }else{
    Array.from(box.children).forEach(item=>{
      const text=item.dataset.search||'';
      item.classList.toggle('hidden', q && text.indexOf(q)===-1);
    });
  }
}
function batchSelectAllVisibleUsers(select){
  const box=document.getElementById('batchUserList');
  Array.from(box.children).forEach(item=>{
    if(item.classList.contains('hidden')) return;
    item.querySelector('input').checked=select;
  });
  updateBatchHint('batchUserList','batchUserHint','个用户');
}
function updateBatchHint(listId, hintId, unit){
  const n=document.querySelectorAll('#'+listId+' input:checked').length;
  document.getElementById(hintId).textContent='已选择 '+n+' '+unit;
}
function saveBatch(){
  const pids=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#batchProjList input:checked')).map(cb=>cb.value);
  const uids=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#batchUserList input:checked')).map(cb=>cb.value);
  const role=document.getElementById('batchRole').value;
  if(!pids.length||!uids.length){ toast('请选择项目和用户',true); return; }
  // 多职位混勾时，按每位用户自身职位分别赋权限：PD→edit，PQE/生产模修→read，其他默认 read
  const userPerms={}, userPos={};
  document.querySelectorAll('#batchUserList input:checked').forEach(cb=>{
    const item=cb.closest('.batch-check-item');
    if(!item) return;
    const raw=item.dataset.positionRaw||'';
    userPerms[cb.value]=_initPermForPos(raw);
    userPos[cb.value]=_posKeyOf(raw);
  });
  // 矩阵覆盖按职位分组提交：每位用户只套用「自己职位」那一套手动覆盖（无则用「全部」标签下的通用覆盖）
  _saveCurPosPerms();
  const posEntries={};
  Object.keys(BATCH_POS_PERM).forEach(pk=>{
    const m=BATCH_POS_PERM[pk]; const arr=[];
    Object.keys(m).forEach(k=>{
      const v=m[k]; if(!v) return;
      const i=k.indexOf('|');
      const phase=k.slice(0,i), dt=k.slice(i+1);
      arr.push({phase: phase||null, docType: dt||null, permission:v});
    });
    if(arr.length) posEntries[pk]=arr;
  });
  _doSaveBatch(pids,uids,role,[],false,userPerms,userPos,posEntries);
}
function _doSaveBatch(pids,uids,role,entries,overwrite,userPerms,userPos,posEntries){
  const body={projectIds:pids,userIds:uids,role,entries,overwrite};
  if(userPerms && Object.keys(userPerms).length) body.userPerms=userPerms;
  if(userPos && Object.keys(userPos).length) body.userPos=userPos;
  if(posEntries && Object.keys(posEntries).length) body.posEntries=posEntries;
  api('/api/permissions/batch',{method:'POST',body}).then(r=>{
    if(r.existing && r.existing.length && !overwrite){
      const rows=r.existing.map(e=>'<tr><td>'+esc(e.projectNo)+'</td><td>'+esc(e.userName)+'</td><td>'+esc(e.userId)+'</td></tr>').join('');
      const body='<div class="confirm-warn"><span class="icon">&#9888;</span><div>这些项目与用户的授权关系已经存在，确认覆盖后将以当前设置替换原有权限。</div></div>'+
                 '<div class="confirm-count">共 '+r.existing.length+' 条已存在授权</div>'+
                 '<div class="confirm-list"><table class="confirm-table"><thead><tr><th>项目编号</th><th>用户姓名</th><th>工号</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows+'</tbody></table></div>';
      askConfirm('覆盖已有授权', body, ()=>_doSaveBatch(pids,uids,role,entries,true,userPerms), '确认覆盖', 'btn-danger', 'wide');
      return;
    }
    if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
    toast('批量授权成功，共'+r.granted+'组');
    closeModal('batchMask');
    renderProjPerms();
  });
}

async function renderBatchPermPanel(){
  const panel=document.getElementById('batchPermPanel');
  if(!panel) return;
  const role=document.getElementById('batchRole').value;
  if(role==='projectAdmin'){
    panel.innerHTML='<div class="batch-perm-admin-hint">项目管理员默认拥有全部阶段/文档类型的可编辑权限，无需逐项设置。</div>';
    return;
  }
  const checked=document.querySelectorAll('#batchProjList input:checked');
  const pids=Array.from(checked).map(cb=>cb.value);
  if(!pids.length){
    panel.innerHTML='<div class="batch-perm-loading">请选择项目以加载其实际阶段</div>';
    return;
  }
  panel.innerHTML='<div class="batch-perm-loading">正在加载项目阶段…</div>';
  let tpl={};
  if(pids.length===1){
    tpl=await api('/api/projects/'+pids[0]+'/phase-tree');
  }else{
    // 多项目取阶段并集
    const trees=await Promise.all(pids.map(pid=>api('/api/projects/'+pid+'/phase-tree')));
    trees.forEach(tree=>{
      Object.entries(tree||{}).forEach(([ph,dts])=>{
        if(!tpl[ph]) tpl[ph]=[];
        (dts||[]).forEach(dt=>{ if(!tpl[ph].includes(dt)) tpl[ph].push(dt); });
      });
    });
  }
  _renderBatchPermTree(tpl||{});
}
function _renderBatchPermTree(tpl){
  const panel=document.getElementById('batchPermPanel');
  const role=document.getElementById('batchRole').value;
  if(role==='projectAdmin'){
    panel.innerHTML='<div class="batch-perm-admin-hint">项目管理员默认拥有全部阶段/文档类型的可编辑权限，无需逐项设置。</div>';
    return;
  }
  let h='<div class="batch-perm-btns">'
    +'<button class="btn btn-success btn-xs" onclick="setBatchPermAll(\'edit\')">全部可编辑</button>'
    +'<button class="btn btn-primary btn-xs" onclick="setBatchPermAll(\'read\')">全部只读</button>'
    +'<button class="btn btn-danger btn-xs" onclick="setBatchPermAll(\'none\')">全部无权限</button>'
    +'</div>';
  h+='<div class="batch-perm-row root"><span class="batch-perm-label"><span class="perm-icon">📁</span>项目根目录</span>'
    +'<select class="batch-perm-sel" data-phase="" data-dt="" onchange="markBatchTouched(this);onBatchPermRootChange()">'+batchPermOpts('')+'</select></div>';
  const phases=Object.keys(tpl);
  if(!phases.length){
    h+='<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3);padding:10px 0">暂无阶段/文档类型模板</div>';
  }else{
    phases.forEach(ph=>{
      const dts=tpl[ph]||[];
      h+='<div class="batch-perm-row phase" data-phase="'+escAttr(ph)+'">'
        +'<span class="batch-perm-toggle" onclick="toggleBatchPermPhase(\''+escAttr(ph)+'\',this)">▶</span>'
        +'<span class="batch-perm-label"><span class="perm-icon">📂</span>'+esc(ph)+'</span>'
        +'<select class="batch-perm-sel" data-phase="'+escAttr(ph)+'" data-dt="" onchange="markBatchTouched(this);onBatchPermPhaseChange(\''+escAttr(ph)+'\')">'+batchPermOpts('')+'</select></div>';
      (dts||[]).forEach(dt=>{
        h+='<div class="batch-perm-row doctype batch-perm-child hidden" data-phase="'+escAttr(ph)+'" data-dt="'+escAttr(dt)+'"><span class="batch-perm-label"><span class="perm-icon">📄</span>'+esc(dt)+'</span>'
          +'<select class="batch-perm-sel" data-phase="'+escAttr(ph)+'" data-dt="'+escAttr(dt)+'" onchange="markBatchTouched(this)">'+batchPermOpts('')+'</select></div>';
      });
    });
  }
  panel.innerHTML=h;
  // 矩阵是覆盖语义：先按每位用户职位（PD→可编辑、PQE/生产模修→只读、其他默认只读）填默认值，
  // 再用这里被改过的 select 作为覆盖。空白=继承，不参与覆盖。
  const tip=document.createElement('div');
  tip.className='batch-perm-tip';
  tip.style.cssText='font-size:11px;color:var(--t3);margin-top:10px;padding-top:8px;border-top:1px dashed var(--border)';
  tip.innerHTML='💡 授权前矩阵已按当前筛选职位自动预览（PD→可编辑、PQE/生产模修→只读），<strong>只有你手动改过的项</strong>才会作为覆盖提交；切到「全部」不预填，每位用户按自身职位授权。';
  panel.appendChild(tip);
  // 当前筛选职位的矩阵预览默认值（按切换的职位自动填可编辑/只读，仅作视觉提示）
  const activeBtn=document.querySelector('#batchUserPosBar .batch-pos-btn.active');
  applyBatchPermFilterPreset(activeBtn?(activeBtn.dataset.pos||''):'');
}
function applyBatchPermFilterPreset(pos){
  // 职位筛选的矩阵预填（视觉辅助）：
  //  - 筛选到具体职位（PD/PQE/生产模修）→ 把未手动改的项预填该职位默认值
  //  - 「全部」→ 不预填具体值，矩阵归零为「继承」，此时授权完全按每位用户自身职位
  // 预填只设 value 不触发 onchange、不标 dirty，不会作为覆盖项提交；
  // 用户已手动改过（dirty）的项保持原值，不被预填覆盖。
  pos=(pos||'').toLowerCase();
  const panel=document.getElementById('batchPermPanel');
  if(!panel) return;
  let val=null;
  if(pos==='pd') val='edit';
  else if(pos==='pqe' || pos.includes('pqe') || pos.includes('生产模修') || pos.includes('模修')) val='read';
  const rootSel=document.querySelector('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-row.root .batch-perm-sel');
  if(rootSel && rootSel.dataset.dirty!=='1') rootSel.value = (val===null?'':val);
  if(val===null){
    // 「全部」：所有未手动改的下拉清成「继承」，避免视觉残留误导
    document.querySelectorAll('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-sel').forEach(sel=>{
      if(sel.dataset.dirty!=='1') sel.value='';
    });
    // 手动改过（dirty）的阶段，其未 dirty 子项跟随阶段值显示，保持视觉级联一致
    document.querySelectorAll('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-row.phase .batch-perm-sel').forEach(sel=>{
      if(sel.dataset.dirty==='1'&&sel.dataset.phase) onBatchPermPhaseChange(sel.dataset.phase);
    });
    return;
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-row.phase').forEach(row=>{
    const sel=row.querySelector('.batch-perm-sel');
    if(sel && sel.dataset.dirty!=='1') sel.value = val;
    const ph=row.dataset.phase;
    if(ph) onBatchPermPhaseChange(ph);
  });
}
function _initPermForPos(rawPos){
  // 按用户职位名推断批量授权的初始权限：PD→可编辑，PQE/生产模修→只读，其他默认只读
  const p=(rawPos||'').trim();
  if(!p) return 'read';
  const lp=p.toLowerCase();
  if(lp==='pd') return 'edit';
  if(lp==='pqe' || p.includes('生产模修') || p.includes('模修')) return 'read';
  return 'read';
}
function _posKeyOf(rawPos){ return String(rawPos||'').trim().toLowerCase(); }
function _batchPermKey(sel){
  // 覆盖集 key：根='|'，阶段='阶段名|'，子文件夹='阶段名|子文件夹'
  return (sel.dataset.phase||'')+'|'+(sel.dataset.dt||'');
}
function _saveCurPosPerms(){
  // 把当前面板里手动改过(dirty)的项保存进当前职位的覆盖集
  const panel=document.getElementById('batchPermPanel');
  if(!panel) return;
  const m={};
  panel.querySelectorAll('.batch-perm-sel').forEach(sel=>{
    if(sel.dataset.dirty!=='1') return;
    m[_batchPermKey(sel)]=sel.value;
  });
  if(Object.keys(m).length) BATCH_POS_PERM[BATCH_CUR_POS]=m;
  else delete BATCH_POS_PERM[BATCH_CUR_POS];
}
function _loadPosPerms(posKey){
  // 加载某职位的覆盖集；该职位没手改过则回到「按职位预填」的视觉预览
  const panel=document.getElementById('batchPermPanel');
  if(!panel) return;
  panel.querySelectorAll('.batch-perm-sel').forEach(sel=>{ delete sel.dataset.dirty; });
  const m=BATCH_POS_PERM[posKey];
  if(m && Object.keys(m).length){
    panel.querySelectorAll('.batch-perm-sel').forEach(sel=>{
      const k=_batchPermKey(sel);
      if(k in m){ sel.value=m[k]; sel.dataset.dirty='1'; }
    });
    return;
  }
  applyBatchPermFilterPreset(posKey);
}
function markBatchTouched(sel){
  // 用户手动改过该 select —— 该值才会作为覆盖项提交；
  // 职位切换时的自动预填（applyBatchPermFilterPreset）只设 value 不触发 onchange，不会标记 dirty
  if(sel) sel.dataset.dirty='1';
}
function batchPermOpts(v){
  return [['','继承'],['edit','可编辑'],['read','只读'],['none','无权限']].map(o=>'<option value="'+o[0]+'" '+(o[0]===v?'selected':'')+'>'+o[1]+'</option>').join('');
}
function setBatchPermAll(val){
  // 「全部可编辑/只读/无权限」= 对根和所有阶段批量设值并标 dirty；
  // 子项跟随阶段值但**不标 dirty**，确保后续单独修改某个阶段时子项能继续级联覆盖。
  const rootSel=document.querySelector('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-row.root .batch-perm-sel');
  if(rootSel){ rootSel.value=val; rootSel.dataset.dirty='1'; }
  document.querySelectorAll('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-row.phase').forEach(row=>{
    const sel=row.querySelector('.batch-perm-sel');
    if(sel){ sel.value=val; sel.dataset.dirty='1'; }
    const ph=row.dataset.phase;
    if(ph) onBatchPermPhaseChange(ph);
  });
}
function onBatchPermRootChange(){
  const rootSel=document.querySelector('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-row.root .batch-perm-sel');
  if(!rootSel||!rootSel.value) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-row.phase .batch-perm-sel').forEach(sel=>{
    if(sel.dataset.dirty!=='1'){ sel.value=rootSel.value; onBatchPermPhaseChange(sel.dataset.phase); }
  });
}
function onBatchPermPhaseChange(ph){
  const phaseSel=document.querySelector('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-row.phase[data-phase="'+escAttr(ph)+'"] .batch-perm-sel');
  if(!phaseSel) return;
  const val=phaseSel.value;
  // 未被用户手动改过的子文件夹视觉跟随阶段值（含改回「继承」时清回继承）；
  // 跟随项不标 dirty，提交时仅阶段级覆盖，由后端级联落库
  document.querySelectorAll('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-row.doctype[data-phase="'+escAttr(ph)+'"] .batch-perm-sel').forEach(sel=>{ if(sel.dataset.dirty!=='1') sel.value=val; });
}
function toggleBatchPermPhase(ph, el){
  const expanded = el.textContent === '▼';
  el.textContent = expanded ? '▶' : '▼';
  document.querySelectorAll('#batchPermPanel .batch-perm-row.doctype[data-phase="'+escAttr(ph)+'"]').forEach(row=>{
    row.classList.toggle('hidden', expanded);
  });
}
/* ==================== 零件 3D 图预览 ==================== */
/* three.js 按需加载：只有打开 3D 弹窗时才拉取，不影响主界面首屏 */
let M3D = {loaded:false, loading:false, renderer:null, scene:null, camera:null,
           controls:null, model:null, grid:null, wire:false, curId:null};

function m3dEnsureThree(){
  return new Promise((resolve, reject)=>{
    if(M3D.loaded) return resolve(true);
    if(M3D.loading) { // 已在加载，轮询等待
      let t=0; const iv=setInterval(()=>{
        if(M3D.loaded){ clearInterval(iv); resolve(true); }
        else if((t+=200)>20000){ clearInterval(iv); reject(new Error('three.js 加载超时')); }
      },200);
      return;
    }
    M3D.loading=true;
    const files=['/static/vendor/three/three.min.js',
                 '/static/vendor/three/GLTFLoader.js',
                 '/static/vendor/three/STLLoader.js',
                 '/static/vendor/three/OrbitControls.js'];
    (function next(i){
      if(i>=files.length){ M3D.loaded=true; M3D.loading=false; resolve(true); return; }
      const s=document.createElement('script');
      s.src=files[i];
      s.onload=()=>next(i+1);
      s.onerror=()=>{ M3D.loading=false; reject(new Error('无法加载 '+files[i])); };
      document.head.appendChild(s);
    })(0);
  });
}

function openModel3DModal(){
  if(!SEL.partId){ toast('请先选中一个零件',true); return; }
  // 每次打开都按当前零件重置：清除上一零件残留的模型与选中态
  M3D.curId=null;
  m3dClearModel();
  openModal('m3dMask');
  m3dLoadList();
  m3dLoadCliStatus();
  m3dEnsureThree().then(()=>{ setTimeout(m3dInitViewer, 60); })
    .catch(e=>{ document.getElementById('m3dEmpty').textContent='3D 组件加载失败：'+e.message; });
}

function m3dLoadCliStatus(){
  api('/api/models3d/cli-status').then(r=>{
    const el=document.getElementById('m3dCli');
    if(r.available){
      el.className='m3d-cli ok';
      el.textContent='✓ 转换工具已就绪'+(r.version?(' (OCCT '+esc(r.version)+')'):'');
    } else {
      el.className='m3d-cli err';
      el.title='将 npi_occt_cli.exe 放入服务运行目录后刷新即可预览';
      el.textContent='✗ 未安装转换工具，仅可上传/下载';
    }
  });
}

function m3dLoadList(){
  const box=document.getElementById('m3dList');
  box.innerHTML='<div class="m3d-loading">加载中…</div>';
  api('/api/models3d?project_id='+SEL.projId+'&part_id='+SEL.partId).then(list=>{
    if(list.error){ box.innerHTML='<div class="m3d-none">'+esc(list.error)+'</div>'; return; }
    if(!list.length){ box.innerHTML='<div class="m3d-none">该零件暂无 3D 模型<br>点击上方按钮上传 STEP/IGES/STL</div>'; return; }
    box.innerHTML='';
    list.forEach(m=>{
      const d=document.createElement('div');
      d.className='m3d-item'+(m.id===M3D.curId?' active':'');
      d.innerHTML='<span class="m3d-tag '+m.status+'">'
          +(m.status==='ready'?'可预览':m.status==='failed'?'失败':'转换中')+'</span>'
        +'<span class="nm" title="'+esc(m.original_name)+'">'+esc(m.original_name)+'</span>'
        +'<span class="sub">'+(m.size?((m.size/1024).toFixed(0)+' KB'):'')+'</span>'
        +'<button class="btn btn-xs" onclick="event.stopPropagation();m3dDownload('+m.id+')">下载</button>'
        +'<button class="btn btn-xs btn-danger" onclick="event.stopPropagation();m3dDelete('+m.id+')">删除</button>';
      d.onclick=()=>{ M3D.curId=m.id; m3dLoadList(); m3dShow(m); };
      box.appendChild(d);
    });
  });
}

function m3dUpload(input){
  const f=input.files && input.files[0];
  input.value='';
  if(!f) return;
  const fd=new FormData();
  fd.append('file', f);
  fd.append('project_id', SEL.projId);
  fd.append('part_id', SEL.partId);
  fd.append('unit', 'mm');
  toast('正在上传并转换，请稍候…');
  fetch('/api/models3d/upload',{method:'POST',body:fd,credentials:'same-origin'})
    .then(r=>r.json())
    .then(r=>{
      if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
      if(r.status==='ready'){ toast('上传并转换成功'); m3dLoadList(); }
      else { toast('已保存，但转换失败：'+(r.error||'未知原因'), true); m3dLoadList(); }
    })
    .catch(e=>toast('上传失败：'+e.message,true));
}

function m3dDownload(id){ window.location='/api/models3d/'+id+'/source'; }
function m3dDelete(id){
  askConfirm('删除 3D 模型','确认删除该模型？原始文件与转换结果都会移除。',()=>{
    api('/api/models3d/'+id,{method:'DELETE'}).then(r=>{
      if(r.error) toast(r.error,true);
      else { toast('已删除'); if(M3D.curId===id){ M3D.curId=null; m3dClearModel(); } m3dLoadList(); }
    });
  },'删除','btn-danger');
}

/* ---------- three.js 场景 ---------- */
function m3dInitViewer(){
  const box=document.getElementById('m3dViewer');
  if(M3D.renderer) return;
  if(typeof THREE==='undefined'){ document.getElementById('m3dEmpty').textContent='three.js 未加载'; return; }
  const w=box.clientWidth||800, h=box.clientHeight||420;
  M3D.renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({antialias:true, alpha:true});
  // glTF 的 baseColorFactor 是线性值，渲染器需做 sRGB 输出转换，否则模型会明显发暗
  if(THREE.sRGBEncoding!=null) M3D.renderer.outputEncoding=THREE.sRGBEncoding;
  M3D.renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio||1);
  M3D.renderer.setSize(w,h);
  box.appendChild(M3D.renderer.domElement);

  M3D.scene=new THREE.Scene();
  M3D.camera=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, w/h, 0.01, 100000);
  M3D.camera.position.set(200,200,200);

  M3D.scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff,0.75));
  const d1=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.7); d1.position.set(1,1,1); M3D.scene.add(d1);
  const d2=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.35); d2.position.set(-1,-0.6,-1); M3D.scene.add(d2);

  M3D.grid=new THREE.GridHelper(100,20,0xc8cdd6,0xe6e9ef);
  M3D.scene.add(M3D.grid);

  if(THREE.OrbitControls){
    M3D.controls=new THREE.OrbitControls(M3D.camera, M3D.renderer.domElement);
    M3D.controls.enableDamping=true;
    M3D.controls.dampingFactor=0.12;
  }
  (function loop(){ requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    if(M3D.controls) M3D.controls.update();
    if(M3D.renderer) M3D.renderer.render(M3D.scene, M3D.camera);
  })();
  window.addEventListener('resize', m3dResize, false);
  // 容器尺寸变化（弹窗打开/窗口缩放）时同步 canvas 尺寸
  if(window.ResizeObserver && !M3D._ro){
    M3D._ro=new ResizeObserver(()=>m3dResize());
    M3D._ro.observe(box);
  }
}

function m3dResize(){
  const box=document.getElementById('m3dViewer');
  if(!box||!M3D.renderer) return;
  const w=box.clientWidth||800,h=box.clientHeight||420;
  M3D.camera.aspect=w/h; M3D.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  M3D.renderer.setSize(w,h);
}

function m3dClearModel(){
  if(M3D.model){ M3D.scene.remove(M3D.model); M3D.model=null; }
  document.getElementById('m3dEmpty').style.display='';
  document.getElementById('m3dEmpty').textContent='从右侧选择一个模型开始预览';
  document.getElementById('m3dMeta').innerHTML='';
  document.getElementById('m3dStat').textContent='';
}

function m3dShow(m){
  m3dEnsureThree().then(()=>{
    m3dInitViewer();
    if(!M3D.renderer) return;
    if(m.status!=='ready'){
      m3dClearModel();
      document.getElementById('m3dEmpty').textContent=
        '该模型无可预览的转换结果'+(m.error_msg?('：'+m.error_msg):'');
      return;
    }
    document.getElementById('m3dEmpty').textContent='正在加载模型…';
    document.getElementById('m3dEmpty').style.display='';
    new THREE.GLTFLoader().load('/api/models3d/'+m.id+'/glb', (gltf)=>{
      m3dClearModel();
      document.getElementById('m3dEmpty').style.display='none';
      M3D.model=gltf.scene;
      npiNormalizeMaterials(M3D.model);
      M3D.scene.add(M3D.model);
      m3dResize();          // 先校正画布尺寸（弹窗刚打开时可能还没就绪）
      m3dFitView(M3D.model);
      m3dRenderMeta(m);
    }, undefined, (err)=>{
      document.getElementById('m3dEmpty').textContent='模型加载失败：'+(err&&err.message||'未知错误');
    });
  }).catch(e=>toast(e.message,true));
}

function m3dFitView(obj){
  const box=new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(obj);
  const size=box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3());
  const center=box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  const maxDim=Math.max(size.x,size.y,size.z)||1;
  const dist=maxDim*2.2;
  M3D.camera.near=maxDim/1000;
  M3D.camera.far=maxDim*100;
  M3D.camera.position.set(center.x+dist, center.y+dist*0.8, center.z+dist);
  M3D.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  if(M3D.controls){ M3D.controls.target.copy(center); M3D.controls.update(); }
  if(M3D.grid){
    M3D.grid.scale.setScalar(maxDim/100);
    M3D.grid.position.set(center.x, box.min.y, center.z);
  }
  M3D._home={p:M3D.camera.position.clone(), t:center.clone()};
}

function m3dResetView(){
  if(!M3D._home||!M3D.camera) return;
  M3D.camera.position.copy(M3D._home.p);
  if(M3D.controls){ M3D.controls.target.copy(M3D._home.t); M3D.controls.update(); }
}
function m3dToggleWire(){
  if(!M3D.model) return;
  M3D.wire=!M3D.wire;
  M3D.model.traverse(o=>{ if(o.isMesh) o.material.wireframe=M3D.wire; });
}
function m3dToggleGrid(){
  if(M3D.grid) M3D.grid.visible=!M3D.grid.visible;
}

function m3dRenderMeta(m){
  const mt=m.meta||{};
  const n=v=>(v==null?'-':(typeof v==='number'?v.toFixed(2):v));
  const bb=mt.bbox||{};
  const sz=bb.size||[0,0,0];
  document.getElementById('m3dMeta').innerHTML=
      '<b>'+esc(m.original_name)+'</b> · '+esc(m.src_format||'')
    + '<br>体积：'+n(mt.volume)+' mm³　　面积：'+n(mt.area)+' mm²'
    + '<br>尺寸：'+n(sz[0])+' × '+n(sz[1])+' × '+n(sz[2])+' mm'
    + '<br>实体/面/三角面：'+(mt.counts?mt.counts.solids:'-')+' / '
      +(mt.counts?mt.counts.faces:'-')+' / '+(mt.counts?mt.counts.triangles:'-')
    + '<br>上传人：'+esc(m.uploader_name||m.uploader_id||'')+' · '+esc((m.uploaded_at||'').slice(0,16));
  document.getElementById('m3dStat').textContent =
    (mt.counts? (mt.counts.triangles+' 三角面') : '');
}

/* ---------- 用户管理 ---------- */
async function renderUsers(){
  const r=await api('/api/users');
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
  CURRENT.users = r;
  _renderUsersFull();
}
function _renderUsersFull(){
  const body=document.getElementById('adminBody');
  body.innerHTML='<div class="admin-toolbar">'
    +'<div class="admin-section-title">👥 用户列表</div>'
    +'<input type="text" id="userFilterUid" placeholder="筛选工号" class="user-filter-input" oninput="_renderUsersFiltered()">'
    +'<input type="text" id="userFilterName" placeholder="筛选姓名" class="user-filter-input" oninput="_renderUsersFiltered()">'
    +'<div class="spacer"></div>'
    +'<button class="btn" onclick="exportUsers()">📥 导出 Excel</button>'
    +'<button class="btn" onclick="openImportUsers()">📤 导入 Excel</button>'
    +'<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openUserMask()">+ 新增用户</button></div>'
    +'<table class="admin-tbl"><thead><tr><th>工号</th><th>姓名</th><th>部门</th><th>职位</th><th>角色</th><th>状态</th><th style="width:280px">操作</th></tr></thead><tbody id="userTableBody"></tbody></table>';
  _renderUsersFiltered();
}
function _renderUsersFiltered(){
  const fUid=(document.getElementById('userFilterUid')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const fName=(document.getElementById('userFilterName')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  let list = CURRENT.users || [];
  if(fUid) list = list.filter(u=>(u.uid||'').toLowerCase().includes(fUid));
  if(fName) list = list.filter(u=>(u.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(fName));
  let html='';
    list.forEach(u=>{
    const roleMap={superAdmin:['tag red','系统管理员'],projectAdmin:['tag orange','项目管理员'],pd:['tag blue','一般用户']};
    const rp=roleMap[u.role]||['tag blue','一般用户'];
    html+='<tr><td>'+esc(u.uid)+'</td><td>'+esc(u.name)+'</td><td>'+esc(u.dept||'')+'</td>'
      +'<td>'+esc(u.position_name||'-')+'</td>'
      +'<td><span class="'+rp[0]+'">'+rp[1]+'</span></td>'
      +'<td>'+(u.frozen?'<span class="tag gray">已冻结</span>':'<span class="tag green">正常</span>')+'</td>'
      +'<td><div class="actions" style="flex-wrap:wrap">'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="editUser(\''+u.uid+'\')">编辑</button>'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger-hover" onclick="deleteUser(\''+u.uid+'\')">删除</button>'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="resetPw(\''+u.uid+'\')">重置密码</button>'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm '+(u.frozen?'btn-success':'')+'" onclick="toggleFreeze(\''+u.uid+'\','+(u.frozen?0:1)+')">'+(u.frozen?'解冻':'冻结')+'</button>'
      +'</div></td></tr>';
  });
  if(!list.length){
    html+='<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center;color:var(--t3);padding:24px">无匹配用户</td></tr>';
  }
  document.getElementById('userTableBody').innerHTML=html;
}
function exportUsers(){
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href='/api/users/export?format=xlsx';
  a.download='NPI用户列表.xlsx';
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
}
function downloadUserTemplate(){
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href='/api/users/import/template';
  a.download='NPI用户导入模板.xlsx';
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
}
function _resetImportDropzone(){
  const dz=document.getElementById('importDropzone');
  if(!dz) return;
  dz.querySelector('.import-dropzone-icon').textContent='📂';
  dz.querySelector('.import-dropzone-text').textContent='点击选择 Excel 文件';
  dz.querySelector('.import-dropzone-hint').textContent='支持 .xlsx / .xls 格式，文件大小建议不超过 5MB';
  dz.classList.remove('has-file');
}
function _setImportDropzone(filename){
  const dz=document.getElementById('importDropzone');
  if(!dz) return;
  dz.querySelector('.import-dropzone-icon').textContent='📄';
  dz.querySelector('.import-dropzone-text').textContent=esc(filename);
  dz.querySelector('.import-dropzone-hint').textContent='已选择文件，下方为导入预览';
  dz.classList.add('has-file');
}
function _onImportDrop(e){
  const dz=document.getElementById('importDropzone');
  if(dz) dz.classList.remove('dragover');
  const files=e.dataTransfer && e.dataTransfer.files;
  if(!files || !files.length) return;
  const input=document.getElementById('importUserFile');
  const dt=new DataTransfer();
  for(let i=0;i<files.length;i++) dt.items.add(files[i]);
  input.files=dt.files;
  onImportUserFileChange();
}
function openImportUsers(){
  document.getElementById('importUserFile').value='';
  document.getElementById('importUserPreview').innerHTML='<div class="import-preview-empty">请先上传 Excel 文件，导入前可预览并检查错误</div>';
  document.getElementById('importUserConfirm').style.display='none';
  _resetImportDropzone();
  openModal('importUserMask');
}
function _buildImportPreviewTable(data){
  const roleColor={新增:'tag green',更新:'tag orange',跳过:'tag gray',错误:'tag red'};
  let html='<div style="margin-bottom:10px;font-size:13px;color:var(--t2)">'
    +'共 <b>'+data.stats.total+'</b> 行：新增 <b>'+data.stats.create+'</b>，更新 <b>'+data.stats.update+'</b>，跳过 <b>'+data.stats.skip+'</b>，错误 <b>'+data.stats.error+'</b></div>';
  html+='<div style="max-height:360px;overflow:auto;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--radius-sm)"><table class="admin-tbl" style="margin:0"><thead><tr><th>行号</th><th>动作</th><th>工号</th><th>姓名</th><th>部门</th><th>职位</th><th>角色</th><th>状态</th><th>说明</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  data.rows.forEach(r=>{
    const c=roleColor[r.action]||'tag';
    html+='<tr><td>'+r.row+'</td><td><span class="'+c+'">'+esc(r.action)+'</span></td>'
      +'<td>'+esc(r.uid)+'</td><td>'+esc(r.name)+'</td><td>'+esc(r.dept)+'</td>'
      +'<td>'+esc(r.position_raw||'')+'</td>'
      +'<td>'+esc(r.role_text||r.role_raw)+'</td><td>'+esc(r.status_text||r.status_raw)+'</td>'
      +'<td style="color:'+(r.action==='错误'?'var(--danger)':'var(--t3)')+'">'+esc(r.action_detail||'')+'</td></tr>';
  });
  html+='</tbody></table></div>';
  if(data.errors && data.errors.length){
    html+='<div style="margin-top:10px;color:var(--danger);font-size:13px"><b>请修正以下错误后再导入：</b><ul style="margin:6px 0 0 18px">'+data.errors.map(e=>'<li>'+esc(e)+'</li>').join('')+'</ul></div>';
  }
  return html;
}
async function onImportUserFileChange(){
  const input=document.getElementById('importUserFile');
  const file=input.files[0];
  if(!file) return;
  _setImportDropzone(file.name);
  const fd=new FormData();
  fd.append('file',file);
  const preview=document.getElementById('importUserPreview');
  preview.innerHTML='<div class="loading-box"><div class="spinner"></div>正在解析...</div>';
  const r=await fetch('/api/users/import?dry_run=1',{method:'POST',body:fd,credentials:'same-origin'}).then(x=>x.json());
  if(r.error){ preview.innerHTML='<div style="color:var(--danger);padding:12px">'+esc(r.error)+'</div>'; return; }
  CURRENT.importUserPreview = r;
  preview.innerHTML=_buildImportPreviewTable(r);
  const btn=document.getElementById('importUserConfirm');
  btn.style.display=(r.stats.error===0 && (r.stats.create+r.stats.update)>0)?'inline-block':'none';
}
async function confirmImportUsers(){
  const data=CURRENT.importUserPreview;
  if(!data || data.stats.error>0 || (data.stats.create+data.stats.update)===0) return;
  const input=document.getElementById('importUserFile');
  const file=input.files[0];
  if(!file){ toast('请先选择文件',true); return; }
  const fd=new FormData();
  fd.append('file',file);
  const btn=document.getElementById('importUserConfirm');
  btn.disabled=true; btn.textContent='导入中…';
  const r=await fetch('/api/users/import?dry_run=0',{method:'POST',body:fd,credentials:'same-origin'}).then(x=>x.json());
  btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent='确认导入';
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
  closeModal('importUserMask');
  toast('导入完成：新增 '+r.created+' 人，更新 '+r.updated+' 人');
  renderUsers();
}
async function openUserMask(id,name,dept,role,position_id){
  await _loadPositions();
  const posSel=document.getElementById('uPosition');
  posSel.innerHTML='<option value="">未设置</option>'+(CURRENT.positions||[]).map(p=>'<option value="'+p.id+'">'+esc(p.name)+'</option>').join('');
  document.getElementById('uEditId').value=id||'';
  document.getElementById('userMaskTitle').textContent=id?'编辑用户':'新增用户';
  const uidEl=document.getElementById('uUid');
  uidEl.value=id||'';
  uidEl.readOnly=!!id;
  document.getElementById('uName').value=name||'';
  document.getElementById('uDept').value=dept||'';
  document.getElementById('uRole').value=role||'pd';
  posSel.value=position_id||'';
  document.getElementById('uPw').value='';
  openModal('userMask');
}
async function editUser(uid){
  const u=(CURRENT.users||[]).find(x=>x.uid===uid);
  if(!u){ renderUsers(); return; }
  await openUserMask(u.uid,u.name,u.dept,u.role,u.position_id);
}
function saveUser(){
  const id=document.getElementById('uEditId').value;
  const uid=document.getElementById('uUid').value.trim();
  const name=document.getElementById('uName').value.trim();
  const dept=document.getElementById('uDept').value.trim();
  const role=document.getElementById('uRole').value;
  const position_id=document.getElementById('uPosition').value;
  const pw=document.getElementById('uPw').value;
  if(!uid||!name){ toast('工号与姓名必填',true); return; }
  if(id){
    const body={name,dept,role,position_id};
    if(pw) body.password=pw;
    api('/api/users/'+id,{method:'PUT',body}).then(r=>{
      if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('已保存'); closeModal('userMask'); renderUsers(); }
    });
  } else {
    api('/api/users/create',{method:'POST',body:{uid,name,dept,role,position_id,password:pw||'123456'}}).then(r=>{
      if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('用户已创建'); closeModal('userMask'); renderUsers(); }
    });
  }
}
function deleteUser(uid){
  askConfirm('删除用户','确定删除该用户吗？此操作会一并解除其所有项目 / 文件夹权限，且不可恢复。',()=>{
    api('/api/users/'+uid,{method:'DELETE'}).then(r=>{ if(r.error)toast(r.error,true); else { toast('已删除'); renderUsers(); } });
  },'删除','btn-danger');
}
function resetPw(uid){ askConfirm('重置密码','确定将该用户密码重置为 123456 吗？',()=>{ api('/api/users/'+uid+'/password',{method:'POST',body:{password:'123456'}}).then(r=>{ if(r.error)toast(r.error,true); else toast('密码已重置'); }); },'重置','btn-danger'); }
function toggleFreeze(uid,fz){ api('/api/users/'+uid+'/freeze',{method:'POST',body:{frozen:fz}}).then(r=>{ if(r.error)toast(r.error,true); else { toast('已更新'); renderUsers(); } }); }

async function renderAudit(){
  const r=await api('/api/audit?limit=300');
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
  const body=document.getElementById('adminBody');
  let html='<div class="admin-toolbar"><div class="admin-section-title">📋 审计日志（最近 300 条）</div></div>';
  if(!r.length){ body.innerHTML=html+'<div class="admin-empty">暂无审计记录</div>'; return; }
  html+='<table class="admin-tbl"><thead><tr><th style="width:140px">时间</th><th>操作人</th><th>动作</th><th>详情</th><th style="width:100px">IP</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  r.forEach(a=>{
    html+='<tr><td title="'+esc(a.ts||'')+'">'+fmtTime(a.ts)+'</td><td>'+esc(a.user_name)+'</td><td><span class="tag gray">'+esc(a.action_label)+'</span></td><td>'+esc(a.detail)+'</td><td>'+(a.ip||'')+'</td></tr>';
  });
  html+='</tbody></table>';
  body.innerHTML=html;
}

/* ---------- 职位管理 ---------- */
async function _loadPositions(){
  if(CURRENT.positions && CURRENT.positions.length) return;
  const r=await api('/api/admin/positions');
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); CURRENT.positions=[]; return; }
  CURRENT.positions=r;
}
async function renderPositions(){
  await _loadPositions();
  const r=CURRENT.positions;
  const body=document.getElementById('adminBody');
  let html='<div class="admin-toolbar"><div class="admin-section-title">💼 职位管理</div><div class="spacer"></div><button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openPosMask()">+ 新建职位</button></div>';
  if(!r.length){ body.innerHTML=html+'<div class="admin-empty">暂无职位</div>'; return; }
  html+='<table class="admin-tbl"><thead><tr><th>职位名称</th><th>描述</th><th>状态</th><th>创建时间</th><th style="width:220px">操作</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  r.forEach(p=>{
    html+='<tr><td>'+esc(p.name)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.description||'')+'</td>'
      +'<td><span class="tag '+(p.active?'green':'gray')+'">'+(p.active?'启用':'停用')+'</span></td>'
      +'<td>'+fmtTime(p.created_at)+'</td>'
      +'<td><div class="actions">'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="editPosition('+p.id+')">编辑</button>'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger-hover" onclick="deletePosition('+p.id+')">删除</button>'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm '+(p.active?'':'btn-success')+'" onclick="togglePosition('+p.id+')">'+(p.active?'停用':'启用')+'</button>'
      +'</div></td></tr>';
  });
  html+='</tbody></table>';
  body.innerHTML=html;
}
function openPosMask(id){
  const p=id?((CURRENT.positions||[]).find(x=>x.id===id)||{}):{};
  document.getElementById('posId').value=id||'';
  document.getElementById('posName').value=p.name||'';
  document.getElementById('posDesc').value=p.description||'';
  openModal('posMask');
}
function editPosition(id){ openPosMask(id); }
function deletePosition(id){
  const p=(CURRENT.positions||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
  askConfirm('删除职位','确认删除职位“'+(p?esc(p.name):id)+'”吗？此操作不可恢复。',()=>{
    api('/api/admin/positions/'+id,{method:'DELETE'}).then(r=>{
      if(r.error) toast(r.error,true);
      else { toast('职位已删除'); renderPositions(); }
    });
  },'删除','btn-danger');
}
function savePosition(){
  const id=document.getElementById('posId').value;
  const name=document.getElementById('posName').value.trim();
  const desc=document.getElementById('posDesc').value.trim();
  if(!name){ toast('职位名称必填',true); return; }
  const url=id?('/api/admin/positions/'+id):'/api/admin/positions';
  const method=id?'PUT':'POST';
  api(url,{method,body:{name,description:desc}}).then(r=>{
    if(r.error) toast(r.error,true);
    else { toast('保存成功'); closeModal('posMask'); renderPositions(); }
  });
}
function togglePosition(id){
  api('/api/admin/positions/'+id+'/toggle',{method:'POST'}).then(r=>{
    if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('状态已更新'); renderPositions(); }
  });
}

/* ---------- 阶段模板 ---------- */
async function renderPhaseTemplates(){
  const r=await api('/api/phase-templates');
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
  const body=document.getElementById('adminBody');
  body.innerHTML='<div class="admin-toolbar"><div class="admin-section-title">📁 阶段模板</div></div>'
    +'<p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3);margin:-4px 0 14px">此模板决定新建零件 / 新增阶段时自动生成的文件夹结构；修改仅影响之后新建的内容，已有零件不受影响。文件夹名可用 "/" 表示层级，如 Drawing/2D。</p>'
    +'<div id="tmplWrap"></div>';
  const wrap=document.getElementById('tmplWrap');
  const phases=Object.keys(r).sort();
  if(!phases.length){ wrap.innerHTML='<div class="admin-empty">暂无阶段模板</div>'; return; }
  let html='';
  phases.forEach(ph=>{
    html+='<div class="admin-card">'
      +'<div class="admin-card-title">📁 '+esc(ph)+'</div>'
      +'<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px;min-height:28px">';
    (r[ph]||[]).forEach(dt=>{
      html+='<span class="tag blue" style="gap:5px;padding-right:6px">'
        +esc(dt)+' <button style="border:none;background:none;color:var(--danger);cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;line-height:1;padding:0 2px" onclick="deletePhaseTemplate(\''+esc(ph)+'\',\''+esc(dt)+'\')">×</button></span>';
    });
    if(!(r[ph]||[]).length) html+='<span class="tag gray">暂无文件夹</span>';
    html+='</div>'
      +'<div style="display:flex;gap:8px">'
      +'<input type="text" id="tmplNew_'+ph.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'_')+'" placeholder="新文件夹名，如 ECN" style="flex:1;padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--radius-sm);font-size:13px">'
      +'<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="addPhaseTemplate(\''+esc(ph)+'\')">+ 添加</button>'
      +'</div></div>';
  });
  // 新增阶段
  html+='<div class="admin-card" style="background:#f8f9fb">'
    +'<div class="admin-card-title">➕ 新增阶段</div>'
    +'<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">'
    +'<input type="text" id="tmplNewPhase" placeholder="新阶段名称" style="flex:1;min-width:140px;padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--radius-sm);font-size:13px">'
    +'<input type="text" id="tmplNewPhaseDoc" placeholder="首个文档类型，如 Drawing/2D" style="flex:1;min-width:180px;padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--radius-sm);font-size:13px">'
    +'<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="createPhaseTemplate()">+ 新增阶段</button>'
    +'</div></div>';
  wrap.innerHTML=html;
}
function addPhaseTemplate(phase){
  const input=document.getElementById('tmplNew_'+phase.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'_'));
  const doc_type=(input.value||'').trim();
  if(!doc_type){ toast('请输入文件夹名',true); return; }
  api('/api/admin/phase-templates',{method:'POST',body:{phase,doc_type}}).then(r=>{
    if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('已添加'); renderPhaseTemplates(); }
  });
}
function deletePhaseTemplate(phase,doc_type){
  askConfirm('删除模板','确认删除 '+phase+'/'+doc_type+' ？',()=>{
    api('/api/admin/phase-templates',{method:'DELETE',body:{phase,doc_type}}).then(r=>{
      if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('已删除'); renderPhaseTemplates(); }
    });
  },'删除','btn-danger');
}
function createPhaseTemplate(){
  const phase=(document.getElementById('tmplNewPhase').value||'').trim();
  const doc_type=(document.getElementById('tmplNewPhaseDoc').value||'').trim();
  if(!phase||!doc_type){ toast('阶段名称和首个文档类型均必填',true); return; }
  api('/api/admin/phase-templates',{method:'POST',body:{phase,doc_type}}).then(r=>{
    if(r.error) toast(r.error,true); else { toast('阶段已创建'); renderPhaseTemplates(); }
  });
}

/* ---------- 系统设置 ---------- */
let SETTINGS_ROOTS=[];
let SETTINGS_ROOT_ROWS=[]; // 最近一次后端返回的完整状态
function fmtBytes(b){
  if(b===0) return '0 B';
  const units=['B','KB','MB','GB','TB']; let u=0;
  while(b>=1024 && u<units.length-1){ b/=1024; u++; }
  return b.toFixed(u===0?0:2)+' '+units[u];
}
async function renderSettings(){
  const r=await api('/api/admin/system-config/upload-roots');
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
  SETTINGS_ROOT_ROWS=(r.roots||[]).slice();
  SETTINGS_ROOTS=SETTINGS_ROOT_ROWS.map(x=>x.path);
  const body=document.getElementById('adminBody');
  body.innerHTML='<div class="admin-toolbar"><div class="admin-section-title">⚙️ 系统设置</div></div>'
    +'<div class="admin-card">'
    +'<div class="admin-card-title">文件存储路径</div>'
    +'<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3);margin-bottom:10px;line-height:1.6">可配置多个存储路径。<b>新上传文件会写入列表最后一个路径</b>；历史文件仍保留在原路径，系统会自动兼容读取、删除、修改。请勿将路径设为公共共享盘。</div>'
    +'<div id="rootRows"></div>'
    +'<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:10px">'
    +'<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="addRootRow()">＋ 新增路径</button>'
    +'<div style="display:flex;gap:10px"><button class="btn" onclick="openCurrentRoot()">打开当前目录</button><button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="saveSettings()">保存设置</button></div>'
    +'</div>'
    +'</div>';
  renderRootRows(r.roots||[]);
}
function renderRootRows(rows){
  const container=document.getElementById('rootRows');
  if(!container) return;
  let html='<table class="admin-tbl"><thead><tr><th style="width:40px">#</th><th>存储路径</th><th style="width:110px">状态</th><th style="width:100px">剩余空间</th><th style="width:180px">操作</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  rows.forEach((row,idx)=>{
    const isCurrent=idx===rows.length-1;
    const status=row.exists?(row.writable?'✅ 可用':'⚠️ 只读'):'❌ 不存在';
    html+='<tr>'
      +'<td>'+(idx+1)+(isCurrent?' <span title="当前写入路径" style="color:var(--green)">●</span>':'')+'</td>'
      +'<td><input type="text" class="root-path-input" data-idx="'+idx+'" data-original="'+esc(row.path)+'" value="'+esc(row.path)+'" placeholder="例如 D:/Data/npi-uploads"></td>'
      +'<td style="font-size:12px">'+status+'</td>'
      +'<td style="font-size:12px">'+(row.exists?fmtBytes(row.free):'-')+'</td>'
      +'<td><div class="actions" style="gap:4px">'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="moveRoot('+idx+',-1)" '+(idx===0?'disabled':'')+'>↑</button>'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="moveRoot('+idx+',1)" '+(idx===rows.length-1?'disabled':'')+'>↓</button>'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="openRoot('+idx+')">打开</button>'
      +'<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="removeRoot('+idx+')" '+(rows.length<=1?'disabled':'')+'>删除</button>'
      +'</div></td>'
      +'</tr>';
  });
  html+='</tbody></table>';
  container.innerHTML=html;
}
function _buildRowsFromInputs(){
  const inputs=document.querySelectorAll('.root-path-input');
  const rows=[];
  inputs.forEach(inp=>{
    const idx=+inp.dataset.idx;
    const val=(inp.value||'').trim();
    const original=(inp.dataset.original||'').trim();
    // 如果用户没改这行的路径，保留当前显示的状态（从 DOM 读取）
    const tr=inp.closest('tr');
    const statusCell=tr?tr.cells[2]:null;
    const freeCell=tr?tr.cells[3]:null;
    const statusText=statusCell?statusCell.innerText:'';
    const exists=statusText.indexOf('不存在')<0;
    const writable=statusText.indexOf('可用')>=0;
    if(val===original && exists){
      const old=SETTINGS_ROOT_ROWS.find(r=>r.path===val);
      if(old){ rows[idx]={...old, path:val}; return; }
    }
    rows[idx]={path:val, exists:exists, writable:writable, free:0, total:0};
  });
  // 补齐 SETTINGS_ROOTS 末尾新增的空行
  while(rows.length<SETTINGS_ROOTS.length) rows.push({path:'', exists:false, writable:false, free:0, total:0});
  return rows;
}
function addRootRow(){
  const rows=_buildRowsFromInputs();
  rows.push({path:'', exists:false, writable:false, free:0, total:0});
  SETTINGS_ROOTS=rows.map(r=>r.path);
  SETTINGS_ROOT_ROWS=rows.slice();
  renderRootRows(rows);
}
function removeRoot(idx){
  if(SETTINGS_ROOTS.length<=1){ toast('至少需要保留一个存储路径',true); return; }
  const rows=_buildRowsFromInputs();
  rows.splice(idx,1);
  SETTINGS_ROOTS=rows.map(r=>r.path);
  SETTINGS_ROOT_ROWS=rows.slice();
  renderRootRows(rows);
}
function moveRoot(idx,dir){
  const newIdx=idx+dir;
  if(newIdx<0 || newIdx>=SETTINGS_ROOTS.length) return;
  const rows=_buildRowsFromInputs();
  [rows[idx],rows[newIdx]]=[rows[newIdx],rows[idx]];
  SETTINGS_ROOTS=rows.map(r=>r.path);
  SETTINGS_ROOT_ROWS=rows.slice();
  renderRootRows(rows);
}
function openRoot(idx){
  api('/api/admin/system-config/open-upload-root',{method:'POST',body:{index:idx}}).then(r=>{
    if(r.error) toast(r.error,true);
    else toast('已尝试打开目录：'+r.path);
  });
}
function openCurrentRoot(){
  openRoot(SETTINGS_ROOTS.length-1);
}
function saveSettings(){
  const inputs=document.querySelectorAll('.root-path-input');
  const roots=[];
  inputs.forEach(inp=>{
    const v=inp.value.trim();
    if(v) roots.push(v);
  });
  if(roots.length===0){ toast('至少需要保留一个存储路径',true); return; }
  api('/api/admin/system-config/upload-roots',{method:'PUT',body:{roots}}).then(r=>{
    if(r.error) toast(r.error,true);
    else { toast('保存成功'); renderSettings(); }
  });
}

/* 选中零件节点时：左侧内联渲染该零件的 3D 图（three.js，替代原「零件概览图」），
 * 右侧显示零件信息卡。项目节点不展示概览图，直接走文件列表（见 selectNode/loadFiles）。
 * 普通成员只读，管理员/可编辑成员可上传。 */
async function renderPartOverview(){
  const wrap=document.getElementById('content');
  const pid=SEL.projId, ptid=SEL.partId;
  const titleName = (TREE.find(n=>n.id==ptid && n.type==='part')||{}).name || '';
  const canAdmin=isProjAdmin(pid);
  p3dDestroy();
  const can3d = canEditProjectContent(pid);
  wrap.innerHTML='<div class="overview-wrap">'
    +'<div class="overview-title">🧊 零件 3D 图：'+esc(titleName)
    +'<span class="meta" style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t3);font-weight:400;margin-left:10px">STEP / IGES / STL · 可旋转缩放</span></div>'
    +'<div class="ov-split">'
    +'<div class="ov-left"><div id="part3dHost" class="p3d-wrap"><div class="loading-box"><div class="spinner"></div>正在加载 3D 图…</div></div></div>'
    +'<div class="ov-right" id="partInfoPanel"><div class="pi-card"><div class="pi-body" style="color:var(--t3);font-size:12px">正在加载零件信息…</div></div></div>'
    +'</div></div>';
  renderPartInfoPanel(ptid, pid, canAdmin);
  renderPart3D(ptid, pid, can3d);
}
/* ---------- 零件 3D 图：详情页左侧内联渲染（替代原「零件概览图」） ----------
 * 复用 m3dEnsureThree() 按需加载 three.js；使用独立的 P3D 渲染器实例，
 * 与弹窗中的 M3D 互不干扰。切换节点/进入文件视图时调用 p3dDestroy() 释放。 */
let P3D={inited:false, renderer:null, scene:null, camera:null, controls:null,
         model:null, grid:null, wire:false, raf:0, ro:null, curId:null, list:[], canManage:false};
window.addEventListener('resize', function(){ p3dResize(); });

function p3dDestroy(){
  try{ if(P3D.raf) cancelAnimationFrame(P3D.raf); }catch(e){}
  P3D.raf=0;
  try{ if(P3D.ro){ P3D.ro.disconnect(); P3D.ro=null; } }catch(e){}
  try{
    if(P3D.controls && P3D.controls.dispose) P3D.controls.dispose();
    if(P3D.renderer){
      const cv=P3D.renderer.domElement;
      if(cv && cv.parentNode) cv.parentNode.removeChild(cv);
      if(P3D.renderer.dispose) P3D.renderer.dispose();
      if(P3D.renderer.forceContextLoss) P3D.renderer.forceContextLoss();
    }
  }catch(e){}
  clearTimeout(P3D._revealT); P3D._pendingReveal=false; P3D._lastW=P3D._lastH=0;
  P3D.renderer=null; P3D.scene=null; P3D.camera=null; P3D.controls=null;
  P3D.model=null; P3D.grid=null; P3D.wire=false; P3D.inited=false; P3D.curId=null;
}

async function renderPart3D(ptid, pid, canManage){
  P3D.canManage=!!canManage;
  const host=document.getElementById('part3dHost');
  if(!host) return;
  host.innerHTML=
    '<div class="p3d-bar">'
    +'<span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t2)">模型</span>'
    +'<select class="p3d-sel" id="p3dSel" onchange="p3dSelect(this.value)"><option>加载中…</option></select>'
    +'<span class="spacer"></span>'
    +'<button class="btn btn-xs" onclick="p3dToggleWire()">线框</button>'
    +'<button class="btn btn-xs" onclick="p3dResetView()">复位</button>'
    +'</div>'
    +'<div class="p3d-viewer" id="p3dViewer"><div class="p3d-empty" id="p3dEmpty">正在加载 3D 图…</div></div>'
    +'<div class="p3d-meta" id="p3dMeta" style="display:none"></div>';
  const list=await api('/api/models3d?project_id='+pid+'&part_id='+ptid);
  if(!list || list.error){
    const el=document.getElementById('p3dEmpty'); if(el) el.textContent='加载失败：'+((list&&list.error)||'未知错误');
    return;
  }
  P3D.list=list||[];
  const sel=document.getElementById('p3dSel');
  const ready=P3D.list.filter(m=>m.status==='ready');
  if(!P3D.list.length){
    sel.innerHTML='<option>—</option>'; sel.disabled=true;
    document.getElementById('p3dEmpty').innerHTML='<div class="ico">🧊</div><div>该零件暂无 3D 图</div>'
      +'<div style="font-size:12px">把 STEP / IGES / STL 文件上传到该零件的文件夹后，这里会自动显示 3D 图</div>';
    return;
  }
  sel.disabled=P3D.list.length<=1;
  sel.innerHTML=P3D.list.map(m=>'<option value="'+m.id+'"'+(m.status!=='ready'?' disabled':'')+'>'
    +esc(m.original_name)+(m.status==='ready'?'':'（未就绪）')+'</option>').join('');
  // 默认显示「最新上传的」；若最新那条不可用，则退回到最新的可用项
  const first=(P3D.list[0].status==='ready') ? P3D.list[0] : (ready[0]||P3D.list[0]);
  sel.value=first.id;
  p3dShow(first);
}

function p3dSelect(id){
  const m=(P3D.list||[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
  if(m) p3dShow(m);
}

async function p3dShow(m){
  try{ await m3dEnsureThree(); }catch(e){
    const el=document.getElementById('p3dEmpty'); if(el) el.textContent='3D 组件加载失败：'+e.message;
    return;
  }
  if(!p3dInitViewer()) return;
  P3D.curId=m.id;
  const meta=document.getElementById('p3dMeta');
  if(m.status!=='ready'){
    p3dClearModel('该模型无可预览的转换结果'+(m.error_msg?('：'+m.error_msg):''));
    return;
  }
  const empty=document.getElementById('p3dEmpty');
  if(empty){ empty.style.display=''; empty.textContent='正在加载模型…'; }
  if(meta){ meta.innerHTML=''; meta.style.display='none'; }
  const glbUrl=m.glb_url||('/api/models3d/'+m.id+'/glb');
  // 零件文件按需转换：首次预览先取元数据（顺带完成转换），再加载 GLB
  if((!m.meta || !Object.keys(m.meta).length) && m.meta_url){
    if(empty) empty.textContent='正在转换模型…（首次预览需转换，请稍候）';
    try{
      const mm=await api(m.meta_url);
      if(mm && mm.error){ p3dClearModel('无法生成 3D 预览：'+mm.error); return; }
      m.meta=mm||{};
    }catch(e){ p3dClearModel('无法生成 3D 预览：'+e.message); return; }
    if(empty) empty.textContent='正在加载模型…';
  }
  new THREE.GLTFLoader().load(glbUrl, (gltf)=>{
    if(P3D.model && P3D.scene){ P3D.scene.remove(P3D.model); P3D.model=null; }
    P3D.model=gltf.scene;
    npiNormalizeMaterials(P3D.model);
    P3D.scene.add(P3D.model);
    P3D._userMoved=false;
    // 零件信息面板等会让 3D 视口尺寸稍后才稳定。稳定前先隐藏模型，
    // 稳定后一次性贴合再显示，避免出现「先小后大」的跳变。
    P3D._pendingReveal=true;
    if(P3D.renderer) P3D.renderer.domElement.style.visibility='hidden';
    p3dResize();
    p3dFitView(P3D.model);
    clearTimeout(P3D._revealT);
    P3D._revealT=setTimeout(p3dReveal, 250);
    p3dRenderMeta(m);
  }, undefined, (err)=>{
    const e3=document.getElementById('p3dEmpty');
    if(e3){ e3.style.display=''; e3.textContent='模型加载失败：'+((err&&err.message)||'未知错误'); }
  });
}

function p3dClearModel(msg){
  if(P3D.model && P3D.scene){ P3D.scene.remove(P3D.model); P3D.model=null; }
  clearTimeout(P3D._revealT); P3D._pendingReveal=false;      // 取消未完成的「显示」
  if(P3D.renderer) P3D.renderer.domElement.style.visibility='';
  const el=document.getElementById('p3dEmpty');
  if(el){ el.style.display=''; el.textContent=msg||'从上方选择模型开始预览'; }
  const meta=document.getElementById('p3dMeta'); if(meta){ meta.innerHTML=''; meta.style.display='none'; }
}

function p3dInitViewer(){
  const box=document.getElementById('p3dViewer');
  if(!box) return false;
  if(P3D.inited && P3D.renderer) return true;
  if(typeof THREE==='undefined'){ const el=document.getElementById('p3dEmpty'); if(el) el.textContent='three.js 未加载'; return false; }
  const w=box.clientWidth||600, h=box.clientHeight||420;
  P3D.renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({antialias:true, alpha:true});
  // 同上：sRGB 输出转换，保证模型本色正确显示
  if(THREE.sRGBEncoding!=null) P3D.renderer.outputEncoding=THREE.sRGBEncoding;
  P3D.renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio||1);
  P3D.renderer.setSize(w,h);
  box.appendChild(P3D.renderer.domElement);
  P3D.scene=new THREE.Scene();
  P3D.camera=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, w/h, 0.01, 100000);
  P3D.camera.position.set(200,200,200);
  P3D.scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff,0.75));
  const d1=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.7); d1.position.set(1,1,1); P3D.scene.add(d1);
  const d2=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.35); d2.position.set(-1,-0.6,-1); P3D.scene.add(d2);
  if(THREE.OrbitControls){
    P3D.controls=new THREE.OrbitControls(P3D.camera, P3D.renderer.domElement);
    P3D.controls.enableDamping=true; P3D.controls.dampingFactor=0.12;
    P3D.controls.addEventListener('start', ()=>{ P3D._userMoved=true; });  // 用户手动旋转/缩放后，不再自动改视角
  }
  P3D.inited=true;
  (function loop(){ P3D.raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    if(P3D.controls) P3D.controls.update();
    if(P3D.renderer) P3D.renderer.render(P3D.scene, P3D.camera);
  })();
  if(window.ResizeObserver && !P3D.ro){ P3D.ro=new ResizeObserver(()=>p3dResize()); P3D.ro.observe(box); }
  return true;
}

function p3dResize(){
  const box=document.getElementById('p3dViewer');
  if(!box||!P3D.renderer||!P3D.camera) return;
  const w=box.clientWidth||600, h=box.clientHeight||420;
  if(w<=0||h<=0) return;
  const sizeChanged=(P3D._lastW!==w || P3D._lastH!==h);
  P3D._lastW=w; P3D._lastH=h;
  P3D.camera.aspect=w/h; P3D.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  P3D.renderer.setSize(w,h);
  if(P3D._pendingReveal){
    // 尺寸还在变：推迟「显示并贴合」，等不再变化再一次性完成
    clearTimeout(P3D._revealT); P3D._revealT=setTimeout(p3dReveal, 250);
  }else if(sizeChanged && P3D.model && !P3D._userMoved){
    // 用户未手动调整视角时，布局/窗口变化后保持默认大小
    p3dFitView(P3D.model);
  }
}

/* 画布尺寸稳定后：最后贴合一次，然后显示模型（避免「先小后大」的跳变） */
function p3dReveal(){
  if(!P3D._pendingReveal) return;
  clearTimeout(P3D._revealT);
  if(P3D.model && !P3D._userMoved) p3dFitView(P3D.model);
  P3D._pendingReveal=false;
  const e=document.getElementById('p3dEmpty'); if(e) e.style.display='none';
  if(P3D.renderer) P3D.renderer.domElement.style.visibility='';
}

function p3dFitView(obj){
  P3D._userMoved=false;                 // 每次贴合都视为「回到默认视角」
  const box=new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(obj);
  const size=box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3());
  const center=box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  const maxDim=Math.max(size.x,size.y,size.z)||1;
  const cam=P3D.camera;
  // 用包围盒 8 个角点做「投影贴合」：迭代求相机距离，使模型在画面里尽量大
  // （比按包围球估算更准，且不裁边；TARGET 为目标填充比例）
  const pts=[];
  const mn=box.min, mx=box.max;
  [mn.x,mx.x].forEach(x=>[mn.y,mx.y].forEach(y=>[mn.z,mx.z].forEach(z=>pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(x,y,z)))));
  const dir=new THREE.Vector3(1,0.8,1).normalize();
  const TARGET=0.92;
  let dist=maxDim*1.5;
  for(let it=0; it<5; it++){
    cam.position.copy(center).addScaledVector(dir, dist);
    cam.lookAt(center);
    cam.updateMatrixWorld(true); cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
    let m=0;
    pts.forEach(p=>{ const v=p.clone().project(cam); m=Math.max(m, Math.abs(v.x), Math.abs(v.y)); });
    if(m<1e-4) break;
    const nd=dist*m/TARGET;                       // 投影尺寸≈与距离成反比，一步即接近
    if(Math.abs(nd-dist) < dist*0.004){ dist=nd; break; }
    dist=nd;
  }
  dist=Math.max(dist, maxDim*0.05);
  cam.position.copy(center).addScaledVector(dir, dist);
  cam.near=Math.max(maxDim/10000, 0.001); cam.far=dist+maxDim*100;
  cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
  if(P3D.controls){ P3D.controls.target.copy(center); P3D.controls.update(); }
  P3D._home={p:cam.position.clone(), t:center.clone()};
}

function p3dResetView(){
  if(!P3D._home||!P3D.camera) return;
  P3D.camera.position.copy(P3D._home.p);
  if(P3D.controls){ P3D.controls.target.copy(P3D._home.t); P3D.controls.update(); }
}
function p3dToggleWire(){
  if(!P3D.model) return;
  P3D.wire=!P3D.wire;
  P3D.model.traverse(o=>{ if(o.isMesh) o.material.wireframe=P3D.wire; });
}

/* 统一 3D 材质：OCCT 导出的 glTF 只带 baseColorFactor，按 glTF 规范默认 metallic=1，
 * 在没有环境贴图时会渲染得发暗发黑。这里统一为金属度 0、粗糙度 0.6，
 * 让模型本色在现有灯光下正常显示；无材质的模型（走默认材质）同样受益。 */
function npiNormalizeMaterials(scene){
  if(!scene || !scene.traverse) return;
  scene.traverse(o=>{
    if(!o.isMesh || !o.material) return;
    const arr=Array.isArray(o.material)?o.material:[o.material];
    arr.forEach(mm=>{
      if(mm && 'metalness' in mm){ mm.metalness=0.0; mm.roughness=0.6; mm.needsUpdate=true; }
    });
  });
}

function p3dRenderMeta(m){
  const meta=document.getElementById('p3dMeta');
  if(!meta) return;
  const mt=m.meta||{};
  const n=v=>(v==null?'-':(typeof v==='number'?v.toFixed(2):v));
  const bb=mt.bbox||{}; const sz=bb.size||[0,0,0];
  const c=mt.counts||{};
  meta.style.display='';
  meta.innerHTML='<b>'+esc(m.original_name)+'</b> · '+esc(m.src_format||'')
    +'<br>体积 '+n(mt.volume)+' mm³ ｜ 面积 '+n(mt.area)+' mm²'
    +' ｜ 尺寸 '+n(sz[0])+' × '+n(sz[1])+' × '+n(sz[2])+' mm'
    +'<br>实体/面/三角面 '+(c.solids!=null?c.solids:'-')+' / '+(c.faces!=null?c.faces:'-')+' / '+(c.triangles!=null?c.triangles:'-')
    +' ｜ 上传人 '+esc(m.uploader_name||m.uploader_id||'-')+' · '+esc((m.uploaded_at||'').slice(0,16));
}

/* 删除当前显示的 3D 图（工具栏「删除」按钮）。
 * 删除后重建内联 3D 视图，避免「删掉了但画面还在渲染」的问题。 */
function p3dDeleteCurrent(){
  const sel=document.getElementById('p3dSel');
  let id=(sel && sel.value && sel.value!=='—') ? sel.value : P3D.curId;
  if(!id){ toast('当前没有可删除的 3D 图',true); return; }
  const m=(P3D.list||[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))||{};
  askConfirm('删除 3D 模型','确认删除「'+(m.original_name||('#'+id))+'」？原始文件与转换结果都会被移除。',()=>{
    api('/api/models3d/'+id,{method:'DELETE'}).then(r=>{
      if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
      toast('已删除');
      if(SEL && SEL.partId) renderPart3D(SEL.partId, SEL.projId, P3D.canManage);
    }).catch(e=>toast('删除失败：'+e.message,true));
  },'删除','btn-danger');
}

function p3dUploadClick(){
  if(!SEL.partId){ toast('请先选中一个零件',true); return; }
  const inp=document.createElement('input'); inp.type='file';
  inp.accept='.step,.stp,.iges,.igs,.stl,.brep,.brp';
  inp.onchange=()=>p3dUpload(inp);
  inp.click();
}

function p3dUpload(input){
  const f=input.files && input.files[0];
  input.value='';
  if(!f) return;
  const fd=new FormData();
  fd.append('file',f);
  fd.append('project_id',SEL.projId);
  fd.append('part_id',SEL.partId);
  fd.append('unit','mm');
  toast('正在上传并转换 3D 模型，请稍候…');
  fetch('/api/models3d/upload',{method:'POST',body:fd,credentials:'same-origin'})
    .then(r=>r.json())
    .then(r=>{
      if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
      if(r.status==='ready') toast('3D 图上传并转换成功');
      else toast('已保存，但转换失败：'+(r.error||'未知原因'),true);
      renderPart3D(SEL.partId, SEL.projId, P3D.canManage);
    })
    .catch(e=>toast('上传失败：'+e.message,true));
}

/* ---------- 零件信息卡（概览图右侧，项目管理员可配置字段） ---------- */
// 默认 8 字段（项目未自定义时回退）
const DEFAULT_PART_INFO_FIELDS=[
  {key:'no_',label:'NO.'},{key:'rev',label:'REV'},{key:'model',label:'模号'},
  {key:'description',label:'Part description'},{key:'process',label:'制程'},
  {key:'pd',label:'PD'},{key:'dqe',label:'DQE'},{key:'stamp_loc',label:'冲压地点'},
];
async function renderPartInfoPanel(ptid, pid, canAdmin){
  const box=document.getElementById('partInfoPanel');
  if(!box) return;
  let fields=[], info={data:{},_canEdit:false};
  try{
    const [fRes,iRes]=await Promise.all([
      api('/api/projects/'+pid+'/info_fields'),
      api('/api/parts/'+ptid+'/info'),
    ]);
    fields=Array.isArray(fRes)?fRes:DEFAULT_PART_INFO_FIELDS;
    info=iRes.error?{data:{},_canEdit:false,error:iRes.error}:iRes;
  }catch(e){
    box.innerHTML='<div class="pi-card"><div class="pi-body" style="color:var(--danger);font-size:12px">加载失败</div></div>';
    return;
  }
  if(info.error){ box.innerHTML='<div class="pi-card"><div class="pi-body" style="color:var(--danger);font-size:12px">'+esc(info.error)+'</div></div>'; return; }
  // 可编辑权以接口返回 _canEdit 为准（后端已按「项目内任一可编辑文件夹权限」放行 PD）；
  // 「字段」配置按钮仍仅项目管理员可用，普通可编辑成员只显示「保存」
  const editable=info._canEdit===true;
  const showFields=!!canAdmin;
  let h='<div class="pi-card">'
    +'<div class="pi-head"><span>零件信息</span>'
    +'<div class="pi-head-actions">'
    +(editable
      ? (showFields?'<button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="openPartInfoFieldsModal('+pid+','+ptid+')">字段</button>':'')
        +'<button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" id="piSaveBtn" onclick="savePartInfo('+ptid+','+pid+')">保存</button>'
      : '<span class="pi-readonly">只读</span>')
    +'</div></div><div class="pi-body">';
  if(!fields.length){
    h+='<div style="color:var(--t3);font-size:12px">暂无字段配置</div>';
  }else{
    for(const f of fields){
      const v=(info.data&&info.data[f.key])||'';
      h+='<div class="pi-row"><label>'+esc(f.label)+'</label>'
        +(editable
          ? '<textarea class="pi-input" data-field="'+escAttr(f.key)+'" rows="2" maxlength="500" placeholder="—">'+esc(v)+'</textarea>'
          : '<div class="pi-val">'+esc(v).replace(/\n/g,'<br>')+'</div>')
        +'</div>';
    }
  }
  h+='</div>';
  if(info.updated_at){
    h+='<div class="pi-foot">最后更新：'+esc(String(info.updated_at).slice(0,16))+(info.updated_by?(' · '+esc(info.updated_by_name||info.updated_by)):'')+'</div>';
  }
  h+='</div>';
  box.innerHTML=h;
  bindPartInfoScrollHint();
}
async function savePartInfo(ptid,pid){
  const box=document.getElementById('partInfoPanel');
  if(!box) return;
  const data={};
  box.querySelectorAll('.pi-input').forEach(inp=>{ data[inp.dataset.field]=inp.value.trim(); });
  const btn=document.getElementById('piSaveBtn');
  if(btn){ btn.disabled=true; btn.textContent='保存中…'; }
  const r=await api('/api/parts/'+ptid+'/info',{method:'PUT',body:{data}});
  if(btn){ btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent='保存'; }
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
  toast('零件信息已保存');
  renderPartInfoPanel(ptid,pid,isProjAdmin(pid));
}
// 零件信息面板：检测内容溢出，切换上/下箭头提示（含滚动监听）
function bindPartInfoScrollHint(){
  const body=document.querySelector('#partInfoPanel .pi-body');
  if(!body) return;
  const update=()=>{
    const overflow=body.scrollHeight-body.clientHeight>2;
    body.classList.toggle('pi-not-top', overflow && body.scrollTop>2);
    body.classList.toggle('pi-not-bottom', overflow && body.scrollTop+body.clientHeight<body.scrollHeight-2);
  };
  body.addEventListener('scroll',update,{passive:true});
  update();
  requestAnimationFrame(update);
  setTimeout(update,80);
}

/* ---------- 字段配置弹窗 ---------- */
function openPartInfoFieldsModal(pid,ptid){
  CURRENT.partInfoFieldsPid=pid;
  CURRENT.partInfoFieldsPtid=ptid;
  api('/api/projects/'+pid+'/info_fields').then(fields=>{
    CURRENT.partInfoFields=Array.isArray(fields)?fields.slice():DEFAULT_PART_INFO_FIELDS.slice();
    renderPartInfoFieldsEditor();
    openModal('partInfoFieldsMask');
  }).catch(()=>toast('加载字段配置失败',true));
}
function renderPartInfoFieldsEditor(){
  const box=document.getElementById('partInfoFieldsBody');
  const list=CURRENT.partInfoFields||[];
  let h='<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--t2);margin-bottom:10px">项目管理员可新增、修改标签或删除字段；key 自动生成后不可改。</div>';
  h+='<div id="partInfoFieldsList">';
  list.forEach((f,idx)=>{
    h+='<div class="pi-field-row" data-idx="'+idx+'">'
      +'<input type="text" class="pi-input" value="'+escAttr(f.label)+'" onchange="updatePartInfoFieldLabel('+idx+',this.value)" placeholder="显示名称" title="显示名称">'
      +'<span class="pi-field-key">'+esc(f.key)+'</span>'
      +'<input type="number" class="pi-input sort" value="'+f.sort_order+'" onchange="updatePartInfoFieldSort('+idx+',this.value)" title="排序">'
      +'<span class="pi-field-del" onclick="deletePartInfoField('+idx+')" title="删除">删除</span>'
      +'</div>';
  });
  h+='</div>';
  h+='<div class="pi-field-add">'
    +'<input type="text" class="pi-input" id="newPartInfoFieldLabel" placeholder="新字段显示名称（如：表面处理）">'
    +'<button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" onclick="addPartInfoField()">+ 添加</button>'
    +'</div>';
  box.innerHTML=h;
}
function updatePartInfoFieldLabel(idx,label){
  const list=CURRENT.partInfoFields;
  if(list&&list[idx]) list[idx].label=label.trim();
}
function updatePartInfoFieldSort(idx,val){
  const list=CURRENT.partInfoFields;
  if(list&&list[idx]) list[idx].sort_order=parseInt(val,10)||0;
}
function deletePartInfoField(idx){
  const list=CURRENT.partInfoFields;
  if(!list) return;
  list.splice(idx,1);
  renderPartInfoFieldsEditor();
}
function keyFromLabel(label,existing){
  let base=label.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_\u4e00-\u9fa5]/g,'').replace(/[\u4e00-\u9fa5]/g,function(c){return c});
  base=base.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g,'_').replace(/_+/g,'_').replace(/^_|_$/g,'').toLowerCase();
  if(!base) base='field';
  let key=base, i=1;
  while(existing.has(key)){ key=base+'_'+i; i++; }
  return key;
}
function addPartInfoField(){
  const input=document.getElementById('newPartInfoFieldLabel');
  const label=(input.value||'').trim();
  if(!label){ toast('请输入字段名称',true); return; }
  const list=CURRENT.partInfoFields||[];
  const existing=new Set(list.map(f=>f.key));
  const key=keyFromLabel(label,existing);
  list.push({key,label,sort_order:list.length});
  input.value='';
  renderPartInfoFieldsEditor();
}
async function savePartInfoFields(){
  const pid=CURRENT.partInfoFieldsPid;
  const ptid=CURRENT.partInfoFieldsPtid;
  const list=CURRENT.partInfoFields||[];
  // 按 sort_order 排序，确保提交顺序即展示顺序
  const payload=list.slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.sort_order||0)-(b.sort_order||0)).map(f=>({key:f.key,label:f.label,sort_order:f.sort_order||0}));
  const r=await api('/api/projects/'+pid+'/info_fields',{method:'PUT',body:payload});
  if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
  toast('字段配置已保存');
  closeModal('partInfoFieldsMask');
  if(ptid) renderPartInfoPanel(ptid,pid,isProjAdmin(pid));
}

/* ---------- 右键菜单 ---------- */
let CTX_TARGET=null;
function showCtxMenu(e, el){
  e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
  const canAdmin=isProjAdmin(+el.dataset.pid);
  if(!canAdmin) return;
  selectNode(el);
  CTX_TARGET=el;
  const d=el.dataset;
  const type=d.type;
  let items='';
  if(type==='project'){
    items+='<div class="ctx-item" onclick="ctxAction(\'renameProject\')">✏️ 重命名项目</div>';
    items+='<div class="ctx-item" onclick="ctxAction(\'createPartCtx\')">+ 新增零件</div>';
    const isHidden = d.hidden==='1';
    items+='<div class="ctx-item" onclick="ctxAction(\'toggleProjectHidden\')">'+(isHidden?'👁 显示项目':'🙈 隐藏项目')+'</div>';
  } else if(type==='part'){
    items+='<div class="ctx-item" onclick="ctxAction(\'renamePart\')">✏️ 重命名零件</div>';
    items+='<div class="ctx-item" onclick="ctxAction(\'createPhaseCtx\')">+ 新增阶段</div>';
  } else if(type==='phase'){
    items+='<div class="ctx-item" onclick="ctxAction(\'renamePhase\')">✏️ 重命名阶段</div>';
    items+='<div class="ctx-item" onclick="ctxAction(\'addDocType\')">📁 在该阶段下新增子文件夹</div>';
  } else if(type==='doctype'){
    // 所有子文件夹（标准模板 + 自定义）均允许重命名/删除
    items+='<div class="ctx-item" onclick="ctxAction(\'renameDocType\')">✏️ 重命名文件夹</div>';
    items+='<div class="ctx-item danger" onclick="ctxAction(\'delDocType\')">🗑️ 删除文件夹</div>';
  }
  if(!items){ hideCtxMenu(); return; }
  const menu=document.getElementById('ctxMenu');
  menu.innerHTML=items;
  menu.style.left=Math.min(e.clientX, window.innerWidth-180)+'px';
  menu.style.top=Math.min(e.clientY, window.innerHeight-160)+'px';
  menu.classList.add('show');
}
function hideCtxMenu(){ document.getElementById('ctxMenu').classList.remove('show'); CTX_TARGET=null; }
document.addEventListener('click', hideCtxMenu);
document.addEventListener('contextmenu', e=>{
  // 任何非树节点的右键先关闭菜单
  if(!e.target.closest('.tn')) hideCtxMenu();
});
async function ctxAction(kind){
  const el=CTX_TARGET;
  hideCtxMenu();
  if(!el) return;
  const d=el.dataset;
  if(kind==='renameProject'){
    const newNo=await askPromptAsync('重命名项目','请输入新的项目编号：', d.name, '保存');
    if(!newNo || newNo===d.name) return;
    const r=await api('/api/projects/'+d.pid,{method:'PUT',body:{project_no:newNo}});
    if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
    toast('已重命名'); loadTree();
  } else if(kind==='renamePart'){
    const newName=await askPromptAsync('重命名零件','请输入新的零件名称：', d.name, '保存');
    if(!newName || newName===d.name) return;
    const r=await api('/api/parts/'+d.ptid,{method:'PUT',body:{name:newName}});
    if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
    toast('已重命名'); loadTree();
  } else if(kind==='renamePhase'){
    const newPh=await askPromptAsync('重命名阶段','请输入新的阶段名称：', d.name, '保存');
    if(!newPh || newPh===d.name) return;
    const r=await api('/api/parts/'+d.ptid+'/phases/'+encodeURIComponent(d.name),{method:'PUT',body:{phase:newPh}});
    if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
    toast('已重命名'); loadTree();
  } else if(kind==='toggleProjectHidden'){
    const isHidden = d.hidden==='1';
    const r=await api('/api/projects/'+d.pid+'/hide',{method:'POST'});
    if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
    toast(isHidden?'项目已显示，普通用户可见':'项目已隐藏，普通用户不可见');
    loadTree();
  } else if(kind==='createPartCtx'){ createPart(); }
  else if(kind==='createPhaseCtx'){ createPhase(); }
  else if(kind==='addDocType'){
    const dtName=await askPromptAsync('新增子文件夹','在「'+d.name+'」阶段下新增子文件夹：', '', '创建');
    if(!dtName) return;
    const r=await api('/api/custom-doc-types',{method:'POST',body:{project_id:+d.pid,part_id: +d.ptid, phase:d.name, doc_type:dtName}});
    if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
    toast('子文件夹已创建'); loadTree();
  } else if(kind==='renameDocType'){
    const newName=await askPromptAsync('重命名子文件夹','请输入新的文件夹名称：', d.name, '保存');
    if(!newName || newName===d.name) return;
    const r=await api('/api/custom-doc-types/rename',{
      method:'PUT',
      body:{
        project_id:+d.pid,
        part_id:+d.ptid||null,
        phase:d.phase,
        old_doc_type:d.name,
        new_doc_type:newName
      }
    });
    if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
    toast('已重命名'); loadTree();
  } else if(kind==='delDocType'){
    askConfirm('删除文件夹','确认删除该文件夹？该文件夹下的所有文件将被永久删除，且无法恢复。', async ()=>{
      const r=await api('/api/custom-doc-types/delete',{
        method:'POST',
        body:{
          project_id:+d.pid,
          part_id:+d.ptid||null,
          phase:d.phase,
          doc_type:d.name
        }
      });
      if(r.error){ toast(r.error,true); return; }
      toast('已删除'); loadTree();
    },'删除','btn-danger');
  }
}

/* ---------- 修改密码 ---------- */
function showChangePw(){ openModal('pwMask'); }
function enforceForceChange(){
  // 初始密码未修改前，锁定界面仅允许改密
  CURRENT.forceChange=true;
  document.getElementById('loginWrap').style.display='none';
  showChangePw();
  toast('请先修改初始密码后再使用系统',true);
}
function submitChangePw(){
  const old=document.getElementById('pwOld').value, nw=document.getElementById('pwNew').value;
  api('/api/change-password',{method:'POST',body:{old,new:nw}}).then(r=>{
    if(r.error) toast(r.error,true);
    else {
      toast('密码已修改');
      closeModal('pwMask');
      if(CURRENT.forceChange){ CURRENT.forceChange=false; location.reload(); }
    }
  });
}

let overlayZ = 200;
function openModal(id){
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  el.style.zIndex = ++overlayZ;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function closeModal(id){
  // 强制修改初始密码期间，禁止关闭改密弹窗
  if(id==='pwMask' && CURRENT.forceChange) return;
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  el.classList.remove('show');
  // leave z-index as-is; next open will bump it to top again
}

/* 启动：检查登录态，先显示加载层，避免登录页一闪而过 */
const loadingEl = document.getElementById('appLoading');
function hideLoading(){
  if(!loadingEl) return;
  loadingEl.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(()=>{ loadingEl.style.display='none'; }, 220);
}
api('/api/me').then(me=>{
  if(me && me.uid){
    CURRENT.user=me;
    if(me.must_change){ enforceForceChange(); }
    else { initApp(me); }
  }
  else { document.getElementById('loginWrap').style.display='flex'; }
  hideLoading();
}).catch(()=>{
  document.getElementById('loginWrap').style.display='flex';
  hideLoading();
});

document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(e.key==='Delete' && SEL.type && SEL.type!=='doctype'){
    deleteSelectedNode();
  }
});
</script>
